# MASTER RESEARCH & ENGINEERING PLAN

**Edition 3.741 G4 scenario-GBSM config provenance read hardening (2026-10-26, focused validation):** The bounded scenario generation CLI now reads its config through the shared stable-file reader, rejecting symlinks/hard links and checking opened-file/path identity and full file identity across the read. Alias regressions pass with the scenario CLI/store/contract slices (24 passed, 5 native Sionna cases skipped); Ruff, compilation, and diff checks pass. This protects exact config-byte provenance only; native scenario behavior and G4 statistical conformance remain unqualified.

**Edition 3.740 G3 strict admission requires raw path criteria and powered design (2026-10-26, focused validation):** Strict signed-reviewer admission now requires qualification-contract v4, joint-evaluator input v6 with solver-budget-bound diffuse diagnostics, and a complete precision/power design. The signed integration test rejects a v3 contract and admits the v4 path; the focused joint-evidence/artifact and qualification-contract modules pass. This prevents strict admission from treating a caller-supplied comparison report as criterion-driven path evidence. Existing non-strict v3 diagnostic preregistration remains supported. This is still input-scoped recomputation: physical independence, provenance truth, reviewer custody, scientific tolerance justification, and measured G3 evidence remain unverified; no qualification or production RT run occurred.

**Edition 3.739 G5.09 signed reviewer-role policy for SNR preregistration (2026-10-26, focused validation):** Added a separate canonical reviewer-policy v1, signed in its own OpenSSH namespace by a distinct custodian with a distinct trust keyring. The signed acceptance principal must be authorized as `independent_reviewer` for the G5 preregistration purpose. Curve schema v11 requires this policy identity, and the independent auditor can reverify the policy against its own signature and trust-keyring inputs. The operator workflow is documented in [G5 SNR preregistration](G5_SNR_PREREGISTRATION.md). The acceptance, schema, audit-contract, and retained-result audit slices pass (one receiver-bound skip); Ruff check/format, Python compilation, JSON schema validation, and diff checks pass. This verifies declared role authorization only; declared time and trust-file custody remain unverified. No receiver run occurred and G5 qualification, production RT, and advanced training remain gated.

All 37 corpus-auditor tests pass; Ruff check/format, Python compilation, JSON schema checks, and diff checks pass. Historical result files and source hashes remain unchanged; tests create temporary source-bound receipts. Deterministic receiver replay and any receiver qualification still require the Halti Torch/Sionna environment and independent evidence.

**Edition 3.669 G7 seed-audit bounded scan reporting (2026-10-26, focused validation):** Advanced the local seed-roster audit report to v2 so a JSON/JSON.GZ file that exceeds the declared input or expanded-JSON ceiling is recorded under `incomplete_files`, while the scanner continues through other regular result files. The report status becomes `INCOMPLETE_SCAN` and the CLI exits 2; it cannot present a partial corpus as clear. Unsafe links/hardlinks and input mutation remain fail-closed errors. The focused audit suite passes 21 tests and Ruff passes. On the current saved research corpus the scanner checked 263 files, reported one oversized City43 compressed diagnostic, found no requested seed collisions in scanned files, and marked 1,046 seed fields malformed; the report therefore remains incomplete and must not be used as a global collision-clearance result.

**Edition 3.668 G4 rotated NLOS covariance reference family (2026-10-26, implementation pending native qualification):** Added an ETSI-derived independent receive-covariance reference for dual-VH two-element CDL-A/B/C at three frozen UT Euler orientations. The 8,192-draw/profile/orientation test covers 144 real Hermitian claims at simultaneous 99% confidence, with a 0.05 normalized mean-power half-width ceiling and nine distinct seeds. Ruff and Python compilation pass for the new definition. The seed audit is now capable of structured partial-scan reporting (Edition 3.669); its current result is `INCOMPLETE_SCAN`, so trusted collision clearance is still deferred. Native regression remains unrun. This finite family covers only standardized-model covariance at these configurations and leaves G4 unqualified.

**Edition 3.667 G3 frozen path-coefficient criterion is now scored (2026-10-26, focused validation):** Contract-bound raw path comparison now applies the frozen `coefficient_relative_tolerance` to matched receiver-relevant path classes using `|a-b| / max(|a|, |b|, tiny)`, where each coefficient is the coherent aggregate within the frozen geometric class. A tolerance miss fails the episode alongside the existing 95% bidirectional relevance-coverage criterion. Contract-bound creation and run-manifest replay both use the exact frozen tolerance; the report names the active threshold. A regression confirms a coefficient mismatch fails despite full geometric coverage. The four focused G3 path/joint-evaluator modules pass 123 tests and targeted Ruff checks pass. Direct diagnostic runs without a frozen contract remain diagnostic and do not infer this margin; all G3 outputs remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`.

**Edition 3.666 G4 rotated LOS covariance orientation family expanded (2026-10-26, implementation pending native qualification):** Extended the ETSI-derived CDL-D/E two-element dual-VH covariance test from one to three UT Euler orientations: mixed-axis `(0.37, 0.28, -0.41)`, pure yaw `(0, 0, 0.65)`, and roll/pitch `(0.28, -0.36, 0)` rad. The frozen Bonferroni 99% family now covers 96 real Hermitian components with six distinct seeds at 16,384 draws/profile/orientation; per-claim half-width remains bounded by 0.05 normalized mean power. Protocol and regression definitions agree. Native execution and seed-collision audit remain deferred; this finite orientation set does not establish continuous orientation, mutual coupling, or G4 qualification.

**Edition 3.665 G5 acceptance logic isolated from receiver runtime (2026-10-26, focused validation):** Moved bounded SNR-family contract freezing and simultaneous-interval scoring into a Torch-free module, preserving the existing acceptance semantics and adding explicit overflow rejection for nonrepresentable numeric values. Its dependency-isolated suite passes 17 tests across roster drift, minimum cluster floor, PASS/FAIL/NOT_EVALUABLE, and malformed interval bounds; Ruff passes on the touched Python modules. No receiver simulation ran; the independent-channel floor is still only an executable minimum, and scientific/field qualification remains open.

**Edition 3.664 G3 preregistration identity canonicalization (2026-10-26, focused validation):** The frozen G3 run-contract builder now rejects padded task, scenario, candidate/challenger, waveform, episode, path-roster, and per-stratum identity strings instead of trimming selected path-roster values or admitting aliases. The qualification-contract module passes 33 focused tests, including seven padded-identity regressions. This secures canonical contract identity only; no scientific evidence or G3 qualification is established, and production RT remains gated.

**Edition 3.663 G5 bounded curve runner import repair (2026-10-26, implementation pending environment validation):** Fixed a malformed evidence-staging indentation that prevented importing the bounded ordered-impairment SNR-curve runner and corrected scope text assembly that Python interpreted as a string call. The Torch-free G5 independent-channel statistics module passes 4 tests. The curve schema module remains uncollected because the current Halti `.venv` does not contain Torch; no receiver data generation or qualification was run.

**Edition 3.662 G3 contract-bound joint evaluator focused integration (2026-10-26):** Updated stale regression fixtures to the current qualification-contract v4/input-v6 boundary and corrected assertions to test fail-closed input admission, exact contract-budget binding, and runtime identity in the run manifest. The joint-evaluation and evidence-artifact modules pass 73 focused tests. Together with the 127-test canonical identity/schema slice and 27-test signed-lock/contract slice, this is targeted software evidence only; material acquisition, independent measurement truth, external trust, scientific qualification, production RT, and advanced training remain gated.

**Edition 3.661 G4 rotated LOS dual-polarized covariance reference definition (2026-10-26, implementation pending native qualification):** Added a source-hash-bound CDL-D/E two-element dual-VH receive covariance definition for UT orientation `(0.37, 0.28, -0.41)` rad. The expected matrix composes the rotated receive polarization basis, rotated local-y ULA steering, independent diffuse ray covariance, and deterministic LOS K-factor term from ETSI-derived inputs. A frozen 16,384-draw/profile, 32-claim 99% family and distinct seed roster are recorded in the test/protocol. The native experiment and seed-collision audit remain deferred; this closes only the missing definition for one orientation, not G4.

**Edition 3.660 G3 path diagnostic schema synchronized with diffuse-path reporting (2026-10-26):** Advanced the path-equivalence roster report to diagnostic schema v2 and added a strict schema for raw-input digests, optional diffuse distribution summaries, frozen solver-budget provenance, descriptive-only status, and limitations. The source bundle already named the v2 schema; the regression exposed that the artifact shape had not been fully specified. Targeted changed-module validation passes 127 tests, including all path-roster tests. This is schema/software integrity evidence only; diffuse summaries remain descriptive, finite solver caps are not truth, G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`, and no native G4/G5 qualification was run.

**Edition 3.659 G1 independent segment-visibility oracle foundation (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added `propagation.visibility.segment_mesh_visibility`, an independent triangle-surface segment classifier with visible/blocked/ambiguous outcomes, explicit meter scaling, affine mesh transform support, and conservative edge/vertex/endpoint/coplanar outcomes. Regression definitions are authored but unrun. This is not yet a production mesh-inventory adapter and does not resolve thin-wall material transmission, scene transforms/units from native assets, or OSM-scene geometric truth; no tests, Ruff, formatting, type checks, or native qualification runs have been performed, and no gate is closed.

**Edition 3.658 G7 split identity enforcement through the legacy model runner (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** ChannelDataset now requires canonical group identities and its child-binding adapter preserves canonical parent/child strings without trimming; the signed-split experiment resolver rejects padded subject/group IDs. Regression definitions cover dataset admission, parent/child binding, and the runner boundary. Model-input data remain separate from labels and metadata; the full downstream adapter audit is still open. No tests or static checks have run, and no release is qualified.

**Edition 3.657 G7 legacy split identity canonicalization (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The legacy independent-split builder, assignment object, and leakage auditor now reject surrounding-whitespace aliases in sample/group IDs, seeds, and configured identity field names rather than silently trimming them. Regression definitions cover malformed builder, frozen-object, and audit inputs. Canonical inputs retain the existing assignment behavior; no tests or static checks have run and release qualification remains open.

**Edition 3.656 G3 identity-boundary completion (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The paired receiver scorer now rejects padded family identities in both expected and observed strata, and the raw path-equivalence evaluator rejects padded scenario/configuration identities instead of normalizing them. Repaired an interrupted conditional in the receiver scorer. These are contract-hardening changes only; no tests or static checks have run, numerical outputs remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`, and G3/production RT gates remain closed.

**Edition 3.655 G5.14 DCI 0_3 TPMI width aggregation (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The decoded-state resolver now derives zero precoding/layer bits for non-codebook scheduled cells, requires a per-cell TPMI width for each codebook cell, and composes those widths by the configured type1a maximum or type2 sum over selected cells. Regression definitions cover mixed cell configurations and both modes. Deriving codebook cell widths directly from all RRC/table conditions remains open. No validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.654 G5.14 DCI 0_3 antenna-port field width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The resolver now derives the per-cell antenna-port width from transform-precoding state, DMRS type, enhanced-DMRS presence, and maximum length across each configured mapping-type roster. It takes the maximum across mapping types, then combines scheduled cells using `antennaPortsDCI0-3` type1a (maximum) or type2 (sum). Regression definitions cover type1a/type2 aggregation and both mapping-type inputs. Table-dependent codebook precoding/layer width remains open. No validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.653 G5.14 DCI 0_3 non-codebook SRI capability selection (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Non-codebook SRI width now uses configured `maxMIMO-Layers` only when UE support for that parameter is declared; otherwise it requires the UE-supported maximum non-codebook PUSCH layer count. Missing fallback capability fails closed, and codebook cells do not need the capability value. Regression definitions cover configured, absent, unsupported-config, and missing-capability cases. No validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.652 G5.14 DCI 0_3 SRS resource indicator width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The resolver derives SRI width from the configured SRI mode, per-cell `txConfig`, first SRS resource-set size, and non-codebook maximum layers. Codebook width is `ceil(log2(N_SRS))`; non-codebook width is `ceil(log2(sum(C(N_SRS,l), l=1..Lmax)))`. Type1a takes the maximum positive per-cell width and type2 sums widths over the selected cell combination. Regression definitions cover both modes, codebook types, selected-cell behavior, and invalid roster/mode inputs. No validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4 and Tables 7.3.1.1.2-28 through 7.3.1.1.2-32B](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.651 G5.14 DCI 0_3 measurement-gap cancellation field (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added the Release 19 measurement-gap-cancellation field after PDCCH monitoring adaptation in the canonical ordered DCI 0_3 layout. The resolver derives zero bits when `mg-CancellationDCI-0-3` is not configured and one bit otherwise. Regression definitions cover both widths and reject non-boolean presence state. No validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.650 G5.14 DCI 0_3 open-loop power-control indication width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The resolver derives zero bits for cells without a configured P0-PUSCH set list, one bit when the SRI field is present, or the configured one/two-bit `olpc-ParameterSetDCI-0-1` width otherwise. The DCI field width is the maximum across applicable selected cells. Regression definitions cover SRI-present, configured-width, no-P0, incomplete-map, and invalid-width cases. No validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.649 G5.14 DCI 0_3 PTRS-DMRS association width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The field width is now derived per selected cell: two bits when PTRS-UplinkConfig is configured, transform precoding is disabled, and maximum PUSCH rank exceeds one; zero otherwise. PTRS and rank inputs must cover the complete configured cell roster. Regression definitions cover cell selection, transform precoding, rank-one suppression, and incomplete input pairs; no validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.648 G5.14 DCI 0_3 SCell dormancy width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The decoded-state resolver derives the SCell dormancy bitmap width from configured dormancy-group IDs and DCI occasion context. It returns zero bits outside the specified primary-cell/DRX-Active-Time/multiple-SCell-DL-BWP context, and requires one through five unique group IDs when the field is applicable. Regression definitions cover bitmap sizes and absent/malformed context; no validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.647 G5.14 DCI 0_3 PDCCH monitoring adaptation width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The decoded-configuration resolver derives the PDCCH monitoring adaptation field width from its enable state and configured skipping-duration and search-space group lists. It implements the Release 19 0/1/2-bit cases and rejects invalid group rosters or an enabled feature without a source list. Regression definitions cover each width path and malformed configurations; no validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.646 G5.14 DCI 0_3 frequency-domain assignment widths (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The decoded-configuration resolver now derives each scheduled cell's frequency-assignment field width from the active UL BWP PRB extent and decoded resource-allocation configuration. It supports type 0, type 1, dynamic switch, and interlace allocation; applies the configured type-1 RBG granularity and type-1 hopping-offset width; and sums widths over the selected scheduled-cell combination. Regression definitions cover per-mode widths, multi-cell combination, and malformed BWP configurations. Mapping follows [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0 clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf) and [TS 38.214 V19.2.0 clauses 6.1.2.2.1–6.1.2.2.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138214/19.02.00_60/ts_138214v190200p.pdf). No tests or static checks have run. ASN.1/PER, CRC/polar decoding, remaining supplied DCI widths, and receiver qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.645 G6 signed split-registry integration for RT trajectory forecast pilot (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The forecast pilot now supports an exact-byte OpenSSH-signed UMCD split-policy registry, externally supplied allowed-signers trust, and an explicit signer identity. It resolves trajectory sample splits through frozen `nr_trajectory_sample` child bindings, rejects unregistered or wrong-kind samples, verifies every stored NR record's parent/child/kind provenance against its binding, and requires supplied trajectories to cover exactly the registered forecast-sample roster. The report binds the verified registry, detached signature, trust file, signer, namespace, and policy digest; calibration partitions are accepted and reported. The legacy unsigned lock remains available with diagnostic-only provenance. Regression definitions are authored but unrun; this does not qualify forecasting or establish site generalization.

**Edition 3.644 G3 signed acquisition-attestation compatibility with v0.4 (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The detached OpenSSH attestation verifier now accepts proposal-only acquisition package v0.4, which binds the derived CSI noise-floor evidence contract, in addition to historical v0.2/v0.3 packages. Regression definitions cover both single-attestation and complete declared-set verification for v0.4 using exact package digest, subject-file roster, namespace, and signer-role policy checks. Reports remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`; signature validity does not establish measurement truth, signer independence, or trust-file custody. No tests or static checks have run.

**Edition 3.643 G5.14 DCI 0_3 selected-combination field widths (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The configuration-derived width resolver now requires the decoded scheduled-cells indicator when multiple combination rows exist and computes MCS, NDI, RV, TPC, and HARQ-process widths over the selected row's cell roster. Per-cell PUSCH counts and RV widths remain validated against the full configured cell roster; MCS is five bits per selected cell, while NDI/RV use each selected cell's maximum schedulable PUSCH count and configured RV width. Regression definitions cover the existing two-cell row and selection of a heterogeneous single-cell row. This does not implement raw DCI/ASN.1 decoding or receiver qualification; no validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.642 G5.14 DCI 0_3 downlink assignment index widths (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The resolver derives first and second DAI widths as the maximum across the one or two configured HARQ-ACK codebooks. First DAI is one bit for semi-static and two for dynamic codebook; second DAI is two bits only for dynamic HARQ-ACK with two sub-codebooks, otherwise zero. Incoherent semi-static/two-sub-codebook state is rejected. Regression definitions cover mixed semi-static/dynamic codebooks and dynamic/two-sub-codebook state. Other DCI widths, ASN.1/PER and CRC/polar decoding, independent conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.641 G5.14 DCI 0_3 HARQ-process number width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The resolver now sums the decoded 0–5-bit HARQ-process-number size for every cell in the selected scheduled-cell list. It rejects missing/extra cell entries, Boolean/noninteger sizes, and values outside the specification range. Regression definitions cover heterogeneous two-cell widths and a zero-width single-cell case. Other DCI fields, ASN.1/PER and CRC/polar decoding, independent conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.640 G5.14 DCI 0_3 scheduled-PUSCH TPC width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The width resolver now derives the TPC-command field as two bits per cell in the complete selected scheduled-cell list, following the ascending serving-cell order defined by TS 38.212. Regression definitions cover one and multiple scheduled cells. Other DCI widths, ASN.1/PER and CRC/polar decoding, independent conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.639 G5.14 DCI 0_3 channel-access CAPC width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The resolver derives the channel-access field width from decoded access-configuration entry counts for every scheduled cell, taking the maximum `ceil(log2(count))` across applicable cells and treating zero as not applicable. It validates exact scheduled-cell coverage and integer counts in the supported 0–64 range. Regression definitions cover four entries in one cell with no field in another, and no applicable configuration. Remaining DCI widths, ASN.1/PER and CRC/polar decoding, independent conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.638 G5.14 DCI 0_3 beta-offset indicator width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The decoded-configuration resolver now derives beta-offset-indicator width as zero when beta offsets are semi-static in every scheduled cell, otherwise two bits. It requires a strict Boolean semi-static-state entry for each cell in the selected scheduled-cell roster. Regression definitions cover all-semi-static and mixed configurations. Other configuration-dependent DCI widths, ASN.1/PER and CRC/polar decoding, independent conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.637 G5.14 DCI 0_3 priority and scheduling-offset indicator widths (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The decoded-configuration resolver now derives the priority-indicator and minimum-applicable-scheduling-offset widths from their higher-layer configuration-presence flags, requiring strict Boolean inputs. Regression definitions cover both configured and absent states. Remaining DCI widths, ASN.1/PER and CRC/polar decoding, independent standards conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.636 G5.14 DCI 0_3 DMRS sequence initialization and UL-SCH widths (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The decoded-configuration resolver now derives DMRS sequence-initialization presence from transform-precoding state across every cell in the selected scheduled-cell list and fixes UL-SCH indicator width at one bit. The transform-precoding map is required to exactly cover the cell list and contain Boolean decoded states. Regression definitions cover one disabled cell in a mixed roster and the all-enabled zero-width case. Other DCI widths, ASN.1/PER and CRC/polar decoding, independent standards conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.635 G5.14 SRS offset-list and CSI request widths (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The decoded-configuration DCI 0_3 width resolver now derives the SRS-offset indicator width from the actual ordered offset-list entries and validates one in-range index per scheduled cell. It also takes the decoded `reportTriggerSize` as the CSI-request width. This adds two configuration-bound field widths; other optional and per-cell DCI widths, ASN.1/PER decoding, and receiver qualification remain open. Regression definition updated; validation deferred.

**Edition 3.634 G5.14 decoded-configuration DCI widths (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The DCI 0_3 layout path now derives format-ID, cell-set, scheduled-cell-combination, BWP-indicator, SRS-request, SRS-offset-indicator, and CSI-request widths from decoded cell-set/BWP/list state, then feeds the ordered layout offset calculator. Widths for other DCI fields, complete ASN.1/RRC decoding, CRC/polar decoding, and receiver qualification remain open. Regression definitions authored; validation deferred.

**Edition 3.633 G5.14 ordered DCI 0_3 field-layout offsets (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an SRS selector adapter that walks the complete TS 38.212 V19.2.0 clause 7.3.1.1.4 field order and derives the selector offset from a strict roster of decoded field widths. It validates the list-dependent selector width and permits only zero trailing padding. Deriving field widths from decoded ASN.1/RRC and cell/BWP configuration, CRC/polar decoding, and receiver qualification remain open. Regression definitions are authored; validation deferred.

**Edition 3.632 G4 CDL-E LOS spatial covariance reference (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Extended the bounded two-port LOS/diffuse spatial-covariance regression to CDL-E alongside CDL-D. Each expected matrix derives from its byte-pinned ETSI profile components and checks the native profile table digest; the existing familywise interval and width requirements are retained. This adds a second profile-specific model-consistency case and does not establish external channel validity or close G4. Regression definition authored; validation deferred.

**Edition 3.631 G7 split-lock writer/reader size agreement (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Split-policy lock publication now enforces the reader's 256 MiB byte ceiling and rejects an oversized canonical registry before creating its destination directory. This prevents persisting a lock that the same implementation cannot read. Regression definition authored; no validation run. It is a serialization/resource invariant only and does not qualify split lineage or a corpus release.

**Edition 3.630 G5 paired correction-effect inference for SNR curves (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Curve schema v4 adds the channel-cluster paired `uncorrected − corrected` macro-BLER effect at every SNR point. One simultaneous max-deviation band now covers the three condition curves and the paired effects across the complete grid, and the curve auditor recomputes the effect and interval from retained per-channel CRC outcomes. This exposes receiver-correction impact while preserving the diagnostic-only result status; no acceptance thresholds or qualification claims are added. Regression definitions are authored and unrun.

**Edition 3.629 G4 scenario GBSM generator implementation (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added explicit one-BS/one-UT-per-batch-row adapters for Sionna 2.2 UMi, UMa, RMa, InH, and InF. The adapter hashes topology and complete generator configuration, returns path coefficients, absolute delays, and centered-grid CFR, and keeps scenario LOS distinct from geometric visibility. Generator capability report/schema advance to v2 and identify this as code support only. The API is not yet connected to canonical episode-store ingestion; spatial-consistency/blockage, multi-cell interference, statistical conformance, and G4 closure remain open. Regression definitions cover all five constructors, shape limits, and CFR reconstruction. No tests or static checks ran.

**Edition 3.628 G3 branch-specific criterion outcome semantics (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The joint numerical roll-up now accepts booleans only from material, CSI, and receiver criteria; `PASS`/`FAIL` status strings only for path equivalence; and `NOT_APPLICABLE` only for propagation metrics. Other representations remain unresolved instead of becoming passes. Regression definitions cover cross-branch malformed outcomes. No tests or static checks were run; all reports remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`.

**Edition 3.627 G4 CDL 3D temporal-reference identity (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The A–E 3D-velocity temporal-covariance family now verifies the exact ETSI-derived per-cluster reference bytes and source-PDF digest before calculating expectations. A dependency-isolated contract regression checks the file digest, manifest identity, and A–E profile roster. The Sionna table hashes remain implementation provenance only; the intervals and native outcomes are unvalidated and do not qualify the full G4 model.

**Edition 3.626 G5 diagnostic SNR-curve schema enforcement (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The G5 result writer/verifier now validates each v1-v3 SNR-curve report against the exact schema bytes whose digest is recorded. The independent auditor separately reports schema validity and schema-digest agreement, closing the path where a hash-consistent report could contain undeclared fields. Regression definitions reject unknown fields. No tests or static checks were run; G5 remains diagnostic-only and unqualified.

**Edition 3.625 G7 split-audit authority reporting (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Child-artifact audit authority text now reflects versioned admission semantics: v1 admits only semantically checked ChannelDataset artifacts, v2 retains byte-only checks for unknown supplemental types, and exact-tree v3/v4 reject types without semantic validators. Regression assertions cover legacy and exact-tree wording. No tests or static checks were run; release qualification is unchanged.

**Edition 3.624 G7 UMCD episode-store split-child admission (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Signed exact-tree child-artifact roster v4 now semantically validates content-addressed UMCD episode manifests and their layer objects. The canonical store reader applies a conservative 512 MiB shard-accounting ceiling; the episode ID must match the frozen parent subject, and every content object must map to exactly one rostered manifest reference with the canonical layer/hash path. Child-artifact audit and outer lineage report advance to v5/v8 while roster v1-v3 behavior is retained. Regression definitions cover schema admission, valid object pairing, and wrong-parent rejection; validation is deferred. The accounting model is not measured RSS. This verifies only declared descendants and does not establish authoritative inventory completeness or signer custody.

**Edition 3.623 G3 joint numerical-criterion roll-up (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The joint evaluator now aggregates the existing material pilot margins, complex-CSI, propagation, receiver-impact, and path-equivalence outcomes into explicit pass/fail/unresolved branch states and a joint numerical-only status. Empty rosters and missing criterion fields remain unresolved; propagation `NOT_APPLICABLE` entries are counted as resolved. Bundle/artifact schemas advance to v2 and the current evaluator source roster includes the new artifact schema. Even a complete numerical pass remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`; these reports do not establish external provenance or scientific admission. Regression definitions are authored but have not run.

**Edition 3.622 G5 DCI 0_3 SRS selector-to-schedule bridge (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported adapter that reads the configured `srs-RequestListDCI-0-3` selector from an already-decoded MSB-first DCI payload and passes the selected row through the existing multi-cell SRS scheduler. An absent request list returns `None`; malformed/truncated selectors retain the decoder's fail-closed behavior. The caller still supplies the bit offset and decoded RRC/configuration, so full DCI layout, CRC/polar decoding, and ASN.1/PER remain open. Regression definitions cover selection and no-list handling; no tests were run. Field semantics follow ETSI TS 138 212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4 and Table 7.3.1.1.4-4.

**Edition 3.621 G7 canonical NR trajectory identities (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** `NRTrajectory` now rejects non-string IDs with a typed contract error and rejects empty or whitespace-padded IDs before trajectory storage or split-lineage use. Regression definitions cover non-string, blank, and noncanonical identities. This prevents malformed trajectory identifiers from entering content-addressed paths or lineage rosters; it does not establish corpus completeness or qualify a split. No tests were run.

**Edition 3.620 G3 receipt-builder schema enforcement (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The run-manifest builder now validates its completed v3 receipt against the versioned schema before returning, including any injected runtime identity. A regression definition rejects malformed caller-supplied identity at build time instead of allowing an invalid receipt to travel downstream. This enforces structural integrity only; runtime claims remain self-reported and G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`. No tests or static checks were run.

**Edition 3.619 G3 manifest source-roster/schema consistency (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Corrected the v2 and v3 run-manifest schemas to require the cardinality of their fixed evaluator-source rosters (33 and 35 entries). Added a regression definition tying each schema's `minItems`/`maxItems` to the corresponding versioned roster, preventing new receipts from contradicting their structural contract. The roster remains self-reported source identity, not independent attestation; G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`, and no tests or static checks were run.

**Edition 3.618 G3 runtime identity in signed run receipts (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** G3 run-manifest v3 records a runtime snapshot captured before contract-bound scoring: Python implementation/version, platform, machine, supported numerical/Sionna package versions, and source revision. The runtime block is included in the exact evaluator-source roster and covered by the manifest signature when supplied. The verifier reports a digest but labels the identity self-reported and unattested; it does not compare historical runs to the verifier host or qualify scientific evidence. v1/v2 receipt readers remain available. Regression definitions are authored but unrun.

**Edition 3.617 G5 paired multi-SNR receiver-input evidence (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Diagnostic curve schema v3 and waveform evidence v4 now retain each SNR point's exact clean, uncorrected, and corrected receiver-input waveforms, with shared TX/oscillator/capture/AWGN draws stored once. The auditor binds every point to its SNR index and recomputes the noisy input, all three input digests, and clean/corrected waveform equations. Point and aggregate evidence stay within compressed, uncompressed, transport-block, and SNR-grid caps. The curve remains descriptive-only; no multi-SNR acceptance criteria or receiver qualification are claimed. Regression definitions are authored but unrun.

**Edition 3.616 G7 exact-tree NR record semantic lineage (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Exact-tree roster v3 now admits standalone NR record manifests together with their content-addressed NPZ payloads. The auditor requires one rostered manifest to reference each payload, checks the canonical content-hash path, reads the record through the store reader, and verifies split subject/child/kind provenance against the frozen binding. Regression definitions cover exact subtree inventory and semantic record admission; they remain unrun. No split release is qualified.

**Edition 3.614 G5 bounded SNR-grid size (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The diagnostic independent-channel receiver curve now rejects grids above 64 points before receiver construction, records the fixed point ceiling, and its auditor/schema enforce the same bound. This bounds report and per-point execution overhead in addition to the existing 16,384-transport-block ceiling. It does not add multi-SNR acceptance criteria or qualify receiver behavior; regression definition is authored and validation remains deferred.

**Edition 3.613 G3.03 typed CSI noise evidence references (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Acquisition-package v0.3 adds the `noise_measurement` inventory role and requires every referenced CSI `h_floor.noise_input_file_ids` entry to resolve to a file with that role. v0.1/v0.2 retain their historical generic file-role contract. A regression definition rejects a v0.3 reference whose file is labeled `reference`. This establishes role-level consistency only; it does not derive the floor from file contents or authenticate the acquisition method. Validation and scientific admission remain open.

**Edition 3.612 G3.03 acquisition CSI floor admission (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Acquisition-package schemas v0.1/v0.2/v0.3 and the semantic verifier now require `h_floor.amplitude_linear` to be finite, strictly positive, and representable as a finite float, consistent with the complex-CSI scorer. Regression definitions reject zero and integers beyond the finite-float range. The verifier confirms only admissibility and file references; it does not derive the floor from the noise evidence or qualify its scientific method. No acquisition package has been scientifically admitted, and validation remains deferred.

**Edition 3.611 G5.14 symbol-varying SRS receiver weights (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The multiport SRS estimator now accepts either a per-RX noise variance or a per-RX/per-slot-symbol variance profile. The latter weights each observed SRS symbol in the LS equations, and the immutable result retains the full profile plus each RX/port covariance. The caller supplies the profile; no noise or interference is inferred, and synchronization/channel evolution remain unmodeled. Regression definitions compare against explicit weighted-LS equations and exercise invalid inputs; they are unrun.

**Edition 3.610 G7 canonical target value-domain admission (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added digest-version keyed contracts for the standardized profile-LOS proxy, standardized/TDL/RT CFR, and geometric-LOS targets. `TargetValue` and NR v3 storage admission now enforce the target name, semantic, units, CFR complex dtype, and scalar binary value domain where specified; unknown research target versions remain application-defined. Regression definitions cover valid and invalid domains and storage-path enforcement. Full target catalog/shape/gauge rules, migration policy, and validation remain open.

**Edition 3.609 G7 exact-tree typed artifact admission (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Signed child-artifact roster v3 now admits only artifact types with implemented semantic validators (`channel_dataset_npz` and `nr_trajectory_store_manifest_json`) and rejects an opaque type before reading artifact payloads. Its strict JSON schema uses the same allowlist. The mixed ChannelDataset-plus-sidecar regression remains on legacy roster v2 to verify its byte-only count and false semantic-completeness report. Regression definitions are authored but unrun; authoritative inventory completeness and validators for other descendant types remain open.

**Edition 3.608 G7 split-audit semantic completeness reporting (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Corrected the signed child-artifact audit so `child_artifact_semantic_rows_verified` is true only when every rostered artifact has a semantic validator. A mixed exact-tree roster with byte-verified but opaque supplemental artifacts now reports semantic verification as false and preserves its byte-only count. Regression definitions cover a signed ChannelDataset-plus-sidecar roster; validation is deferred. This does not complete semantic validators for every descendant type or establish external inventory authority.

**Edition 3.607 G4 standardized-generator capability matrix (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a strict hash-bound report derived from the TDL/CDL applicability ledger. It names every supported scalable/fixed profile and table version, the one-TX-node/one-RX-node MIMO topology, family-specific antenna features, unsupported multi-node/interference topologies, and the unimplemented UMi/UMa/RMa/InH/InF scenario-GBSM family. The report describes code scope, not conformance; no scenario GBSM is claimed as supported. Regression definitions cover digest, schema, and capability boundaries; they remain unrun.

**Edition 3.606 G5.14 bounded raw DCI 0_3 SRS selector decoding (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported MSB-first decoder for the SRS-request-list selector in an already-decoded DCI 0_3 payload. It derives the field width from the configured list length, handles absent and one-entry lists, and rejects truncation and reserved codepoints. The caller supplies the payload offset; full DCI field-layout derivation, CRC/channel decoding, ASN.1/PER, and receiver qualification remain open. Regression definitions are authored but unrun. Source: ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4 and Tables 7.3.1.1.4-4.

**Edition 3.605 G7 canonical NR target definitions (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** New NR record writes use schema v3 and require exactly one digest-verified, versioned target definition for every stored target. Standardized PUSCH, TDL-PUSCH, and RT trajectory producers now declare units, reference, aggregation rule, source, and definition version through a canonical factory. The v3 reader verifies the definition digest; v1/v2 remain readable without retroactively upgrading historical claims. Regression definitions cover missing and unversioned definitions plus round-trip persistence. This does not finish domain-specific shape/range/gauge policy or scientific target admission; no validation was run.

**Edition 3.604 G5.14 multi-cell periodic SRS scheduling (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported composer for a frozen serving-cell roster that resolves each cell's configured periodic/semi-persistent SRS resource sets using its own absolute slot and caller-selected active UL BWP. Every active allocation is checked against that cell's BWP bounds; output preserves serving-cell order, BWP identity, and cells with no active occasion. Regression definitions cover per-cell slot/BWP variation and exact-roster rejection. This consumes decoded configuration and does not establish cross-cell orthogonality/interference, decode ASN.1/DCI, or qualify a receiver. No validation was run.

**Edition 3.603 G5.14 periodic SRS resource-set composition and BWP feasibility (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported periodic/semi-persistent resource-set composer with per-resource occasion overrides and active-subset output, plus a BWP-aware wrapper that checks every active symbol allocation against the caller-selected UL BWP bounds. Regression definitions cover mixed and absent occasions, duplicate IDs, unsupported fields, an in-BWP schedule, and an out-of-BWP rejection. This accepts decoded configuration and caller-selected BWP state; it does not decode ASN.1/RRC or DCI, infer inter-cell interference, or qualify receiver behavior. No validation was run.

**Edition 3.592 G4 complex-pseudocovariance roster uses independent ETSI profiles (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The CDL-A–E circularity regression now obtains LOS status and output cluster counts—and therefore its familywise claim count—from the byte-pinned ETSI reference roster. Sionna package table hashes remain implementation provenance only. The zero-pseudocovariance null, draw count, familywise alpha, and precision bound are unchanged. The LOS diffuse-only decomposition still uses Sionna 2.2.0 internals. Regression definitions are authored but unrun; no broad conformance claim is made.

**Edition 3.591 G4 directional antenna-pattern expectations use independent ETSI rows (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Rebased the all-profile directional-element and dual-end antenna-pattern power regressions on byte-pinned ETSI cluster delays, powers, angles, angular spreads, and LOS K-factor references. The existing independent TR 38.901 element gain law, orientation/pattern matrix, confidence families, and precision limits are retained; Sionna table digests are implementation provenance only. No tests were run. Array-coupling and broad physical/statistical qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.590 G4 CDL angular-sector expectations use independent ETSI rows (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The all-profile four-angle sector-power confidence regression now derives cluster order, normalized diffuse power, nominal ray angles, angular spreads, and LOS specular/K-factor contributions from the byte-pinned ETSI CDL fixtures. The Sionna packaged-table digest remains an implementation identity check only. Existing 99% familywise intervals, claim family, and precision limit are unchanged; no tests were run. This closes the reference-source gap for this angular-marginal branch, not the separate antenna-pattern/orientation or all-profile statistical qualification gaps.

**Edition 3.589 G4 dual-polarization CDL expectations use independent ETSI rows (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Reworked the dual-VH CDL-A–E power regression references to calculate NLOS XPR and LOS K-factor/orientation expectations from the byte-pinned ETSI per-cluster and scalar reference fixtures. The Sionna packaged-table digest is checked only as system-under-test implementation provenance; it no longer supplies expected powers or angles. The declared familywise confidence and precision criteria are unchanged. Regression definitions are authored but unrun; this does not qualify all polarization/antenna configurations or physical RT.

**Edition 3.588 G5 network PHY power/noise and composition integrity (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Multi-transmitter grid composition and SINR accounting now reject Boolean/complex/sparse/nonnumeric power and noise inputs before coercion, reject power/noise scales not representable in the receiver dtype, and reject nonfinite scaling/channel products. SINR and receiver-noise paths verify that the recorded network grid equals the sum of its source contributions. Regression definitions cover malformed inputs, scale boundaries, and inconsistent composition; no tests were run. This hardens a synchronized frequency-domain baseband primitive and does not model timing/CFO, RF effects, link budget/beam truth, or qualify a multi-cell receiver.

**Edition 3.587 G4 joint delay/angle expectations use independent CDL source rows (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The all-profile four-angle characteristic and joint delay/angle regressions now derive cluster powers, delays, and angles from the ETSI-transcribed CDL fixture. The native Sionna ray generation remains the system under test, and its package-table digest remains provenance only. This removes shared parameter inputs from these joint-distribution expectations but does not qualify all angular laws, antenna patterns, polarization, or physical-channel behavior; no tests were run.

**Edition 3.586 G4 statistical expectations use independent CDL source rows (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The all-profile A–E joint TX/RX spatial covariance reference and 3D-velocity temporal covariance reference now derive cluster powers and angles from the ETSI-transcribed per-cluster fixture; Sionna package JSON is retained only for implementation table-digest provenance. The expectations retain the independent ray-offset/coupling calculations and confidence bounds already declared by those regressions. This removes a shared-parameter-source weakness but does not qualify other array/pattern/covariance families, all temporal regimes, or physical-channel behavior; no tests were run.

**Edition 3.585 G4 profile-version source coverage (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The exact ETSI source manifest now pins TR 38.901 v16.1.0 as well as v19.2.0 and TS 38.101-4 v19.2.2. A regression definition joins every version declared by the standardized TDL/CDL applicability ledger to a pinned source and explicitly rejects the unsupported SCM/UMi family. The manifest remains identity-only; this does not qualify profile conformance or broaden supported models.

**Edition 3.584 G4 CDL per-cluster angle/power references (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added source-hash-bound ETSI TR 38.901 v19.2.0 CDL-A–E cluster delay, power, four nominal angles, per-cluster angular spreads, and XPR references. CDL-D/E retain LOS specular and diffuse first-cluster rows separately; regression definitions compare output cluster ordering, delay, linearly aggregated power, angles, and scalar angular/XPR metadata. These deterministic table checks do not qualify ray generation, covariance, temporal evolution, receiver impact, or physical generalization; no validation was run.

**Edition 3.583 G4 fixed-TDL per-component tap references (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a source-hash-bound transcription of each Annex B delay/power/fading component for all seven fixed TDL profiles. The reference preserves TDL-D's LOS specular and diffuse components separately at the shared first delay cluster. Regression definitions validate source/schema/roster structure and compare resolved delays and linear-aggregated per-cluster powers against the independent ETSI table rows. These deterministic checks do not qualify fading statistics, LOS K-factor behavior, temporal/covariance behavior, or NR receiver impact; no validation was run.

**Edition 3.582 G5.09 standalone condition-input evidence (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added waveform-evidence v3 for standalone independent-channel receiver runs. It retains and digest-binds each channel's exact clean-aligned, impaired-uncorrected, and synchronization/CFO-corrected PUSCH receiver inputs alongside shared TX/oscillator/capture and standardized AWGN draws. The auditor verifies the per-condition byte digests, reconstructs the uncorrected input, and checks clean/corrected transformations against the retained waveforms. Evidence-backed result schemas advance to v19/v20; v1/v2 sidecars remain readable. The multi-SNR curve keeps its shared v2 raw-draw sidecar because its points have different AWGN amplitudes. Regression definitions are authored but unrun; this is replay evidence, not receiver qualification.

**Edition 3.579 G4 fixed-TDL PDP RMS recomputation (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The native regression definition now recomputes RMS delay spread from resolved per-cluster delays and mean powers, then checks that independent calculation against the adapter-reported scalar and pinned ETSI value with a tolerance derived from the standard's 0.1 dB tap-power rounding. This closes the self-reported-scalar-only weakness in the code path. It still does not independently compare every tap power or qualify fading/statistical behavior; no tests were run.

**Edition 3.580 G4 CDL scalar reference contract binding (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a dependency-light contract regression that validates the compact CDL-A–E scalar fixture against its strict JSON schema and binds its standard/version/source URL/SHA-256 to the pinned ETSI source manifest. It also checks that the source-component counts agree with output delay clusters plus the LOS specular component. The native metadata comparison remains authored but unrun; selected scalar checks do not establish full CDL conformance.

**Edition 3.581 G5.09 SNR-curve waveform reconstruction evidence (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added optional shared TX/oscillator/capture and standardized AWGN draws for the bounded paired diagnostic SNR curve. A staged sidecar is hash-bound to every point, each point binds per-channel noisy receiver-input digests, and the curve auditor reconstructs these inputs from the retained draws and SNR-specific variance. Regression definitions cover shared manifest binding, publication, overwrite refusal, schema admission, and independent reconstruction. Multi-SNR acceptance, broader receiver coverage, and validation remain open.

**Edition 3.576 G3 scorer implementation inventory (2026-10-26, code present; consolidated validation and scientific admission pending):** Reconciled checklist status with the implemented material-calibration, complex-CSI, propagation-metrics, and paired NR receiver scorers, their common-family joint evaluator, and acquisition-design assurance path. Regression suites have not been run in this implementation pass. External measurement/runtime trust, representative independent acquisition units, preregistration evidence, and scientific acceptance remain open; outputs stay `NOT_QUALIFIED`.

**Edition 3.575 G4 fixed-TDL tap-summary references (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added hash-bound Annex B tap-count, maximum-excess-delay, and delay-resolution summaries for all seven fixed TDL profiles. Native regression definitions compare these properties with each resolved Sionna profile in addition to the existing RMS-delay scalars. The compact fixture omits full tap powers, fading distributions, and statistical behavior; validation is deferred.

**Edition 3.574 G5.09 bounded receiver workload (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The independent-channel ordered-impairment runner rejects requests above 16,384 transport blocks before constructing its Sionna receiver. Regression definitions verify the preflight guard. The ceiling bounds computation only; the runner remains a single-operating-point synthetic fixture and this does not establish G5 qualification. No checks or receiver runs have been made.

**Edition 3.573 G7 bounded NR trajectory reads (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** NR trajectory pointer manifests now use stable single-link reads capped at 1 MiB, and trajectory JSON content is capped at 64 MiB; the writer enforces the same limits before publication. Regression definitions cover over-limit manifest/content inputs, hard-linked manifests, early rejection before content access, and writer-side refusal. No validation has run. This extends bounded input handling but does not satisfy measured end-to-end RSS or cross-format retry/failure requirements.

**Edition 3.572 G7 bounded NR manifest inventory reads (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The NR sharded iterator's manifest scan now reads each resolved manifest through a stable descriptor with a 1 MiB cap, checks regular-file/single-link identity before and after reading, and rejects oversize input before record-payload estimation. It retains the existing SQLite-backed deterministic inventory and symlink-alias duplicate detection. Regression definitions cover oversized and hard-linked manifests; no validation has run. This closes an input-bound gap, not the remaining cross-format retry/failure or measured-RSS requirements.

**Edition 3.571 G4 fixed-TDL independent RMS scalar references (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a compact, source-hash-bound reference for the Annex B RMS delay-spread scalars of fixed TDL A10/A30/B100/C60/C300/D10/D30. Native regression definitions compare each emitted profile metadata value with its ETSI scalar and bind the fixture to the pinned TS 38.101-4 V19.2.2 source identity. The reference omits the normative tap tables and makes no statistical conformance claim; validation remains deferred.

**Edition 3.570 G3 paired NR receiver evaluator contract hardening (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Reconciled the backlog with the existing `g3_receiver_impact` scorer and its contract-bound joint-evaluator integration, which already provide paired common-SNR BLER effects, cluster-bootstrap intervals, bracketed SNR@10% shifts without extrapolation, transport-block precision, and receiver configuration-row digest binding. Hardened direct evaluator inputs so malformed/unhashable configuration IDs, LOS/NLOS labels, and expected strata fail with explicit contract errors. Regression definitions are authored but unrun. Numerical outputs remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`; this does not establish receiver execution truth or external scientific authority.

**Edition 3.569 G5.09 exact receiver CRC accounting (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The independent-channel ordered-impairment producer now counts CRC failures directly from the retained per-block Boolean outcomes and computes block BLER from that exact integer numerator. A regression definition cross-checks each condition against the complete channel/block roster. The report contract and frozen fixture criteria are unchanged; no receiver qualification is added, and no validation has run.

**Edition 3.568 G4 normative-reference source identity audit (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a versioned identity-only manifest for ETSI TR 38.901 v19.2.0 and TS 38.101-4 v19.2.2 and a bounded CLI auditor for exact locally downloaded PDF bytes. Strict schemas define the manifest and audit report. The audit uses stable, bounded, single-link regular-file reads, rejects duplicate JSON keys and unsupported manifest fields, and reports source-byte success while retaining `NOT_QUALIFIED`. Regression definitions cover matching bytes, tampering, missing/unsafe inputs, manifest rejection, report-schema conformance, and CLI output; no validation has run. ETSI PDFs are not checked into the repository. This pins source identity only; extraction, independent parameter comparison, and G4 statistical conformance remain open.

**Edition 3.567 G5.14 same-grid SRS inter-cell occupancy audit (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a typed SRS resource-occurrence contract and bounded audit that expands resolved frequency allocations into slot-symbol-subcarrier coordinates on one caller-defined common grid. The versioned diagnostic reports exact cross-cell shared RE coordinates up to a fixed cap and aggregate pairwise resource overlap counts. Regression definitions cover overlapping resources, disjoint comb/slot resources, and incomparable grid contexts. It explicitly does not infer cyclic-shift orthogonality, RF interference, or receiver impact; full DCI/RRC, positioning-report integration, and end-to-end receiver qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.566 G3 diffuse-path distribution evaluator integration (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added joint evaluator input v6 and roster diagnostic schema v2. Contract-v4 runs now bind the exact frozen solver-budget object into each raw-path report. Deterministic/specular paths continue through identity/class matching; diffuse stochastic paths are summarized independently by unweighted and coefficient-power-weighted delay, power, zenith, and wrapped azimuth-offset quantiles, circular azimuth-center shifts, and normalized power quantiles. No diffuse ray identities or acceptance thresholds are introduced, and finite solver caps are explicitly not ground truth. Regression definitions cover v6 schema admission, source-bundle pinning, budget binding, report fields, and legacy replay boundaries. Consolidated validation is deferred; reports remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`, G3 criteria remain open, and production RT stays gated.

**Edition 3.565 G3 diffuse-path distribution diagnostic primitive (2026-10-26, integration pending):** Added a descriptive comparison primitive for stochastic diffuse path sets: fixed 5/50/95% unweighted and coefficient-power-weighted quantiles for absolute delay, path power, zenith, and wrapped azimuth offsets; circular azimuth-center deltas; and a hash-bound frozen solver-cap object. It rejects deterministic/specular inputs, never assigns stochastic ray identities, invents no acceptance thresholds, and reports finite solver caps as non-ground-truth provenance. Regression definitions are authored but unrun. Versioned input/schema, run-contract binding, joint-report integration, and scientific qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.564 G7 strict split-registry JSON admission (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The bounded registry loader now maps invalid UTF-8/JSON and parser recursion to explicit rejection, requires object roots/containers, validates exact v2/v3 field rosters, and rejects Boolean/float schema-version aliases before reconstruction. Existing duplicate-key, digest, and canonical-reconstruction checks remain. Regression definitions cover bad encoding/JSON, root/field/container shapes, and numeric version aliases; no checks have run. This is fail-closed parsing, not split-lineage completeness, external authority, or release qualification.

**Edition 3.563 G4 CDL metadata single-snapshot table binding (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** CDL profile metadata now parses and hashes one `Traversable.read_bytes()` snapshot instead of parsing through one resource read and hashing a second. A dependency-isolated regression definition asserts one read, the exact-byte digest, and values derived from that payload. No checks have run. This binds metadata interpretation to its digest; it does not establish independent standards-table correctness or prove the Sionna channel generator's internal table read used the same file handle.

**Edition 3.562 G5 simultaneous estimator-curve uncertainty (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The paired TDL-PUSCH LS versus finite-delay-DFT comparator now emits schema v3. In addition to pointwise batch-cluster intervals, it resamples a shared cluster roster jointly across SNR and reports a simultaneous familywise 95% max-absolute-centered-deviation band. It rejects SNR grids whose cluster rosters differ and bounds bootstrap draw-by-grid allocation. Regression definitions cover duplicated-point agreement, roster mismatch, and allocation limits; no checks have run. This remains conditional synthetic evidence, not G5.09 qualification or site/measurement generalization.

**Edition 3.561 G7 bounded split-policy trust inputs (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Split-policy registry locks and signed payloads now use stable snapshots with a 256 MiB cap, detached signatures are capped at 1 MiB, and trusted `allowed_signers` files at 4 MiB. The reader streams bounded chunks and retains before/after file identity checks. Regression definitions cover exact-limit acceptance, oversize rejection, and invalid limits; no checks have run. This constrains verifier input and memory use only; trust-file custody, authoritative corpus completeness, and release qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.560 G3 raw path-equivalence recomputation (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added joint-evaluator input v5, which carries solver-valid deterministic candidate/challenger paths and full CFR components instead of a supplied comparison result. The evaluator constructs effective path classes, recomputes one-to-one matches, coverage, and receiver relevance using the declared configuration, and binds each raw episode payload by SHA-256. Qualification contract v4 binds all parameters used by that computation; new contract-bound runs require contract v4/input v5, validate the v5 input schema before scoring, and include both new schemas in the source bundle. Contract v3/input v4 remains supported for historical diagnostic receipts. Regression definitions cover raw computation, exact field rosters, schema/source binding, successful receipt replay, and frozen-parameter drift rejection. No validation has run; reports remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`, G3 is open, and production RT remains gated.

**Edition 3.539 G3 raw path scorer parameter contract v4 (2026-10-26, implementation in progress):** Added G3 qualification contract v4 to bind vertex and direction tolerances, complex-CSI floor, and transmit/noise power per resource element alongside the prior delay and coverage settings. V2/v3 verification remains supported. Regression definitions cover valid reconstruction, schema conformance, and invalid parameter rejection; they are unrun. Contract-bound joint evaluation still admits the v4 comparison-report input and does not yet recompute scores from raw path records, so this is a prerequisite only and creates no qualification evidence.

**Edition 3.538 G7 strict split identity inputs in the legacy split API (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** `make_independent_split()` and `audit_group_leakage()` now require nonempty string sample/group identities and no longer coerce missing or numeric values into plausible split keys. The seed and identity-field names are also checked before processing. Regression definitions cover missing, blank, numeric, and malformed audit identities; checks remain deferred. Existing string-ID assignments retain their deterministic behavior. This hardens input integrity only and does not establish complete lineage, authoritative inventory, or release qualification.

**Edition 3.537 G5 sample-domain per-curve batch-cluster uncertainty (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Sample-domain TDL-PUSCH SNR curves now report a deterministic 95% percentile interval formed by resampling whole batch-seed clusters, alongside the legacy codeword-level Wilson diagnostic. The report records the independent batch count, bootstrap seed/draw count, and emits a null interval when fewer than two clusters are available. Regression definitions cover deterministic resampling, input rejection, and the one-cluster case; none have run. This is conditional on the synthetic batches and does not establish independent-scene/site uncertainty or change G5 acceptance criteria.

**Edition 3.536 G7 NR target reason-code persistence (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** `NRChannelRecord` now requires each false-masked target element to be zero-filled and paired with a non-`VALID` `TargetValidity`; true-mask elements must use `VALID`. The reason series is exposed by `NRTrajectory` and stored compactly in NR record v2 with a checked codebook. The reader remains compatible with v1 and marks missing legacy reasons as `legacy_reason_unrecorded`, zeroing masked historical values during migration. The RT trajectory generator labels no-path CFR targets `not_observable`. Regression definitions cover mask/reason drift, round trips, and v1 migration. This improves target contract integrity; complete semantic target constraints and qualification remain open, and no checks have run.

**Edition 3.535 G5 paired LS/DFT sample-domain receiver curves (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Sample-domain TDL-PUSCH curve v2 retains a batch-seed cluster ID for every codeword. The paired comparator accepts only Sionna-LS versus finite-delay-DFT curves with matched payload hashes, channel-truth hashes, noise streams, batch seeds, codeword counts, and SNR grid, then computes paired BLER-difference intervals by resampling batch clusters. Runner and comparator CLIs and reproduction guidance now expose the path; v1 curves remain readable. Regression definitions are authored but unrun. This is a conditional simulation diagnostic with ideal synchronization, not confirmatory G5.09 evidence or field/scene qualification.

**Edition 3.534 G5.09 receiver/transport-block identity contract (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** New ordered-impairment reports v15–v18 bind the complete Sionna PUSCH adapter and decoder/estimator configuration to runtime version metadata and retain an explicit ordered transport-block roster beneath each independent channel. The auditor checks payload-seed derivation, stable channel/payload/block IDs, per-condition CRC alignment, and shared timing/CFO values against channel-level records; earlier report schemas remain supported. Regression definitions are authored but unrun. This closes a traceability gap in the bounded synthetic receiver fixture only; one fixed AWGN point, synthetic channel scope, multi-configuration receiver evaluation, G5.09 qualification, and production RT authorization remain open.

**Edition 3.533 G7.04 split/family NR trajectory target-support audit (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported aggregator for target support over an explicit split × family × target roster and wired it into the UMCD corpus audit. Each trajectory manifest is hash-bound and must be present in the exact manifest inventory; its split and source family derive from an inventoried parent episode and the frozen registry/bundle. Missing targets, empty expected cells, and duplicate sample use remain visible or fail closed. The versioned report schema and regression definitions cover target masks, missing support, sample reuse, and unlisted trajectory manifests. This is input-scoped coverage only; checks remain deferred and release qualification is not claimed.

**Edition 3.528 G7.08 signed child artifact semantic row coverage (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Extended the signed per-kind child artifact audit to require ChannelDataset NPZ artifacts and verify each row's child ID, child kind, and parent subject against the frozen registry binding. The auditor extracts only the bounded `metadata_json.npy` member after streamed byte/hash verification and emits row counts and metadata digests; `children_audited` is true only after exact child roster and row-semantic checks pass. Regression definitions cover valid rows and wrong child/parent bindings. The audit remains input-scoped and does not establish complete-corpus authority, acquisition provenance, or release qualification; no checks have run.

**Edition 3.527 G5.14 DCI 0_3/1_3 decoded SRS request-list resolution (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported resolver for a decoded request-list index and per-cell request row. It applies the per-cell two-bit typeB SRS request in ascending serving-cell order, including the optional leading SUL selector and carrier-scoped resource-set matching. Regression definitions cover multi-cell trigger selection, single-cell SUL routing, and malformed indexes/rosters. This does not decode DCI bits or RRC ASN.1, resolve multi-cell scheduled-cell combinations or SRS offsets, implement typeA, or qualify G5.14. No checks have run. Mapping follows [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, Table 7.3.1.1.2-24 and DCI 0_3/1_3 SRS request-list definitions](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.526 G5.14 configured SRS request modes and supplementary-uplink routing (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The typeB resolver requires the configured `srs-RequestDCI-*` mode for DCI 0_2/1_2 (one- or two-bit request field) and retains the two-bit request field for DCI 0_1/1_1. Optional supplementary-uplink configuration adds a leading non-SUL/SUL carrier selector, and selected resource sets are scoped using explicit carrier metadata. The schedule-composition API propagates these parameters. Regression definitions cover the configured modes, field bounds, missing configuration, and carrier routing; no checks have run. DCI 0_3/1_3, typeA, full ASN.1 decoding, and receiver conformance remain open. [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, Tables 7.3.1.1.1-1, 7.3.1.1.2-24, and 7.3.1.1.3-1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.508 G7 manifest-path completeness (2026-10-25, validation deferred):** Corpus auditing now enforces canonical episode-manifest paths and exact relative-path set equality, with a regression for same-basename aliases. The corpus remains unqualified; validation is deferred to the consolidated pass.

**Edition 3.507 G4 dual-end directional patterns (2026-10-25, validation deferred):** Added a protocol and fresh-seed native regression covering paired directional patterns and distinct 3D orientations at both CDL endpoints, with an independent table-derived cluster-power reference. It remains unrun and does not establish broad G4 or physical-scene validity.

**Edition 3.506 G3 source-bound run receipt (2026-10-25, validation deferred):** The joint evaluator run manifest now records and checks the complete fixed evaluator/scorer/helper/schema dependency roster and aggregate digest as well as input/output bytes. This is current-source reproducibility evidence only; runtime-loaded code identity, third-party dependency identity, provenance, reviewer authority, and scientific qualification remain unresolved.

**Edition 3.505 G3 joint run-manifest implementation (2026-10-25, validation deferred):** The numerical five-branch evaluator now has a standalone manifest format and verifier that binds exact input/output files and replays the captured input. This does not upgrade the acquisition package or imply provenance or qualification. Regression definitions are authored and remain unrun.

**Edition 3.505 G3 joint run-manifest implementation (2026-10-25, validation deferred):** The numerical five-branch evaluator now has a standalone manifest format and verifier that binds exact input/output files and replays the captured input. This does not upgrade the acquisition package or imply provenance or qualification. Regression definitions are authored and remain unrun.

**Edition 3.504 G3 five-branch file-bound evaluator artifact (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added serialized canonical JSON input/output and deterministic replay for all five G3 scorer branches, with exact input-byte binding and explicit nonqualification status. Regression definitions are unrun. This remains separate from signed G3.03 acquisition packaging and does not close G3 or authorize production D1.

**Edition 3.503 G5.14 selected SRS resource-set scheduling composition (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a supported typeB DCI SRS-request path that selects matching aperiodic resource sets and resolves each selected set into target-slot timing plus per-symbol frequency allocations.
