Regression definitions are unrun. Full RRC/DCI variants, symbol-level timing feasibility, complete multiport scheduling, and G5.14 qualification remain open. Source: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, Table 7.3.1.1.2-24](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.502 G5.14 SRS request-to-resource-set resolution (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported resolver for SRS request values on DCI formats 0_1/0_2/1_1/1_2 with `srs-TPC-PDCCH-Group=typeB`; configured primary and trigger-list values select matching aperiodic resource sets. TypeA and DCI 0_3/1_3 are rejected. Regression definitions remain unrun; full RRC/DCI scheduling and G5.14 qualification remain open. Source: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, Table 7.3.1.1.2-24](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.524 G5.14 DCI-specific SRS request width (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Identified that the resolver must distinguish SRS-request bit widths by DCI format. The initial format distinction was subsequently expanded in edition 3.525 to cover the configured 0_2/1_2 request mode and SUL carrier-selection bit. This does not implement the separate multi-cell 0_3/1_3 request-list mapping or typeA antenna-switching mode. [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, Tables 7.3.1.1.3-1 and 7.3.1.1.1-1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.525 G5.14 configured SRS request modes and SUL selection (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The typeB request resolver now requires the configured `srs-RequestDCI-0-2/1-2` mode for formats 0_2/1_2, implements mode 1 (one request bit) and mode 2 (two request bits), and decodes the leading non-SUL/SUL bit when supplementary uplink is configured. Resource-set records must identify their carrier in SUL configurations, and selection is scoped to that carrier. Formats 0_1/1_1 retain their two-bit trigger code plus the optional SUL selector. Regression definitions cover both modes, carrier scoping, invalid widths, and missing configuration. No checks have run; DCI 0_3/1_3 request-list mapping, typeA antenna switching, complete ASN.1 RRC decoding, and receiver conformance remain open. [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, Tables 7.3.1.1.1-1, 7.3.1.1.2-24, and 7.3.1.1.3-1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.501 G5.14 aperiodic SRS schedule composition (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported typed adapter that binds caller-selected aperiodic target-slot timing to each resource-symbol type-1 frequency allocation and hop counter. DCI-to-resource-set selection and complete RRC decoding remain caller responsibilities; this does not close G5.14 conformance. Regression definitions cover selected offset mapping, target-slot identity, per-symbol hops, and missing-index rejection. No checks have run; production D1 remains gated.

**Edition 3.496 G5.14 SRS multiport transmit-power normalization (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Updated ordinary, positioning, and frequency-hopped multiport mappers to apply the TS 38.211 `1/sqrt(N_ap)` sequence amplitude factor before resource-grid placement. The four-port three-transmit option uses the specified effective port count of three and rejects use with any port count other than four. Regression definitions update two-port β scaling and cover the four-port special case and its invalid configuration. Caller-supplied port sequences remain caller-scheduled; no standards-derived orthogonal schedule is claimed. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.495 G5.14 caller-scheduled multiport hopped SRS mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Extended the per-symbol frequency-hop mapper to `[ports, SRS symbols, occupied REs]` inputs and added immutable per-port result grids that retain the shared hop-counter, position-index, positioning-offset, and RE rosters. This maps caller-supplied port sequences and does not derive standardized port schedules or orthogonalization. Regression definitions cover two ports across distinct frequency hops with positioning offsets, exact RE placement, masks, and read-only outputs. Higher-layer schedule resolution, standards-derived port scheduling, independent conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.494 G5.14 per-symbol SRS frequency-hop grid mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a one-port resource mapper that accepts a resolved frequency allocation per SRS symbol, preserves each allocation's `n-SRS`/frequency-tree roster, and supports the positioning-resource comb offset on top of the hop position. A single allocation may also be broadcast across the resource symbols for fixed-position mapping. Regression definitions cover distinct hop positions across symbols and the combined hopping/positioning path. The mapper consumes caller-resolved allocations; standards-driven higher-layer scheduling, multiport hopped mapping, independent conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.493 G5.14 SRS transmission-counter resolution from scheduled occasion data (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added TS 38.211 `n-SRS` counter calculation for aperiodic and periodic/semi-persistent resources from already-resolved resource-symbol index, repetition factor, eight-port TDM mode, absolute slot, periodicity, and slot offset. Off-schedule periodic slots return no transmission. Regression definitions cover repetition groups, eight-port TDM, occasion increments, and off-schedule handling. Resolving `T-SRS`/`T-offset` from RRC configuration and full resource/set scheduling remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.492 G5.14 type-1 SRS frequency hopping for explicit transmission counters (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Implemented the TS 38.211 frequency-tree index recurrence for `b-hop < b-SRS`, including the specified unit radix at the hopping level and even/odd bandwidth-count cases. The fixed-allocation resolver now accepts caller-supplied `n-SRS` and derives the allocation for that transmission; it fails closed when active hopping lacks the counter. Regression definitions include a hand-enumerated eight-counter sequence from a standardized table row, allocation binding, required-counter behavior, and existing no-hop table-tree cases. Counter derivation from periodic/semi-persistent schedules, aperiodic resource scheduling, and higher-layer configuration remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.491 G5.14 caller-scheduled multiport positioning SRS mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported `[ports, SRS symbols, occupied REs]` mapper for `SRS-PosResource` that reuses the per-symbol positioning-offset implementation and returns per-port grids, masks, symbol-wise RE rosters, and offsets. It accepts caller-provided port sequences and therefore does not claim standards-derived port scheduling or orthogonalization. Regression definitions cover two distinct ports across four positioning symbols and mask/index integrity. No tests or static checks have run. Active frequency hopping, higher-layer resource/set scheduling, independent conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.490 G5.14 positioning SRS per-symbol resource-grid mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a single-port `SRS-PosResource` mapper that applies the TS 38.211 Table 6.4.1.4.3-2 offset independently at each resource symbol and records each symbol's actual subcarrier roster. The fixed frequency origin and bandwidth are caller-resolved; ordinary `SRS-Resource` mapping remains on the zero-offset path. Regression definitions cover four-symbol placement, per-symbol sequence rows, one-row broadcast with beta scaling, exact masks and RE indices, immutable views, and invalid configurations. A malformed duplicate-keyword regression was corrected; no tests or static checks have run. Multiport positioning resources, active frequency hopping, higher-layer resource/set scheduling, independent conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3 and Table 6.4.1.4.3-2](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.489 G5.14 fixed-position SRS frequency-tree and positioning-offset resolution (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a TS 38.211 type-1 no-frequency-hopping resolver that computes every `n_b` from the standardized `c-SRS`/`b-SRS` table and `freqDomainPosition`, `freqDomainShift`, and comb offset, returning a validated allocation for the existing mapper. Added all supported `SRS-PosResource` per-symbol offsets from Table 6.4.1.4.3-2; ordinary `SRS-Resource` correctly uses zero. Active frequency hopping fails closed because its counter/tree sequence is not implemented yet. Regression definitions cover every supported positioning-table row, a multi-level allocation and mapped RE bounds, invalid inputs, and active-hopping rejection. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3 and Tables 6.4.1.4.3-1/2](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.488 G3 joint numerical-evaluator orchestration (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an in-memory runner and recomputation verifier that binds the material-calibration, complex-CSI, propagation-metric, receiver-impact, and diagnostic path-equivalence scorers to one validated acceptance-family-v4 digest. Per-branch input/output hashes make the numerical reports reproducible and detect bundle tampering; all output remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`, and the runner explicitly cannot prove external measurement truth, provenance, independent units, preregistration, representativeness, reviewer authority, or rights. Regression definitions cover five-branch recomputation, common-family binding, tampering, input-byte sensitivity, malformed/mixed inputs, v3-family rejection, and nonfinite evidence. No checks have run; G3 acceptance criteria and evidence gates are unchanged.

**Edition 3.487 G5.14 complete SRS bandwidth table lookup (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Expanded the fixed-bandwidth resolver to all 64 `c-SRS` rows and all four `b-SRS` levels (`m_SRS,b` and `N_b` from TS 38.211 Table 6.4.1.4.3-1). The returned sequence length and occupied-subcarrier extent follow the selected row and transmission comb. Regression definitions retain all 64 `b-SRS=0` values and check representative multi-subband entries through `b-SRS=3`, invalid levels, and consistency of the public value object. Frequency-position/hopping derivation and resource scheduling remain open; no tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, Table 6.4.1.4.3-1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.487 G5.14 complete SRS bandwidth table lookup (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Expanded the fixed-bandwidth resolver to all 64 `c-SRS` rows and all four `b-SRS` levels (`m_SRS,b` and `N_b` from TS 38.211 Table 6.4.1.4.3-1). The returned sequence length and occupied-subcarrier extent follow the selected row and transmission comb. Regression definitions retain all 64 `b-SRS=0` values and check representative multi-subband entries through `b-SRS=3`, invalid levels, and consistency of the public value object. Frequency-position/hopping derivation and resource scheduling remain open; no tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, Table 6.4.1.4.3-1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.486 G5.14 fixed-bandwidth SRS allocation extent (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added all 64 standardized `c-SRS` `m_SRS,0` bandwidth entries for `b-SRS=0`, with comb-derived sequence lengths, and bounded single-/multiport mapping to an allocated bandwidth inside a wider carrier grid. Regression definitions cover every table row for combs 2/4/8, invalid configuration values, and no-write-outside-allocation placement. Frequency-position derivation, `b-SRS>0`, frequency hopping, complete higher-layer scheduling, and standards/receiver qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, Table 6.4.1.4.3-1 and clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.485 G5.14 caller-scheduled multiport SRS code path (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added explicit cyclic-shift sequence generation per transmit port and a `[ports, symbols, subcarriers]` grid mapper with immutable masks. Regression definitions cover distinct-shift orthogonality for a 72-element sequence, multiport mapping, invalid/duplicate shift schedules, shape errors, and mask integrity. This implements caller-specified shifts; deriving standards-valid port schedules and frequency allocations from higher-layer configuration, broader SRS conformance, and receiver-impact qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Normative sequence basis: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clauses 5.2.2 and 6.4.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.468 G5.14 bounded SRS low-PAPR sequence generation (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added TS 38.211 type-1 low-PAPR sequence generation for explicit sequence lengths ≥36, group indices, base-sequence indices, and cyclic-shift indices, including the prime-length root construction. Short-sequence tables, derivation of valid SRS parameters from higher-layer configuration, sequence/group hopping, per-port cyclic-shift scheduling, and full SRS conformance remain open. Independent scalar-formula regression definitions are authored but unrun. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_TS/138200_138299/138211/19.01.00_60/ts_138211v190100p.pdf), clauses 5.2.2.1 and 6.4.1.4.2.

**Edition 3.467 bounded G5.14 SRS resource-grid mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a one-port, one-hop mapper for caller-supplied complex SRS sequence rows. It applies the slot-end-relative SRS symbol start, transmission comb sizes 2/4/8, comb offset, sequence-to-RE placement, and an explicit resource mask. The implementation follows the bounded mapping contract in [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_TS/138200_138299/138211/19.01.00_60/ts_138211v190100p.pdf), clauses 6.4.1.4.1–6.4.1.4.3. Sequence generation, c-SRS/b-SRS allocation derivation, hopping, multiport orthogonalization, scheduling, and independent standards validation remain open; regression definitions are authored but unrun.

**Edition 3.466 G7.09 split parent/child ID collision guard (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Frozen split registries now reject a child artifact ID that collides with any statistical parent ID. This prevents consumers that resolve parent IDs before child IDs from silently assigning the parent interpretation to a child and bypassing its explicit inheritance binding. A regression definition covers the ambiguous-ID case; validation is pending and no corpus split is qualified.

**Edition 3.465 G7.07 legacy CFR input firewall (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added `ChannelDataset.model_inputs()` returning a read-only CFR-only view, and routed legacy experiment feature construction through it. The view exposes no labels, grouping, domain/source IDs, metadata, or generation report. Regression definitions assert field isolation and read-only behavior. This formalizes the current classifier input boundary but does not audit every downstream adapter or prove corpus-wide leakage absence; no tests or static checks have run.

**Edition 3.464 G5.09 receive-time path activity envelopes (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The sampled multipath operator now accepts finite real per-path activity envelopes in `[0,1]` over receive samples, allowing callers to express path appearance, disappearance, and fading while retaining the existing constant-coefficient behavior by default. Regression definitions cover active/inactive transitions with nonzero delay and malformed envelopes. This is a bounded synthetic mechanism, not a validated path birth/death law or deployment channel model; no tests or static checks have run, and G5.09 remains open.

**Edition 3.463 G3 matched path-class diagnostic integrity (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The roster evaluator now rejects matched class records with missing, negative, boolean, or non-finite vertex, delay, direction, or aggregate complex-CFR deltas. These values remain diagnostic because the frozen criteria do not define a separate per-class margin; CSI and delay/angular population limits are evaluated by their dedicated G3 scorers. Regression definitions cover malformed matched-class deltas. No tests or static checks have run; G3 qualification and production RT remain gated.

**Edition 3.462 G4.07 TDL-A–E receive-covariance family authored (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added five CPU-native cases under one simultaneous 99% family covering every real/imaginary entry of a configured complex 4×4 receiver covariance matrix across scalable TDL-A–E. TDL-D/E use a diffuse tap to keep the estimand separate from the specular LOS atom. These are regression definitions only; they have not run and do not establish full fading or receiver conformance.

**Edition 3.461 G5.09 NPY metadata preflight (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The retained-evidence auditor now inspects ZIP roster/expanded sizes and each NPY header before `np.load`, rejecting unsupported versions/dtypes and shape-to-payload mismatches. This catches forged allocation metadata before array materialization while preserving the compressed and uncompressed caps. A forged-shape regression is authored; no tests or audit have run.

**Edition 3.460 G5.09 avoid duplicate decompressed waveform arrays (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The auditor now retains the arrays materialized by `NpzFile` after archive-roster and aggregate uncompressed-size checks instead of copying them a second time. This reduces the evidence-loading peak without changing the accepted 512-MiB compressed/1-GiB uncompressed contract; bounded memory is not a receiver qualification result.

**Edition 3.459 G3 material angular-array strict parsing (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Extended the shared G3 real-number parser to shaped arrays and applied it to measured/predicted material angular-bin power and solid-angle matrices. Coercible booleans, strings, complex values, and non-finite cells now fail before scoring. Regression definitions cover each matrix field; this does not establish measurement validity or qualify G3.

**Edition 3.458 G3 shared strict real-number parsing (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a shared scalar/vector parser and applied it to receiver-impact scoring and scenario assurance. Their SNR grids, comparison SNR, observed record SNRs, and scenario probabilities reject boolean/string/complex/non-finite coercions. Regression definitions cover each boundary. This improves evidence-input integrity only; qualification and production RT remain gated.

**Edition 3.457 G3 receiver scorer strict SNR inputs (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The paired NR receiver-impact scorer now uses the shared strict real-number parser for its SNR grid, comparison point, and per-outcome SNR. This is evidence-input integrity only; no receiver qualification or production RT authorization follows.

**Edition 3.454 G5.09 streaming waveform-evidence budget regressions (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added regression definitions for both rejection boundaries: cumulative raw NPY payload above the uncompressed cap and ZIP output crossing the compressed high-water cap. **Edition 3.453 G5.09 streaming waveform-evidence archive (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Replaced full-channel `np.stack` temporaries with channel-wise NPY member writes into the size-limited compressed NPZ buffer. This bounds archive construction memory beyond the stored channel chunks but does not qualify receiver performance; tests and receiver runs remain deferred.
**Edition 3.452 G5.09 sidecar destination safety (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The receiver rejects unsafe/non-NPZ evidence names and existing destinations before initializing the receiver, then publishes atomically without overwrite; the auditor applies the same sibling-name rule. **Edition 3.451 G5.09 waveform-evidence manifest contract (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added the versioned JSON Schema for the raw waveform sidecar metadata. This formalizes the retained artifact contract only; no new result was generated and G5.09 remains open.
**Edition 3.450 G5.09 bounded waveform-evidence writer (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The ordered-impairment runner now enforces the same 1-GiB uncompressed and 512-MiB compressed waveform-evidence limits as its auditor. Per-channel tensor bytes are accounted before retention, and NPZ writes use a high-water-limited buffer that fails before exceeding the compressed cap. A regression covers the write cap. No receiver run was made; this evidence-retention path does not qualify G5.09.
**Edition 3.449 G4.07 structured seed-collision audit (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a bounded `.json`/`.json.gz` corpus scanner for reserved integer seeds in seed-named fields, inclusive ranges, and seed-bearing result keys. It records requested roots and eligible-file counts, hashes each scanned file, and reports exact match paths. It rejects ambiguous JSON and linked, changing, or oversized files; numeric string/float encodings are conservative collision candidates and malformed seed fields/ranges block a clear verdict. The two frozen CDL Fourier protocols state their seed ranges and minimum result roots. The scanner has not been run; the required collision audit remains a pre-run gate, and no G4 result or qualification claim is added.
**Edition 3.448 G3 fail-closed path-record parsing (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The deterministic path-equivalence builder and serialized comparison boundary now reject booleans, strings, complex values, non-finite values, and malformed path/class objects rather than allowing NumPy to coerce them into plausible numeric evidence. Class signatures, vertices, delays, and unit endpoint directions are checked structurally. Regression source covers malformed coercible inputs; no result was generated and path equivalence remains diagnostic-only.
**Current implementation update (2026-10-25):** G3 joint-family v4 binds the path-equivalence family/LOS-NLOS/independent-episode roster alongside material, complex-CSI, propagation, and receiver inferential claims. Every episode must independently meet both directional coverage thresholds; these deterministic diagnostic checks are excluded from the inferential Bonferroni count and cannot qualify or admit a model. The evaluator checks the exact family hash and roster, and independently reconciles directional coverage with the reported relevant/matched class counts. A bounded JSON CLI is available as a packaged module and `loslab g3-path-roster-audit`; it uses a versioned input schema and rejects duplicate-key, non-finite/deeply nested, linked, oversized, or changing input files. Acquisition planning and serialized schemas accept v4 alongside v3. Implementation remains unvalidated; production RT generation remains gated.
**Edition 3.447 G4 spherical angular-power primitives (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added reusable full-sphere binning with exact solid-angle weights, normalized integrated power and per-steradian density, explicit pole/azimuth-wrap rules, and the pinned `Rz(alpha) Ry(beta) Rx(gamma)` global-to-element-local transform. The new element-frame mapper composes that inverse rotation with weighted full-sphere binning and records its coordinate frame and orientation convention. Analytic regressions cover uniform spherical density, boundary assignment, orientation, and power conservation. These are numerical support primitives only; no native CDL or geometric-RT angular qualification was run, and G4 remains open.
**Edition 3.446 G5.09 raw waveform evidence path (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The independent shared-sync receiver runner can now retain hash-bound TX samples, oscillator phase trajectories, and impaired captures in a compressed NPZ sidecar; the auditor independently rebuilds the expected and deliberately wrong-order impairment chains with NumPy and checks those diagnostics against the result. The auditor now bounds sidecar bytes before reading and rejects symlinks, hard links, non-regular files, and file changes during the read. The existing v7 result has no waveform sidecar and remains on its prior scalar-recomputation path; no new experiment was run. This adds an independently auditable implementation path but does not qualify the synthetic law, close G5.09, or open production generation.

**Edition 3.438 G4.07 directional-pattern orientation reference (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The CDL-A–E independent 38.901 antenna-pattern power check now adds positive-tilt and mixed-sign Euler orientations and transforms each ray into the element-local frame before evaluating cluster and LOS gain. **Edition 3.431 G4.07 bounded joint four-angle characteristic family (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a fresh, frozen finite-frequency family covering 304 representatives of the conjugate-paired four-angle integer vectors in `{-2,-1,0,1,2}^4` with at least two nonzero entries, across CDL-A–E (3,040 real/imaginary claims). This is one deterministic integration case, not a mobility performance study or broad G5.09 qualification; independent channel families, Doppler/SNR curves, impairments, and receiver robustness remain open.
**Edition 3.362 G4.07 directional CDL element-pattern confidence (2026-10-24):** Added independent pattern-weighted per-cluster power checks for the 38.901 and handheld element patterns over CDL-A–E. One 99% Bonferroni family spans 194 profile/pattern/cluster claims from 16,384 native CPU draws per case; every reference is enclosed and each simultaneous interval meets the 5%-of-total-power precision cap. The reference pins table hashes and separately accounts for the LOS/diffuse merged first cluster. This is a one-element, vertical-polarization, zero-orientation model check, not arbitrary array/pattern or measured-channel validation.
**Edition 3.361 G5.01 Type-1 four-port DM-RS orthogonality (2026-10-24):** Extended the native Type-1 PUSCH DM-RS orthogonality test to two and four ports/layers. The CPU cases check shared pilot RE masks and zero cross-layer pilot Gram entries; the complete native PUSCH integration file, Ruff, formatting, and direct test-module mypy pass. This fills one named port-coverage gap, while other CDM mappings, hopping, transform precoding, noisy receiver qualification, and full standard conformance remain open.
**Edition 3.360 G5 CP-profile audit integrity and family result (2026-10-24):** Hardened the independent audit for five saved TDL-A–E, 60/80/100-dB CP-exceedance curves. It now checks source-file digests against the supplied local snapshot, all 20,480 retained CRC outcomes against each count/BLER/Wilson summary, paired pass tables, and 128 unique parent IDs/truth hashes per report before recomputing 15 paired parent-bootstrap claims under one 95% Bonferroni family. The saved v3 audit confirms positive time-domain-minus-ideal-CFR BLER differences with adjusted intervals excluding zero for A@60, B@60/80, and C@60/80 dB; D/E intervals are narrow because failures are sparse, not because the receiver is broadly qualified. Ruff, format, mypy, and four focused audit tests pass. This remains a synthetic CP-exceedance diagnostic and does not close G5.09 or G3.
**Edition 3.359 artifact-scoped ODbL release declarations (2026-10-24):** Public-release manifest v2 now binds ODbL classification to each artifact–scene pair, so outputs from one scene can receive different reviewed classifications. The validator requires ODbL-1.0 for artifacts classified as databases, rejects scene-wide classifications and duplicate lineage hashes, and requires a structurally valid HTTPS source-data offer URL. Focused tests cover mixed artifact classifications, notices, malformed offers, and fail-closed database licensing. This validates declarations only: it cannot determine legal classification, verify URL availability, or clear the audited OSM scenes for public release.
**Edition 3.358 CDL-A–E four-angle rank-quartile coupling (2026-10-24):** Extended the native Sionna 2.2 CDL ray-angle check beyond all six 20×20 pairwise rank tables with the joint rank-quartile law across AoA/AoD/ZoA/ZoD. For each hash-pinned profile, 8,192 independent diffuse-ray draws test all 4⁴ cells under a separate 99% Bonferroni family of 1,280 claims; each simultaneous interval has half-width ≤0.001. All five profiles pass, along with Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks. This validates coarsened four-angle rank coupling for the pinned standardized diffuse model, not the full continuous angular law, realized complex per-ray fields, arbitrary arrays/patterns, geometric RT, measured channels, or G4 closure.
**Edition 3.357 G5.14 noise-whitened blind cell acquisition (2026-10-24):** Per-chain complex-noise variances now weight the PSS matched-filter score, fractional-timing refinement, CP-based CFO refinement, SSS candidate likelihood, and downstream PBCH MRC. Adversarial two-chain PSS/SSS checks show that an intentionally high-energy wrong-cell branch is suppressed when it has correspondingly high noise variance; the lower-noise branch determines the correct cell identity. The unequal-noise frequency-selective blind PBCH test still recovers the intended SSB and MIB. Focused NR-sync and PBCH receiver suites pass with Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks. This is a controlled synthetic receiver robustness test, not broad G5 or field qualification.
**Edition 3.356 G5.14 per-chain-noise PBCH MRC (2026-10-24):** PBCH known-occasion and blind-acquisition APIs now accept either one shared complex-sample noise variance or one variance per receive chain. Soft combining weights each channel estimate by its branch inverse variance and derives LLR reliability from the summed branch information. Unequal-gain/frequency-selective known-occasion tests verify combined LLRs equal the sum of independently decoded branch LLRs under unequal noise; a blind two-chain acquisition also decodes with unequal branch noise. Malformed length, nonpositive, nonfinite, boolean, and complex variance inputs are rejected. The focused PBCH OFDM receiver file passes with Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks. This is synthetic static-channel receiver behavior, not broad G5 or deployment qualification.
**Edition 3.355 CDL-A–E native pairwise ray-coupling rank check (2026-10-24):** Extended the four one-dimensional profile-table angular marginals with all six pairwise combinations of AoA/AoD/ZoA/ZoD. For each hash-pinned profile, the test samples 8,192 native Sionna 2.2 CDL draws and checks all 20×20 per-ray rank cells against independent random coupling in one conservative 99% Bonferroni family of 12,000 claims. Diffuse cluster powers weight each draw; CDL-D/E specular LOS atoms are excluded from this diffuse ray-coupling statistic and remain covered only by their separate LOS checks. All five profiles pass; Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks pass. This verifies the pinned model's pairwise diffuse ray-angle coupling rule, not realized complex ray coefficients, the full four-angle joint law, arbitrary antenna-pattern response, geometric RT truth, external physical validation, or G4 closure.
**Edition 3.354 G5.14 blind two-chain PSS/SSS and PBCH acquisition (2026-10-24):** Extended controlled blind acquisition to receive-chain matrices. PSS timing/CFO search sums matched-filter energy noncoherently across branches, SSS detection combines branch correlation energies using PSS-derived branch channels, and the aligned PBCH block uses the MRC receiver. A two-branch unequal-gain multipath capture with common CFO recovers timing, PCI, CFO, candidate/half-frame, CRC, and MIB. The combined 43-case native synchronization/PBCH slice passes with Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks. This is one static two-chain synthetic case, not broad antenna/channel coverage, continuous-stream acquisition, or G5 qualification.
**Edition 3.353 G5.14 known-occasion PBCH receive diversity (2026-10-24):** Extended the SSB OFDM receiver to accept one or more complex receive-chain waveforms, estimate PBCH-DM-RS per chain, and perform maximal-ratio combining with equal independent branch-noise variance. The two-branch, unequal-gain frequency-selective test decodes the expected MIB and verifies combined descrambled LLRs equal the sum of the independently decoded branch LLRs. All 21 known-occasion receiver tests pass; Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks pass. This adds synthetic receive-diversity behavior only; blind multi-antenna acquisition, mobility, and broad standards/receiver qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.352 CDL-A–E angular-sector power confidence (2026-10-24):** Extended the table-derived angular-power test to all five CDL profiles. The native CPU reference uses 8,192 independent draws/profile, four cosine sectors for AoA/AoD/ZoA/ZoD, and one 99% Bonferroni family of 80 claims. For CDL-D/E, the first zero-delay tap is partitioned in expectation between its specular LOS atom and first diffuse cluster using the table K-factor; the complete merged tap power remains the stochastic unit. All five profile cases pass, with sector half-widths ≤0.05 normalized power, plus Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks. This remains a model-defined one-dimensional angular marginal, not per-ray realized field truth, joint angular dependence, arbitrary-pattern validation, geometric RT, or physical-channel qualification.
**Edition 3.351 CDL-A/B/C angular-sector power confidence (2026-10-24):** Added a native CPU comparison of stochastic cluster-tap power, distributed over the pinned 20-ray angle atoms, with profile-table angular power in four cosine sectors for AoA/AoD/ZoA/ZoD. The 8,192-draw checks cover all 12 profile/direction combinations under one 99% Bonferroni family of 48 sector claims; every interval includes the table reference and has half-width ≤0.05 normalized power. The three focused profile cases pass with Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks. This is a bounded one-dimensional angular-marginal consistency check for NLOS CDL-A/B/C; it does not establish a full joint angular distribution, arbitrary antenna-pattern response, RT truth, or physical validity.
**Edition 3.350 bounded CDL-D/E coherent LOS-field check (2026-10-24):** Added a native CPU statistical check that uses independent Sionna channel draws and SHA-pinned TR 38.901 v19.2 profiles. For the first composed LOS/diffuse tap, the complex-mean power agrees with the K-factor specular share and the centered complex variance agrees with the first diffuse-cluster share for both CDL-D and CDL-E, under one Bonferroni-adjusted 99% family across four claims. All 10 focused LOS moment/composition tests pass; Ruff, formatting, mypy, and whitespace checks pass. This verifies a bounded standardized-model composition observable only; it does not establish geometric-RT truth, external physical validity, or G4 closure, and production RT remains gated.
**Edition 3.349 G3 package-wide external-attestation audit (2026-10-24):** Added a batch verifier that loads and checks the package once, verifies each detached signature against the same package/trust snapshots, and requires exactly one valid sidecar per package-declared attestation record. Its report lists covered types and signer count but does not infer reviewer independence; qualification remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`. The CLI is `scripts/audit_g3_attestation_set.py`. Fourteen focused signature tests and the combined 55-case G3 package/signature slice pass with Ruff, format, mypy, and whitespace checks. Actual signer independence and lab evidence remain external; G3 and production RT stay gated.
**Edition 3.348 G3 signer-role authorization (2026-10-24):** Strengthened the detached-attestation verifier with a second externally governed trust input that binds each OpenSSH principal to an organization, permitted G3 attestation types, and signer roles. A cryptographically valid signature now fails if its principal is absent or its declared role, organization, or attestation type is outside that policy. The exact trust-policy digest is included in the result; the policy itself must be governed outside the acquisition package. Eleven focused signature tests pass, including role/type/organization rejection. This authenticates configured authorization metadata only; it does not prove independence or the signed claim. No institutional trust registry or lab evidence exists, and G3/production RT remain gated.
**Edition 3.347 G3 external-attestation signature verifier (2026-10-24):** Added a detached OpenSSH-signature envelope that binds a named external claim to the exact G3 proposal-package bytes and the SHA-256/size entries for its subject files. Verification uses a separate trusted `allowed_signers` file and a G3-specific signature namespace; the package consistency verifier also runs before a signature can be reported as verified. Eight tests exercise v0.2/v0.3 packages, valid signatures, exact-byte/subject tampering, untrusted principals, inconsistent inventory, and hard-linked signatures. The report explicitly leaves scientific truth, signer independence, and qualification unassessed. No lab attestation or institutional trust key has been supplied; G3 and production RT remain gated.
**Edition 3.346 G3.03 package requires joint acceptance family (2026-10-24):** The v0.3 acquisition-package verifier now rejects scorer inputs that omit the hash-bound family spanning material-calibration and complex-CSI claims. Legacy v0.2 remains unchanged. This prevents a structurally consistent v0.3 package from silently falling back to a material-only multiplicity family; it does not authenticate external review or qualify measurements. The 41 focused package/schema tests, Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks pass. G3 remains unqualified and production RT stays gated.
**Edition 3.345 CAEZ official-versus-preprocessed count audit (2026-10-24):** Reconciled the current ETH dataset-page sample counts against the already audited, Git-ignored local NPZ reports and the official preprocessed-directory listing. Deltas are −1 indoor, −17 outdoor, and +2,000 hallway. Official processing code documents timestamp filtering for all three and optional spatial filtering for indoor/outdoor, which may account for some losses but cannot be asserted without exact input/version and retained-row lineage; filtering alone cannot explain a larger hallway output against the same input. The official listing has no remote checksums. The metadata-only report preserves these as unresolved and keeps CAEZ exploratory; it adds no LOS/NLOS or G3 material evidence and follows the no-redistribution license.
**Edition 3.344 G5.09 independent retained-data audit (2026-10-24):** Added a standalone auditor that recomputes the one-time shared-sync confirmation's retained timing/CFO arrays, channel-cluster BLER summaries, paired channel bootstrap, CFO cluster-tail bounds, and frozen acceptance verdict, while checking result/configuration/protocol/source digests. All derivable checks pass for the 64-channel × 8-block artifact; the exact experiment source matches the current worktree, but was uncommitted when run. Verify the source blob against the eventual research implementation commit without repeating the frozen seed set. The two reported scalar checks for which raw waveforms were not retained remain unverified independently. This is conditional synthetic evidence only; G5 remains open and RT production stays gated.
**Edition 3.343 G4 CDL-D/E diffuse ray-table geometry (2026-10-24):** Extended the native ray-angle reconstruction check to the LOS CDL-D/E tables. The test explicitly excludes the table's first, separately composed specular LOS row, then verifies all remaining diffuse-cluster powers and all 20 AoA/AoD/ZoA/ZoD rays per cluster against SHA-pinned TR 38.901 v19.2 tables. The focused G4.07 file passes 17 cases. This closes only deterministic diffuse-ray construction for CDL-D/E; coherent LOS composition, stochastic angular-power confidence, general arrays/patterns, and geometric-RT/material validity remain open. G4 is not closed and RT production stays gated.
**Edition 3.342 G3 complex-CSI bootstrap assurance (2026-10-24):** Extended the assumption-only acquisition planner to model CSI scene-cluster summaries and run the evaluator-aligned cluster median, cluster p95, Bonferroni percentile-bootstrap upper bound, and exact cluster-failure upper-bound rules. The v3 JSON schema/CLI requires scenarios to exactly match the shared joint-family LOS/NLOS strata, uses the same familywise claim allocation, reports CSI and material acquisition units separately, and always remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`. Synthetic CLI/planner tests, Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks pass. Hypothetical scenario distributions and all G3 measurement, provenance, independence, representativeness, and preregistration evidence remain open; this does not authorize production RT.
**Edition 3.341 G4.07 CDL ray-angle distribution geometry (2026-10-24):** Added a native Sionna 2.2 check of the complete per-cluster 20-ray angular atom sets and normalized cluster powers for AoA/AoD/ZoA/ZoD in hash-pinned CDL-A/B/C profiles. All three pass against independently reconstructed TR 38.901 v19.2 tables within 1e-4 degrees. The 15-case focused file and Ruff/format/mypy pass. This closes selected deterministic ray-generation geometry only; it is not statistical angular-power confidence evidence and does not cover LOS profiles, general antenna patterns, arbitrary arrays, or RT/material validity.
**Edition 3.340 G5.09 shared CP synchronization confirmation (2026-10-24):** The predeclared one-time run on independent seeds 991001–991064 (64 channels, eight blocks/channel) passes every frozen synthetic-fixture limit with shared per-channel timing/CFO acquisition and fixed 2-/6-sample CP guards: zero timing error, CFO absolute P95/max 103.32/131.38 Hz, clean/corrected BLER 0/6.64%, reference error 1.02e-6, and wrong-order RMSE 0.00169. Zero of 64 channel clusters had a block exceed either 250 or 350 Hz; the exact one-sided 95% upper bound is 0.04573. The paired corrected-minus-clean channel-bootstrap interval is [0.0469, 0.0879]. An independent retained-data auditor reproduces the block summaries, cluster bootstrap, tail bounds, and verdict. The exact source bytes are present in the worktree and match the artifact hash, but were uncommitted at run time; after the research implementation commit, verify the committed blob with the auditor. Do not rerun this frozen one-time seed set; if the committed source differs, preregister a new independent confirmation. This is a conditional synthetic fixture pass, not broad G5 closure or RT authorization.
**Edition 3.339 G5.09 shared CP synchronization protocol preregistration (2026-10-24):** Before confirmation, froze shared timing/CFO acquisition across eight repeated blocks per independent channel: concatenate blocks on OFDM-symbol boundaries, use a fixed 2-sample timing guard and fixed 6-sample family-maximum CFO guard, then apply one acquired timing/CFO pair to all blocks. The detector receives no path truth. Frozen limits are unchanged; the one confirmation is 64 channels with seeds 991001–991064 and eight blocks/channel, saved to a source-hash-bound result. The separate 990001–990064 development set passes point limits (timing 0; CFO P95/max 91.10/110.08 Hz; corrected BLER 9.96%) but its paired channel-bootstrap interval is [0.0664, 0.1367], so it cannot establish a ≤10% population BLER. The confirmation was subsequently run and audited as recorded in edition 3.340; it remains conditional synthetic evidence and does not authorize production RT.
**Edition 3.338 G5.09 channel-cluster CFO tail audit (2026-10-24):** The independent-channel receiver report now supplements its pooled CFO P95/max values with a conservative exact one-sided bound on the prevalence of channels having any block exceedance at the frozen 250-Hz and 350-Hz limits. Reanalysis of the existing failed fixed-guard diagnostic finds 26/64 and 7/64 such channels, with upper bounds 0.517 and 0.196; this neither changes acceptance limits nor rehabilitates reused development seeds. The new report schema and focused test pass with Ruff, formatting, and targeted mypy. A fresh independent confirmation is still needed after the method is frozen; G5.09 and RT production remain open.
**Edition 3.337 joint G3 assumption-driven sample planning (2026-10-24):** Added acquisition-design schema/CLI v2 and a separate-stratum planner that allocates alpha and power across the shared five-material-plus-two-CSI claim roster. It reports material acquisition-unit and CSI scene-cluster counts separately and refuses to imply one combined sample size. The optional material bootstrap-assurance calculation uses the full family alpha count. Synthetic planner tests pass; hypothetical pass probabilities, angular-rule compatibility, bootstrap assurance, external preregistration, and real qualification evidence remain open.
**Edition 3.336 shared G3 acceptance-family accounting (2026-10-24):** Added a deterministic hash-bound G3 claim roster covering five material-calibration interval claims per material stratum and two complex-CSI upper-bound claims per CSI stratum. Both scorers can verify the same family digest and apply Bonferroni alpha across the complete roster; independent local families remain available for diagnostics and identify themselves as non-joint. The contract and both scorer integrations pass focused tests, Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks. External preregistration, the complete powered design using this total claim count, evidence verification, and G3 qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.335 split-lock signature verification tests (2026-10-24):** Verified the Release Split Policy v2 OpenSSH trust boundary against a temporary external `allowed_signers` file. The focused split-policy suite passes 53 tests; Ruff, formatting, and targeted mypy pass. Adversarial tests reject modified lock bytes, an untrusted principal, and a hard-linked signature. Test keys are ephemeral and establish no institutional signer or release approval; corpus lineage and release audits remain open.
**Edition 3.334 G3.03 full-sphere angular input contract (2026-10-24):** Added evaluator-input v2 with full-sphere integrated-power matrices, explicit coordinate-frame identity, cell solid-angle validation, and fail-closed handling of missing angular bins. Acquisition package v0.3 binds this contract; v0.2 remains a legacy structural proposal. Focused evaluator, package-verifier, and CLI tests pass; Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks pass. The joint CDF metric remains project-defined, material scoring remains pilot-only, and production RT remains gated.
**Edition 3.333 G3 complex-CSI interval multiplicity correction (2026-10-24):** The complex-CSI numerical scorer now uses one Bonferroni 95% family over both inferential claims (cluster-bootstrap p95 upper bound and exact cluster-failure upper bound) for every preregistered scene-family × LOS/NLOS stratum. The frozen physical e_H limits are unchanged; confidence bounds are stricter. This local CSI interval family is not yet integrated with the separate G3.03 material-calibration claims, and the cross-evaluator multiplicity/sample-size design remains unresolved. The scorer is numerical-only and cannot qualify data or authorize production RT.
**Edition 3.332 G5.09 acquisition guard truth-leakage correction (2026-10-24):** Audited the independent-channel ordered-impairment receiver probe and found that its initial synchronizer guard used each realized channel's actual echo delay, leaking channel truth; that report is retained only as a diagnostic. Fixed-guard development diagnostics on the same 64 channels show that a split fixed 2-sample timing guard and 6-sample family-maximum CFO guard avoids per-channel truth but still fails the frozen acceptance limits: timing error is −2 in 5/512 blocks and CFO P95/max is 263.71/441.05 Hz against required zero timing error and <250/350 Hz. Corrected BLER is 48/512 versus clean 5/512; uncorrected BLER is 512/512. These seeds are not confirmatory and must not be retuned; a revised protocol requires fresh independent seeds. G5.09 remains open and RT production remains gated.
**Edition 3.331 G7.12 compressed episode-store fidelity test (2026-10-24):** Added byte-exact round-trip checks for complex128 path coefficients, complex64/complex128 physical CFR, float32/float64 target values, validity masks, reason codes and target definitions through the compressed UMCD episode store. The exercised NPZ ZIP compression is lossless with no quantizer. The focused two-test store slice, Ruff and format checks pass. This closes one serializer regression only; alternate storage formats and production-corpus quantization/storage fidelity remain unaudited, so G7.12 stays open.
**Edition 3.330 G3 external channel-validation leads (2026-10-24):** Cataloged a 3.3–3.8-GHz operational n78 outdoor-to-indoor PDP dataset and a 4.85-GHz double-directional 8×8 urban dataset as possible, separately scoped empirical channel checks. Neither provides target-material bistatic diffuse evidence for G3.03. Both have unresolved reuse metadata in their Zenodo records; the newer lab page also states non-commercial terms alongside CC BY 4.0. No data were downloaded or admitted. See the [source/licensing audit](G3_SCENE_FAMILY_LICENSE_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md).
**Edition 3.329 G5.09 independent-channel ordered-impairment receiver probe (2026-10-24):** Extended the combined TX/RX IQ-imbalance, phase-noise, CFO, timing and AWGN CPU PUSCH fixture to 64 independently seeded synthetic two-path channels with eight paired codewords per channel. The hash-bound 512-block result has clean BLER 5/512, corrected BLER 23/512 and uncorrected BLER 512/512; the channel-cluster bootstrap interval for corrected-minus-clean BLER is [0.0176, 0.0566]. Timing and sample-reference checks pass, but the frozen CFO maximum fails at 386.87 Hz against <350 Hz (P95 208.70 Hz). Preserve this as a conditional synthetic failure; do not retune criteria post hoc or count it as G5 closure. The implementation source was uncommitted at run time; regenerate after the planned implementation commit. Artifact: [64-channel report](results/g5-ordered-impairments-independent-channels-cpu-64x8-20261024.json).
**Edition 3.328 G3.03 target-frequency source disposition (2026-10-24):** A current source check found a 3.5-GHz Virginia Tech thesis with building-material penetration attenuation and a 26–36-GHz specular-reflection study whose data are part of a commercial project. Neither supplies open, target-frequency bistatic reflected-plus-diffuse angle data, uncertainty, and independent held-out units for the frozen G3.03 criteria. The material-calibration blocker remains; no data were downloaded and no RT was run. Details are in the [scene-family/material audit](G3_SCENE_FAMILY_LICENSE_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md).
**Edition 3.327 G3 qualification cost baseline (2026-10-24):** A hash-pinned estimate uses actual depth-3 LLVM CPU runtimes from 120 100k and 120 1M runs in each of three existing procedural scenes. The 354-cluster zero-failure floor projects to roughly 107 seconds of solver time at historical median pair cost for one link/cluster, or about 11 minutes for six links; the conservative historical-max envelope is about 13.5/81 minutes. This is not a target-scene forecast: all three scenes are one procedural family with zero scattering, and receiver compute plus full path/CFR storage are not represented. The documented result is a baseline only; no qualification run is authorized.
**Edition 3.326 G5.09 family-wise profile-matrix inference (2026-10-24):** Added a reproducible audit of the five saved 128-parent TDL-A–E curves. It verifies report/configuration/point hashes, a common source snapshot, and unique parent lineage, then applies 100,000 paired-parent bootstrap draws per claim with Bonferroni control across all 15 DMRS-LS profile/SNR contrasts. The corrected intervals retain evidence of positive time-domain versus ideal-CFR BLER contrast for TDL-A/B/C at 60 dB and B/C at 80 dB; all other intervals include zero. This sharpens conditional standardized-model evidence only; sparse LOS events and one Sionna setup do not qualify G5, G3, physical sites, or production RT.
**Edition 3.325 G5.09 five-profile CP-exceedance diagnostic (2026-10-24):** Re-ran the fixed 6-RB/30-kHz PUSCH diagnostic for TDL-A/B/C/D/E with 128 independent channel parents per profile, paired across 60/80/100-dB SNR, and regenerated A/C reports after detecting stale source hashes. Provenance, current source/config/point hashes, unique-parent counts, and paired cluster-bootstrap intervals were independently checked for all five reports. The TD-minus-ideal-CFR effects vary substantially by profile: strongest for NLOS C/A/B, sparse and unresolved for LOS D/E. Intervals are nominal per comparison (no family-wise correction across the 15 profile/SNR comparisons); this one standardized setup is exploratory evidence only. G5 remains open; it establishes neither physical-site generalization nor receiver acceptance.
**Edition 3.324 G3.03 frozen-criterion planning guardrails (2026-10-24):** The input schema and CLI require family-wise alpha 0.05 and bootstrap candidates at or above the evaluator 20-independent-unit floor, preventing a planning file from silently weakening the frozen 95% rule or proposing an unassessable candidate. The focused planner/CLI slice passes 39 tests; targeted mypy, Ruff and formatting pass. Planning output remains unverified and NOT_QUALIFIED.
**Edition 3.323 G3.03 reproducible planning CLI (2026-10-24):** scripts/plan_g3_acquisition_design.py now consumes a strict JSON contract for a unique material × polarization × incidence-stratum set, shared worst-stratum assumptions, and optional finite-support IID bootstrap scenarios. Its report binds the exact input and planner/runner source hashes; missing assumptions remain unresolved, analytic and bootstrap designs are deliberately not collapsed into one sample size, and the report always returns NOT_QUALIFIED. The synthetic example demonstrates sensitivity (12,131 units/stratum from conservative analytic assumptions versus a 20-unit bootstrap candidate under an intentionally simple two-atom scenario); these are illustrative only, not a protocol recommendation. The G3 planning/CLI tests pass (37), targeted mypy and Ruff pass. External justification and preregistration of real design assumptions, evaluator-aligned assurance, and measurement evidence remain open.
**Edition 3.322 G4.08 named-case Doppler covariance (2026-10-24):** The existing paired-independent-draw temporal-correlation test now includes all 17 fixed Annex B.2.2 cases. At lag 1/(4 f_D,max), 8,192 independent channel draws per case agree with the independent Jakes/Rician correlation reference; a 3.8-standard-error Bonferroni rule covers the 34 real/imaginary named-case claims at at least 99% family confidence. All 33 existing plus named-profile checks pass. This verifies how the standardized Sionna TDL implementation realizes the registered maximum Doppler; it does not establish measured physical validity, receiver performance, or RT qualification.
**Edition 3.321 G4.08 native Annex B.2.2 matrix (2026-10-24):** Expanded native Sionna case coverage from two representatives to all 17 TS 38.101-4 V19.2.2 Annex B.2.2 FR1/FR2 profile/Doppler combinations, sourced directly from the immutable case registry. Each case passes channel generation and standardized-record serialization/reopening/admission on CPU (17 passed). This closes the native adapter-to-record execution gap only; it does not validate RF accuracy, broader bandwidth/numerology, physical propagation, or receiver requirements.

**Edition 3.320 bounded G5.09 ordered TX/RX IQ and oscillator fixture (2026-10-24):** Added 0.5-dB TX/RX IQ gain imbalance with +0.02/-0.02-rad phase imbalance to the existing timing/CFO/phase-noise PUSCH chain and independently spelled reference. On one static two-tap channel, the pre-existing limits pass: 0/128 clean, 2/128 sync-corrected impaired CRC failures, and 128/128 without synchronization correction; timing error is zero and CFO P95/max error is 185.89/226.80 Hz. The exact-source-hash-bound result passes the provenance-field auditor, while `source_matches_git_revision=false` records that code is still uncommitted. The post-hoc 95% one-sided exact upper bound is 4.84%, conditional on this fixture and not a frozen acceptance criterion. No IQ compensation or phase-noise tracking is modeled; this does not establish deployment performance or G5.09 closure.

**Edition 3.319 prospective G7.08 split-lock signature verification (2026-10-24):** Added OpenSSH detached-signature verification for a frozen split-policy registry using the dedicated `umcd-split-policy-v2@github.com` namespace and externally governed `allowed_signers` trust file. Verification snapshots the lock, detached signature and keyring and checks the exact bytes before parsing; focused tests use Ed25519 keys. The 53-test split-policy suite passes, including modified-lock, wrong-principal and hard-link rejection. This supplies a prospective signing mechanism only; no existing results are retroactively preregistered, and G7.08 remains open until a final corpus is frozen, audited and signed before tuning.

**Edition 3.318 G5.09 finite-symbol Doppler reference and coefficient precision (2026-10-24):** Fixed sample-domain path-coefficient conversion to preserve complex128 values supplied for a complex128 waveform. A new independent finite-sum reference checks all 64 output FFT bins for a delayed path at 0, 0.025, 0.1, and 0.2 Doppler/SCS with a 16-sample CP. The two focused impairment suites pass 31 tests; Ruff and formatting pass. This is analytic channel-model validation, not receiver mobility qualification or deployment evidence; G5.09 remains open.

**Edition 3.317 G4.08 named fixed-profile Doppler admission (2026-10-24):** Added an optional explicit TS 38.101-4 V19.2.2 Annex B.2.2 case ID across TDL generation and standardized-record validation. **Edition 3.316 criterion-oriented G3 receiver-impact scorer (2026-10-24):** Added a paired PUSCH receiver-outcome diagnostic with exact expected family × LOS/NLOS strata, at least 20 channel clusters per stratum as a pilot floor, complete candidate/challenger transport-block/SNR pairing, cluster-then-block bootstrap intervals, fixed-common-SNR BLER equivalence and precision checks, and monotone bracketed SNR@10% BLER estimates without extrapolation. Decoder outcomes are counted per configuration and bound by a canonical input-record SHA-256. Eight focused tests pass; the combined G3 scorer/planner/material/acquisition-contract slice passes 132 tests; Ruff, formatting, and targeted mypy pass. Numeric success is not G5 receiver qualification or G3 physical evidence.

**Edition 3.315 reusable G3 path-equivalence matcher (2026-10-24):** Added deterministic/specular effective-class construction using ordered interaction/object signatures, caller-frozen geometry/delay/direction tolerances, and a one-to-one assignment. The scorer retains valid raw path multiplicity, treats primitive IDs as descriptive, computes class coherent contributions and the frozen CSI-omission / 6-dB-over-noise relevance rules, and reports bidirectional relevant-class coverage plus all unmatched relevant classes. Repeated zero-interaction records are grouped as one effective class without assigning individual identities. Diffuse stochastic records are rejected from identity matching and need separate distributional evaluation. Twelve focused tests pass; the combined G3 scorer/planner/material/acquisition-contract slice passes 124 tests; Ruff, format, and targeted mypy pass. A single solver comparison is diagnostic only; no G3 path/RT qualification evidence was generated.

**Edition 3.314 criterion-oriented G3 delay/angular scorer (2026-10-24):** Added a paired metric scorer for resolvable first-arrival delay, RMS delay spread, and 3D arrival/departure angular spread using the frozen bandwidth-relative and physical-unit tolerances. It uses equal-cluster summaries and marginal per-metric/stratum bootstrap/exact-binomial bounds without multiplicity correction; expected family × LOS/NLOS × total/specular/diffuse strata must match exactly. Missing/unresolved values need reason codes, and not-applicable components are not substituted with zero. All 9 focused tests pass; the combined G3 scorer/planner/material/acquisition-contract slice passes 112 tests; Ruff, format, and targeted mypy pass. The scorer remains numerical-only: no RT/measurement truth, external cluster audit, path-level matching, or receiver-impact qualification was produced.

**Edition 3.313 criterion-oriented G3 complex-CSI scorer (2026-10-24):** Added a standalone numerical scorer for the frozen symmetric unnormalized complex-CFR metric. It requires a preregistered expected family × LOS/NLOS set that exactly matches observed strata, plus common grid/configuration IDs, CFR shape, and `H_floor` across the comparison. It reports equal-cluster-weighted median/p95 errors, a chunked cluster-bootstrap one-sided upper bound for p95, and a one-sided exact-binomial bound on clusters with any episode above the frozen 0.05 error threshold. It does not align phase or normalize channels, and it always reports `NUMERICAL_CRITERIA_ONLY_NOT_QUALIFIED`; external axis/noise-floor/provenance/independence/representativeness checks remain necessary. All 16 focused tests pass; the combined G3 scorer/planner/material/acquisition-contract slice passes 103 tests; Ruff, formatting, and targeted mypy pass. No qualification data or production RT run was created.

**Edition 3.281 Core-1 G4 evidence reconciliation (2026-10-24):** Re-ran the named native CPU Core-1 qualification slice: all 110 checks pass, comprising 50 TDL/CDL profile-topology covariance cells, 50 profile-topology path-axis reconstruction cases, and 10 joint CDL-A–E TX/RX covariance cases for 2×2 and 4×4 arrays. **Edition 3.280 bounded G5.09 higher-Doppler stress extension (2026-10-24):** Extended the independent-channel CPU PUSCH experiment to 750- and 1,500-Hz echo Doppler, with static, symbol-static, and continuous-time waveforms paired on 64 channel draws, payload sets, and noise realizations. A five-claim Bonferroni family controls the baseline bound and four effect intervals at 99% family-wise confidence. Both static-to-mobile effects exceed the frozen 0.10 BLER-increase floor and their lower interval bounds are positive. At 750 Hz, the isolated continuous-minus-symbol-static increase is 0.0137 [−0.0098, 0.0449]; at 1,500 Hz it is −0.0020 [−0.0098, 0]. Neither meets the 0.10 intra-symbol effect criterion. The findings do not establish an ICI-driven receiver impact; G5.09 remains open. The experiment and report-verification tests pass. The reproducible report is `docs/research/results/g5-independent-channel-pusch-doppler-stress-20261024.json`; production RT remains gated.

**Edition 3.279 bounded G5.09 independent-channel ICI decomposition (2026-10-24):** The 64-channel, 8-block-per-channel CPU PUSCH study now includes matched static, 750-Hz symbol-static, and 750-Hz continuous-Doppler conditions, sharing channel draws, payloads, and waveform noise. One Bonferroni family covers the baseline upper bound and both paired effects at 99% family-wise confidence. Static reception is 0/512 with a multiplicity-adjusted one-sided channel-prevalence upper bound of 0.0853. Continuous Doppler is 202/512 (0.3945) and passes the frozen mobility-sensitivity criteria. Symbol-static reception is 195/512 (0.3809); the isolated continuous-minus-symbol-static increase is 0.0137 with a 99% cluster-bootstrap interval [−0.0078, 0.0430]. This fails the preregistered 0.10 minimum intra-symbol increase and includes zero, so it supplies no qualifying ICI-impact evidence and does not close G5.09. The bound study and report-verification tests pass. Reproducible evidence is `docs/research/results/g5-independent-channel-pusch-ici-20261024.json`; production RT remains gated.

**Edition 3.278 bounded G4 CDL-A–E joint TX/RX covariance at 2×2 and 4×4 (2026-10-24):** The hash-pinned independent TR 38.901 v19.2 ray-law reference now covers joint transmit/receive covariance for square 2- and 4-element ULAs at both link ends. Across five profiles and both array sizes, one Bonferroni family spans 1,360 unique real Hermitian components. With 16,384 independent native CPU draws per case, all ten profile/array cases meet the simultaneous 99% confidence inclusion and maximum 0.05 normalized-power interval half-width criteria. CDL-D/E retain the specular LOS outer product separately from diffuse covariance. This is standardized-model consistency evidence, not geometric RT truth, receiver-impact evidence, or broad physical validation. Focused native cases pass; remaining G4 configurations and production RT remain open.

**Edition 3.277 joint-family G3 sample-size planning (2026-10-24):** The independent-acquisition planner now applies one Bonferroni family to all five distributional G3.03 claims and the complex-CSI cluster-failure criterion across every stratum, allocating both alpha and power consistently. Its v3 output combines the exact-binomial CSI requirement with the data-free Hoeffding lower bounds and checks the angular-maximum upper bound against the six-claim requirement. In the one-stratum illustrative assumptions exercised by the planner tests, the CSI component requires 293 independent outer clusters and the six-claim Hoeffding lower bound is 10,583. These are not a complete sample-size guarantee; the explicit angular pass-probability assumption, evaluator-aligned bootstrap assurance, external acquisition-unit/provenance verification, and actual target-frequency measurements remain open. The planner remains data-free and non-admitting. All 33 planner tests and the G3 evaluator/package suites pass. The complete suite passes 1,616 tests with 3 skipped and 84.69% coverage; CI-scoped Ruff/format and mypy (203 source files) pass.

**Edition 3.275 bounded G5.14 blind PSS/SSS-to-PBCH integration (2026-10-24):** Connected configured blind PSS/SSS acquisition to PBCH OFDM demodulation and a full Lmax=4, two-half-frame BCH-CRC hypothesis search. One seeded capture with 23-sample leading timing, 2.5-kHz CFO, a 5-sample two-tap echo, and 24-dB transmit-referenced AWGN recovers PCI, timing, CFO within 100 Hz, the exact SSB index/half-frame, CRC, SFN, and typed MIB. The combined PBCH/NR-sync suite passes 143 tests; full-tree Ruff, formatting (365 files), mypy (202 sources), uv lock --check, and uv build pass. This establishes one named controlled receiver chain only. Broader acquisition, channels/numerologies, waveform/power conformance, mobility, multi-antenna performance, and physical validation remain open. No full repository rerun was made after this addition; G5 and production RT remain open.

**Edition 3.276 independent-channel G5.09 receiver sensitivity (2026-10-24):** Added `experiments/g5_independent_channel_receiver.py` and the hash-bound result `docs/research/results/g5-independent-channel-pusch-20261024.json`. The preregistered CPU PUSCH probe pairs static and 750-Hz echo-Doppler reception across 64 independent seeded channel draws, with the same eight payloads and waveform-noise realization within each draw. The synthetic channel law fixes a 0.85 direct coefficient, echo delays uniformly selected from 2–8 samples, echo amplitudes uniformly selected from 0.20–0.50, uniform echo phase, and proper-complex waveform AWGN variance 0.01. Channel draws, not nested codewords, are equally weighted inference units. The paired effect uses a two-sided 99.5% percentile cluster-bootstrap interval. Since all baseline channel clusters have zero failures, their percentile bootstrap is degenerate; the conservative baseline bound instead uses the one-sided 99.5% Clopper-Pearson upper bound on channel-level any-failure prevalence, 0.0795, which also bounds mean BLER. The static reference is 0/512 CRC failures; the Doppler condition is 202/512 (BLER 0.395); the paired mean increase is 0.395 with a 99.5% interval [0.242, 0.553]. Both frozen reference/effect criteria pass for this declared synthetic distribution. This is conditional receiver sensitivity evidence; it neither isolates intra-symbol ICI nor qualifies measured/deployment channels or closes G5.09. Four report/statistics tests pass; full repository validation passes with 1,616 tests, 3 skips, 84.69% coverage, CI-scoped Ruff/format, mypy (203 source files), `uv lock --check`, and `uv build`. Reproduce with `OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 MKL_NUM_THREADS=1 uv run --no-sync --locked python experiments/g5_independent_channel_receiver.py`.

**Edition 3.274 bounded G5.14 known-occasion PBCH OFDM receiver (2026-10-24):** Added known-timing OFDM demodulation, PBCH-DM-RS per-symbol channel estimation/interpolation, equalization, noise-scaled LLR generation, physical descrambling, BCH decoding, and typed MIB recovery for one receive antenna. A flat near-noiseless case and 16 seeded 24-dB AWGN trials with a two-tap channel (1–5 sample excess delays within the 18-sample CP; transmit-referenced SNR) all recover CRC and MIB. The 121-test focused PBCH suite, Ruff, format, and mypy pass. Timing/candidate are supplied to the receiver; this does not qualify synchronization search, standardized SSB waveform/power, mobility, multi-antenna operation, or general receiver performance. The repository suite passes 1,611 tests with 3 skipped and 85.06% coverage; full-tree Ruff/format/mypy, lock validation and package build pass. Hosted CI has not run; G5 and production RT remain open.

**Edition 3.273 bounded G5.14 fixed-schema BCCH-BCH MIB parsing (2026-10-24):** Added a typed serializer/parser for the MIB alternative in the fixed 24-bit BCCH-BCH message and connected it to the ideal PBCH round trip. Integration uncovered and corrected the BCCH-BCH boundary: the one-bit MIB CHOICE precedes the six SFN MSBs, so they occupy bits 1–6; the generator validation and SFN recovery now use those positions. The focused PBCH suite passes 119 tests; targeted Ruff, format, and mypy pass. This does not support general ASN.1/PER extensions, messageClassExtension, noisy physical reception, channel estimation, power scaling, OFDM, or broad receiver qualification. No full repository suite was run for this slice; scientific gates and production RT remain open.

**Edition 3.272 bounded G4 CDL-D/E delay-angle moment (2026-10-24):** The CDL-D/E delay/cos(AoA) cluster-moment companion test passes for both hash-pinned v19.2 LOS profiles using 8,192 independent native CPU draws per profile. The reference defines the merged zero-delay tap's angular feature from the power-weighted LOS-specular and first-diffuse components and averages the diffuse cluster's 20 ray offsets; this is an ensemble-power model-consistency reference, not geometric path truth. Error and interval-width limits are each 0.05, with one Bonferroni-adjusted 99% family-wise rule across CDL-A-E. The focused A-E angular suite passes five cases; Ruff and format checks pass. No full repository suite was rerun for this bounded addition. Full angular validity, complex CSI, geometric RT, receiver impact, and broad G4 qualification remain open; production RT stays gated.

**Plan-finalization boundary (edition 3.265, 2026-10-24):** The staged execution specification is complete for the currently chosen project scope. This is a planning milestone only; research execution, qualification, and dataset release remain incomplete.
**Current execution snapshot (edition 3.290, 2026-10-24):** G3 now has a proposal-only v0.2 [acquisition package schema](../../datasets/schema/g3.03-acquisition-package.v0.2.schema.json) and a bounded semantic verifier that checks exact inventory bytes, rights coverage, scorer/split/evaluator bindings, row-to-acquisition joins, angular/CSI dimensions, and held-out assignment coverage. It still cannot establish authenticity, independence, calibration truth, legal sufficiency, representative acquisition, or a powered multi-estimand design; it cannot qualify a measurement. RT production generation is hard-disabled until a trusted signature keyring is implemented. Explicit exploratory pilot generation requires a gate-issued request binding and confines file outputs to `/tmp`; its bound is an engineering safeguard, not qualification or an OS sandbox. The mandatory named Core-1 matrix now passes its native CPU gate: 50 TDL/CDL profile-topology covariance cells, 50 path-axis reconstruction cases, and 10 joint CDL-A–E TX/RX covariance cases for 2×2 and 4×4 arrays. The covariance matrix covers its 1,535-component family, and joint-array cases cover a 1,360-component family, both under their frozen nominal 99% simultaneous interval and precision criteria. This is model-consistency evidence, not arbitrary-array, geometric-RT, polarization/time-complete, or external physical qualification. A hash-pinned delay/cos(AoA) cluster moment passes for CDL-A–E under one 99% family-wise interval rule across all five profiles; D/E use power-weighted specular-plus-diffuse angular composition in their merged zero-delay tap. This remains one standardized-model statistic, not full angular validity. Broad physical qualification remains open. G5.14 now assembles, interleaves, and payload-scrambles 32-bit PBCH inputs from serialized 24-bit MIB payloads and timing/index fields for Lmax 4/8/10/20/64, then provides BCH coding/decoding, 864-bit physical-channel scrambling, QPSK, and 4×240 SS/PBCH-grid mapping. Payload generation, recovery, fixed-schema BCCH-BCH MIB parsing, a bounded blind PSS/SSS-to-PBCH chain, configured one-port SSS EPRE-to-baseband mapping, Release 19 Case A-G candidate-symbol timing, and TS 38.211-derived numerology/normal-CP/sample-rate configuration are tested; general ASN.1/PER extensions and messageClassExtension decoding, acquisition qualification across broader numerologies and channels, broad channel-estimation/equalization qualification, full-frame burst/sample-clock integration, calibrated multi-port RF power scaling, mobility/multi-antenna validation, timing/beam qualification, PRS/SRS, and end-to-end conformance remain open. G5 also has a narrow duration-8 mapping-B PUSCH DM-RS check, and G6 has exact-timestamp trajectory target alignment; each is component evidence only and leaves the corresponding broad gate open. G7 adds a corpus-integrity audit command, but no frozen production corpus, external legal review, measured RSS/corpus-scale bound, or release attestation exists. The bounded City43 Phase-0 analysis attributes the selected CFR instability to returned direct-like record multiplicity and coherent accumulation, without determining the internal Sionna creation stage or establishing a solver defect. No finite ray/depth budget is ground truth. Scientific gates and production RT remain open.
**Edition 3.271 bounded G5.14 PBCH payload recovery (2026-10-24):** Added receiver-side recovery of the payload-scrambling offset from unmasked SFN timing bits, followed by payload descrambling, inverse interleaving, and extraction of serialized MIB bits, SFN, half-frame, and transmitted candidate-index prefix fields for Lmax 4/8/10/20/64. A native ideal-data chain passes from payload generation through BCH coding, physical-channel scrambling, QPSK hard decisions, BCH CRC decoding, and payload recovery. The encoder now copies immutable NumPy payloads before PyTorch conversion, avoiding its non-writable-array warning. The focused G5 suite passes 144 tests; targeted Ruff/format and mypy pass for the changed PBCH modules. No full-suite rerun has occurred since the preceding 1,577-pass/3-skip, 84.94%-coverage checkpoint. ASN.1 MIB parsing and channel-affected receiver qualification remain open; no research or production gate changes.
**Edition 3.270 bounded G5.14 PBCH payload processing (2026-10-24):** Added TS 38.212 V19.1.0 32-bit PBCH payload assembly from a serialized 24-bit MIB payload, timing and candidate-index fields, the category-dependent payload interleaver, and self-inverse payload scrambling/descrambling for Lmax 4/8/10/20/64. The six SFN MSBs in BCCH-BCH bits 1–6 are checked against the supplied SFN after its leading MIB CHOICE bit.
