**Edition 3.129 City43 building-scene path-cap follow-up (partial convergence evidence):** Extended the matched 1k/10k/1M cap comparison to the 16-building synthetic City43 scene, using the independently audited center TX and one clear/one blocked RX endpoint, depth 3, 20k ray samples/source, seeds 11/23/47, and synthetic uncalibrated S=0.5 overrides for concrete, glass, and metal. At cap 1k, the LOS link returns 981 paths (978 diffuse) for every seed and median RMS delay spread 22.26 ns. At caps 10k and 1M, it returns 1,852–1,885 paths (1,843–1,876 diffuse), with median RMS delay spread 34.05 ns and effectively matching path-power and angular-spread summaries. The blocked link returns 335–349 paths (323–338 diffuse) at every cap; its aggregate summaries also match across caps for this fixture. Thus a 1k cap materially changes the LOS result here, whereas 10k matches the finite 1M comparison for these reported observables. This does not establish a universally safe cap or cross-cap complex-CFR equivalence: it is one synthetic scene, material override, depth, and sample budget; the 1M result is not ground truth. Gate G3.05 remains open pending more scene/material/depth/budget cases and direct channel-vector error checks. Reports: `docs/research/results/rt-city43-los-nlos-depth3-s05-cap{1000,10000,1000000}-20261022.json`; matched summary: `docs/research/figures/rt-city43-los-nlos-path-cap-sweep-s05-20261022.json`. The new [3D City43 buildings and link figure](figures/rt-city43-los-nlos-cap-sweep-scene-20261022.png) renders all 16 scene OBJ meshes; `scripts/plot_rt_city43_cap_sweep_scene.py` reproduces it from the summary, raw link report, and scene assets.

**Edition 3.130 cross-cap complex-channel error and CFR-vector provenance:** The native LLVM runner now offers opt-in schema v4 reports that retain aligned float64 real/imaginary CFR samples on the exact absolute-delay, unnormalized 256-subcarrier grid; the cap auditor validates matched configuration/grid shapes and computes seed-paired relative complex-L2 error against the largest evaluated cap. Re-running all 1k/10k/1M cases on the same City43 links, scene hash, depth 3, 20k samples, three seeds, and S=0.5 overrides gives LOS median relative CFR error 0.1313 at cap 1k (seed range 0.0857–0.1480), but only 4.55e-7 at cap 10k against cap 1M. The blocked-link error remains below 2.62e-7 even at cap 1k. This directly demonstrates that the 1k LOS cap changes the complex channel in this fixture and that 10k matches the finite 1M CFR reference to solver numerical precision here; it still does not establish a generally sufficient cap or make the finite reference ground truth. G3.05 remains open pending scene-family/material/depth/budget coverage and predeclared tolerances. The reproducible [CFR-aware figure](figures/rt-city43-los-nlos-path-cap-sweep-s05-cfr-20261022.png), compact per-seed error summary, and three CFR-bearing run reports use the `*-cfr-20261022` suffix. The runner flag is `--include-cfr-vectors`; legacy v3 reports remain readable and explicitly report that cross-cap CFR error is unavailable without stored vectors.

**Edition 3.131 second-geometry CFR cap stress and numerical replay audit:** Applied the same seed-paired CFR method to the controlled `box_one_screen` LOS/screen-blocked pair at depth 6, 100k samples/source, seeds 11/23/47, and uncalibrated screen S=0.5. Against cap 1M, median relative CFR L2 error for LOS is 0.417 at cap 1k (range 0.382–0.637) and 0.300 at cap 10k (0.282–0.371), despite the 10k case returning only 8,210–8,245 paths versus 10,748–10,850 at 1M. NLOS error is 0.565 at cap 1k (0.546–0.651) but below 7.15e-7 at cap 10k, where path counts and aggregate observables match the 1M comparison. In contrast, the City43 10k CFR errors were below 6.1e-7 for both links. This confirms cap sufficiency is link/geometry dependent; G3.05 stays open and no universal 10k recommendation is justified. The [box-scene CFR cap figure](figures/rt-box-los-nlos-path-cap-sweep-depth6-s05-cfr-20261022.png) and three matched reports are the `rt-box-los-nlos-convergence-depth6-s05-cap*-cfr-20261022.json` files.

Repeated the identical cap-1k box sweep as a numerical determinism check: all 42 matched link/seed/depth cases preserve path counts; 13 CFR hashes are bitwise identical, and all 42 complex-CFR relative L2 errors are at most 4.26e-7 (median 9.76e-8). Thus this exact LLVM environment is numerically replayable within the audit's 1e-6 tolerance, but not bitwise deterministic for every sample. This is a single same-backend/single-configuration replay, so G3.08 remains open pending repeated configurations/backends and a preregistered tolerance. The reusable `scripts/audit_rt_solver_replay.py` and [per-case replay report](results/rt-box-los-nlos-convergence-depth6-s05-cap1000-replay-20261022.json) keep the numerical tolerance distinct from hash equality; both source runs are preserved.


**Edition 3.132 matched diffuse-scattering intervention:** Repeated the paired screen-link experiment at S=0 and synthetic S=0.5 with 3 seeds, depths 0–3, and 1k/20k rays/source (30 matched cases per report, complex CFR retained). The S=0 control has zero diffuse paths throughout; at S=0.5, diffuse paths occur in 21/30 cases and never at depth zero. At depth 3/20k rays, median diffuse-path counts are 1,151 (range 1,139–1,161) for LOS and 44 (41–47) for blocked NLOS. Median relative CFR L2 differences from the zero-scattering control are 0.065 and 0.044, respectively. This confirms that the pinned solver responds to the explicit synthetic material intervention in this one controlled scene; it does not establish calibrated scattering, scene-general validity, or ray-budget convergence. The [budget figure](figures/rt-box-scattering-control-s05-v4-20261022.png), matched CFR reports, and machine-readable comparison are recorded under `rt-box-scattering-control-*-v4-20261022`.


**Edition 3.133 City43 building-scene scattering intervention:** Applied the paired S=0 versus concrete-only S=0.5 override to the audited 16-building City43 scene, with two center links, 3 seeds, depth 0-3, and 1k/20k ray budgets. The audit finds zero diffuse paths in 30/30 baseline cases and diffuse paths in 24/30 intervention cases; no depth-zero case produces diffuse paths. At depth 3/20k rays, median diffuse counts are 1,256 LOS and 256 blocked NLOS. Relative CFR L2 medians against the zero-scattering baseline are 0.072 LOS and 0.482 NLOS. The [City43 building/link view](figures/rt-city43-los-nlos-cap-sweep-scene-20261022.png) and [scattering response plot](figures/rt-city43-scattering-control-concrete-s05-v4-20261022.png) make the actual mesh and material intervention visible. This is still a synthetic solver control, not concrete calibration or measured propagation.


**Edition 3.134 City43 building-scene solver replay:** Repeated the same native LLVM City43 concrete-scattering configuration, including both audited links, three seeds, depths 0-3, and 20k samples/source. All 24 path counts match; only 6 CFR hashes are bitwise identical, while median and maximum complex-CFR relative L2 errors are 2.02e-7 and 3.35e-7. This extends tolerance-based replay evidence to the actual building mesh, not to a second backend or all configurations. G3.08 remains open.

**Edition 3.135 common timing-origin shift:** A native Sionna 2.2 LLVM regression shifts the 10 m free-space path delay by 0.37 ns and verifies the absolute-delay NR-grid CFR rotates by `exp(-j 2π (f_c + f_k) Δτ)` while preserving the shifted CIR delay. The same-path CFR with `normalize_delays=True` is invariant to that common shift, making the removed timing reference explicit. The full focused RT-to-NR native module passes 17 tests. This validates the conversion convention only; it does not establish receiver observability of ToA with unknown timing/carrier references or resolve multipath reference selection.

**Edition 3.136 City43 spatial-holdout geometry-label audit:** Recomputed the geometric direct-segment labels for all 120 links in the 3-TX/3-disjoint-RX-region City43 pilot. The independent Mitsuba segment oracle agrees with all 60 LOS and 60 NLOS labels; there are no mismatches or ambiguous cases. The per-link audit is `results/rt-urban43-spatial-holdout-label-audit-20261022.json` and binds the data and City43 scene-tree hashes. The result verifies label consistency for this synthetic scene, not material calibration, RT channel correctness, scene-general model performance, or field truth. The experiment remains a within-one-scene descriptive location-shift pilot with three regions.

**Edition 3.137 explicit-array orientation and spatial-phase check:** A native Sionna 2.2 CPU/LLVM LOS fixture checks a two-element half-wavelength TX array at 0° and 90° yaw with explicit element tracing. Both path delays match independently rotated Euclidean TX-element/RX distances within 2 ps; path amplitudes match Friis, and the baseband CFR matches analytic carrier phase to `1e-4` relative tolerance. The RT-to-NR and array-layout native modules pass 25 tests together. This verifies one isotropic free-space explicit-array case; synthetic-array equivalence, directional patterns, cross-polarization, spatial-correlation statistics and broader orientation gold values remain open.

**Edition 3.138 synthetic-array approximation stress:** Compared Sionna 2.2 synthetic-array CFRs with explicit element tracing for an 8-element half-wavelength TX array, one isotropic RX, and direct-path free space at 3.5 GHz. Relative complex-CFR L2 error is 0.00475 at 100 m and 0.2618 at 1 m. The contrast confirms that the synthetic-array approximation is range/aperture dependent; near-field data need explicit qualification and cannot inherit a far-field error assumption. This two-distance test does not define a universal cutoff or cover multipath, directional patterns, or cross-polarization. The 26 focused RT-to-NR and array-layout native tests pass on CPU/LLVM.

**Edition 3.139 explicit 2×2 MIMO spatial-channel check:** A pinned Sionna 2.2 CPU/LLVM free-space solve checks all four channel coefficients between two-element half-wavelength TX/RX arrays. Path delays match the independently computed pairwise Euclidean distances within 2 ps; the complex CFR matrix matches the analytic Friis/carrier-phase matrix within `1e-4` relative L2 error, and the normalized receive spatial-correlation matrix agrees with the same analytic reference within `1e-4`. This verifies one isotropic, direct-path array configuration; multipath correlation and polarization-dependent spatial statistics remain open. The focused RT-to-NR and array-layout native modules pass 27 tests together.

**Edition 3.140 provenance-complete RT episode publishing:** `build_sionna_rt_episode_bundle` and `write_sionna_rt_episode_bundle` now connect explicit Sionna link/frame bindings to the canonical immutable UMCD bundle writer. A separate provenance record is generated for every populated paths, channel, target, and receiver-observation layer, bound to the same verified cache identity and caller-supplied materials. A test publishes and reloads a multi-link bundle and verifies the run hash and material configuration. Full repository suite: 592 passed, 3 skipped, 87.41% coverage; maintained Ruff checks/format pass across 241 files and mypy passes across 145 source files. Existing generators have not yet been migrated to call the publisher, and production material/asset closure remains unresolved.

**Edition 3.141 canonical RT trajectory generation:** `generate_rt_nr_trajectory(..., umcd_store_root=...)` now invokes the publisher for each retraced route. One immutable episode contains complete link/frame path truth, unnormalized absolute-delay CFR, geometric LOS labels, receiver-only NR observations, and position/orientation timelines. Sionna runtime material values, frequency callbacks, scattering-pattern state, and object-to-material bindings are serialized into both the physical run identity and layer provenance; unsupported non-serializable material state fails closed. A native LLVM `simple_reflector` test reads the saved episode and verifies all four populated layer provenance records. Full suite: 592 passed, 3 skipped, 87.35% coverage; maintained Ruff check/format, mypy (145 source files), lock verification, and `uv build` pass. This is a retraced moving-UE/static-obstacle fixture, not validated Doppler or moving-blockage data; static scene generators and material calibration remain open.

**Edition 3.147 static RT UMCD publisher and building-visible spatial QA:** `generate_raytraced(..., umcd_store_root=...)` now publishes each accepted static sample as an immutable bundle with Sionna paths, absolute CFR, geometric visibility target, noisy-CFR receiver input, and per-layer RT provenance. `FrequencyDomainModelInputs` records noise variance, SNR, carrier, and subcarrier offsets; its semantics explicitly distinguish direct noisy CFR from PUSCH/DMRS. A CPU/LLVM native box-scene test writes and reads four bundles and checks inference-only input access. Full local suite: 596 passed, 3 skipped, 87.39% coverage; mypy passes on 160 source files and `uv lock --check`/`uv build` pass. The [City43 spatial-holdout figure](figures/rt-urban43-spatial-holdout-20261022.png) visibly renders 16 synthetic buildings, three TX positions, and 120 sampled RX links. These additions do not establish scene realism, measured-channel validity, trained-model performance, or independent-site generalization.

**Edition 3.148 trajectory Doppler correction and verification:** The dynamic RT producer now assigns `RTTrajectoryConfig.receiver_velocity_mps` to its Sionna receiver after extracting the static UMCD scene definition and before PathSolver execution. Its previous implementation moved the receiver position between snapshots but left the solver's device velocity at zero, so path-level Doppler was not representing the configured route. A native LLVM test now verifies direct-path Doppler sign/magnitude against the geometric radial-velocity equation and checks phase-rate consistency using each snapshot's absolute delay. The full suite passes with 596 passed, 3 skipped, and 87.40% coverage; maintained Ruff, format, mypy, `uv lock --check`, and `uv build` pass. This does not yet verify Doppler on reflected paths against an independent moving-interaction solution or model intra-slot evolution.

**Edition 3.149 Doppler-evolved NR waveform observations:** `generate_rt_nr_trajectory` now evaluates each RT path solution over the PUSCH OFDM-symbol samples using Sionna's path-wise Doppler, then multiplies the transmitted NR grid by this time-varying channel before adding AWGN and estimating CSI. It preserves the first-symbol channel target and adds `channel_cfr_ofdm_symbols` as a separate truth target with explicit masks and provenance. Native LLVM confirms the channel changes over symbols for a moving UE and the focused trajectory round trip passes. This uses `1/Δf` symbol spacing (CP omitted), fixed ray geometry within a slot, and no within-slot path births/deaths; those approximations and limitations are recorded in sample provenance.

**Edition 3.150 moving-object RT retracing and independent LOS oracle:** The NR trajectory producer supports named constant-velocity scene-object translations, restores each pose from the captured initial center at every timestamp, sets Sionna RT object velocity, and retraces the scene to expose path and visibility changes. Per-frame object positions and velocities are recorded and included in run identity. Geometric LOS labels now come from an independent finite-segment Mitsuba query and are checked against the depth-zero PathSolver result; ambiguous labels and oracle disagreements stop publication. A native LLVM box-scene test moves a screen out of the TX–RX line and verifies the NLOS-to-LOS transition and pose provenance. Rotation, deformation, and CP-accurate within-slot geometry remain open.

**Edition 3.151 CP-aware OFDM-symbol timing:** Doppler-evolved PUSCH channel samples now use the pinned Sionna 2.2 `ResourceGrid.ofdm_symbol_duration`, which includes its configured scalar cyclic prefix, rather than `1/Δf`. Per-record provenance stores CP samples, symbol interval, and sample rate. Native LLVM trajectory tests verify these values against the Sionna resource-grid equation. Sionna 2.2 deliberately repeats one scalar CP duration across a slot, so exact TS 38.211 per-symbol CP variation and geometry evolution within a slot remain open.

**Edition 3.152 reflected-path Doppler cross-check:** A three-snapshot, native LLVM `box_one_screen` trajectory now matches each persistent first-order reflection by scene object and checks Sionna's path Doppler against `-f_c dτ/dt` from a centered finite difference of absolute geometric delay. This extends the LOS Doppler check to two reflected interactions in one synthetic scene. It does not yet validate object-motion Doppler against independent measurements, path births/deaths during a slot, or varied materials and geometries.

**Edition 3.153 exact NR symbol-start timing:** RT trajectory CFR is evaluated at each OFDM symbol's explicit start time using per-path Doppler and the normal-CP schedule from 3GPP TS 38.211 clause 5.3.1, including slot-dependent long-CP placement. Unit tests cover 15 and 60 kHz schedules; native LLVM parity tests confirm that the explicit-time adapter reproduces `Paths.cfr` for uniform sample times with synthetic and explicit arrays. Provenance records the exact symbol-time vector and slot index. The grid-level PUSCH PHY retains a scalar CP abstraction, and scene geometry and path topology remain frozen within each slot. Full suite: 613 passed, 3 skipped, 87.30% coverage; maintained CI Ruff, format, mypy (161 source files), lock, and package-build checks pass. The [City43 spatial-holdout figure](figures/rt-urban43-spatial-holdout-20261022.png) visibly shows extruded synthetic buildings in 3D and their footprints from above.

**Edition 3.154 joint aligned-grid network PHY baseline:** Added a power-aware multi-TX/multi-RX resource-grid mixer that applies the full `[batch,rx,rx-port,tx,tx-port,symbol,subcarrier]` channel to already port-precoded transmitter grids, preserves every source contribution, and sums sources at each receiver before adding independently seeded receiver AWGN. A measured SINR helper separates the serving source and coherently sums non-serving waveforms before taking interference power; it marks the zero-signal/zero-disturbance ratio invalid. Eight analytic tests pass: joint signal equals source-wise filtering and addition (M08); disabling the interferer reproduces the desired-only grid (M09); increased interferer power increases controlled-case interference and lowers SINR (M10); receiver noise replays deterministically but differs across RX nodes. Full multi-node native RT link extraction already has separate local LLVM coverage, but it is not yet wired through this composer in an end-to-end PUSCH/DMRS episode. This interface assumes aligned same-numerology grids and does not model CFO, timing offsets, adjacent-channel leakage, PA/RF effects, or pilot contamination. Full suite: 621 passed, 3 skipped, 87.19% coverage; maintained Ruff, format, mypy (162 source files), and lock checks pass.

**Edition 3.155 native pilot-contamination check:** A native Sionna PUSCH/DMRS test feeds two transmitters the same generated pilot-bearing grid through distinct serving and cochannel channels, then estimates CSI at the shared receiver. The no-interferer LS estimate equals the serving channel, while reused-DMRS estimates equal the coherent sum of serving and interfering channels at pilot REs; the estimator does not reveal a perfect serving-only CSI. This locally validates M11 for one aligned, single-port, single-layer SISO receiver case with no noise and constant channels. It does not cover orthogonal pilots, multiport separation, asynchronous clocks, or realistic mobility. Full local suite: 622 passed, 3 skipped, 87.19% coverage; Ruff, format, mypy (162 source files), lock, and package build pass. The preceding portable CI run validates commit `e15c655`; CI for this test-only follow-up will be checked after push, and hosted CI skips native Sionna tests.

**Edition 3.156 native multi-node RT channel parity:** A 2-TX×2-RX CPU/LLVM free-space `Paths` solve now maps through the NR CFR adapter and joint network-grid composer. The tx0→rx0 absolute-delay frequency response from that multi-node solve matches an independent 1×1 solve at the same geometry, carrier, array, and solver seed (M01). Joint received grids from the actual multi-node channel equal the sum of separately activated source contributions in the aligned no-impairment case (M08). The native multi-node extraction and both focused native PHY checks pass locally. This covers static LOS/SISO with a single shared homogeneous array and excludes multipath, MIMO ports, scheduling/power control realism, and asynchronous sources. Full suite: 623 passed, 3 skipped, 87.19% coverage; Ruff, format, mypy (162 source files), lock, and package build pass. Hosted CI skips native Sionna RT and is pending for this follow-up commit.

**Edition 3.157 stable name-based network identity:** Scene extraction now emits UMCD nodes, arrays, and links in deterministic device-name order while preserving each transmitter/receiver's actual Sionna tensor-axis index in the link map. Contract tests reverse both device insertion orders and verify identical named episode records, array geometry/weights, links, and name-to-axis resolution. A native CPU/LLVM test repeats a 2×2 solve with reversed TX/RX insertion, then confirms the canonicalized multi-link path-oracle table matches across ordering, including per-link delays, complex gains, angles, Doppler and interaction rows (M02). This verifies insertion-order invariance for the static homogeneous-array fixture; device renaming, heterogeneous arrays and solver behavior across changed physical scene conditions remain distinct concerns. Full suite: 624 passed, 3 skipped, 87.19% coverage; maintained Ruff, format, mypy (162 source files), lock and package-build checks pass.

**Edition 3.158 bounded channel reciprocity check:** Native CPU/LLVM ray traces were run in both link directions for the same static free-space geometry, with a 3.5-GHz carrier, one isotropic single-port antenna at each endpoint, LOS only, and identical solver settings. The absolute-delay NR CFR responses match within numerical tolerance after swapping transmitter and receiver (M03). This verifies the reciprocal propagation model for this ideal reference, not hardware reciprocity, directional/dual-polarized arrays, FDD frequency separation, calibration errors, or moving endpoints. Full local suite: 625 passed, 3 skipped, 87.19% coverage; maintained Ruff, formatting, mypy (162 source files), lock verification and package build pass. Hosted CI excludes native RT.

**Edition 3.159 native RT-to-PUSCH network episode:** A native CPU/LLVM 2-TX×1-RX SISO free-space solve now feeds both cross-link CFRs through the aligned-grid network composer. The same generated Sionna PUSCH/DMRS grid is transmitted on both sources, and the resulting receiver grid is passed to the Sionna LS estimator. Across DMRS REs, the serving-only estimate matches its RT CFR and the cochannel estimate matches the coherent sum of both RT CFRs within 1% relative tolerance, closing the earlier gap between separately tested RT composition and the NR receiver episode. This validates only a static, direct-only, aligned, noise-free fixture with reused pilots and isotropic single-port arrays; it does not cover independent payloads, scheduling, asynchronous timing/CFO, multiport separation, realistic power control, or field channels. Full suite: 626 passed, 3 skipped, 87.19% coverage; Ruff check/format pass. Hosted CI excludes native Sionna RT.

**Edition 3.160 fail-closed convergence-run provenance:** `run_rt_solver_convergence.py` now compares its exact bytes with the current Git `HEAD` version and refuses to run when the script is modified, untracked, or outside a Git checkout. This prevents recording only a script hash for a runner that cannot be recovered from the declared source revision. Tests cover committed, modified, and untracked runners. Audit of the earlier local scattering sweeps found that their package-source hash matches the recorded tracked source revision, but their runner hash does not match any committed version; those outputs remain exploratory. Full suite: 628 passed, 3 skipped, 87.17% coverage; Ruff, format, mypy (162 source files), lock verification, and package build pass. Hosted CI [run 39792996783](https://github.com/nntk072/5g-nr-channel-condition/actions/runs/39792996783) passed all four jobs; hosted CI does not execute native Sionna RT.

**Edition 3.161 reproducible scattering sensitivity control:** Re-ran paired zero-scattering and screen-material scattering-coefficient 0.5 controls with the committed runner and matching package-source/script hashes. Each report includes complex CFR vectors for 256 bins, two geometry-labeled links, seeds 11/23/47, 20,000 samples/source, and solver depths 0–3. With zero scattering, depth-3 valid path counts were 48 LOS and 32 blocked-NLOS. At coefficient 0.5, LOS produced 1,187–1,209 paths (1,139–1,161 classified diffuse); blocked-NLOS produced 73–79 paths (41–47 diffuse). Under the recorded per-path coefficient-squared power metric, diffuse power was 2.86–2.99% for LOS and 0.247–0.320% for blocked-NLOS. First-arrival delays remained 3.33564 ns and 13.34256 ns for the solved LOS/NLOS cases. These are simulator sensitivity results for one built-in screen scene, not calibrated material or field-channel evidence; one path-sample budget does not establish convergence. Raw provenance-complete reports: [zero scattering](results/rt-box-scattering-committed-131ccfd/scattering-0-depth-3.json) and [coefficient 0.5](results/rt-box-scattering-committed-131ccfd/scattering-0.5-depth-3.json). The full local suite remains 628 passed, 3 skipped, 87.17% coverage.

**Edition 3.162 moving-obstacle retrace assertion:** Strengthened the native CPU/LLVM moving-screen trajectory regression: a screen translated 10 m over 1 s at 10 m/s changes the independent geometry oracle from blocked to visible, the depth-zero Sionna path count from 0 to 1, and the saved full-path CFR; both snapshots retain valid full-solver paths. This verifies retracing and channel change for one controlled screen crossing, not path births/deaths within an OFDM slot or realistic moving-scene statistics.

**Edition 3.163 moving-object Doppler validation:** A native CPU/LLVM UMCD path-truth regression moves the reflective screen at 1 m/s through three 100-ms snapshots with stationary endpoints. For the path interacting at the screen, Sionna's Doppler agrees with the independent geometric interaction formula `f_D = f_c/c · (k_out − k_in)·v` at each snapshot and with `−f_c·dτ/dt` from centered differences of the saved absolute delays; both checks use 2% relative and 0.1-Hz absolute tolerances. The sidecar limitation now identifies this narrow validation rather than declaring moving-object Doppler wholly unvalidated. This is one specular screen path in one synthetic scene; diffuse/multiple interactions, other objects and materials, and path birth/death transitions remain unqualified.

**Edition 3.164 explicit no-path trajectory policy:** RT trajectory generation now defaults to rejecting empty full-path snapshots. Opt-in `no_path_policy="noise_only_mixed_episode"` stores zero placeholders for unavailable CFR targets, applies element-wise false masks in both NR records and the canonical UMCD `PhysicalChannelView`, sets `channel_truth_valid=false`, and preserves the path oracle's `NO_PATH` solve status. A native LLVM moving-screen episode transitions from blocked/no-path to visible/direct-path; its blocked receiver grid contains nonzero AWGN using the existing common trajectory-relative noise variance. All-no-path episodes still fail closed because that relative-SNR policy has no signal-power reference. The targeted UMCD/trajectory and configuration suite passes 41 tests; Ruff, formatting, mypy (three touched source modules), and diff checks pass. This is explicit simulator no-path handling, not a measured outage or proof that the physical channel is zero; an absolute calibrated noise/link-budget mode remains required for standalone all-outage samples.

**Edition 3.165 absolute-power outage observations:** Added `noise_only_absolute_power` for trajectories with empty paths, including episodes where every frame is a simulator no-path. Configuration requires `snr_db=None` and explicit `tx_power_per_re_w`; receiver noise is `k_B T Δf F`, and the transmitted PUSCH grid is scaled by `sqrt(P_RE)`. Channel estimates are converted back to gain units and their error variances divided by `P_RE`. A native LLVM blocked one-frame test verifies finite noise-only receiver inputs and estimates, positive thermal noise, false CFR masks, and an UMCD round trip with `NO_PATH` and an all-false physical-channel validity mask. This is a parameterized simulation using declared power, temperature, noise figure, and subcarrier bandwidth; it does not validate those parameters against a real receiver or establish measured outage behavior. Relative-SNR all-no-path episodes continue to fail because they have no signal-power reference.

**Edition 3.166 matched-depth evidence and NR observation build path:** The high-budget controlled box sweep reports 100,000 samples/source and three seeds at each depth 0–9. From depth 8 to 9, median LOS path count, CFR mean power, and RMS delay spread change by +52.6%, +55.1%, and +13.4%; the blocked link changes by +77.0%, −18.0%, and +20.7%. These paired results fail to establish depth convergence, so G3.01 remains open and no production `max_depth` is declared. Separately, the TDL/CDL→PUSCH/DMRS adapter now creates receiver-visible standardized-channel records with oracle targets isolated, plus a reproducible local build command and immutable, provenance-rich report. This is still an ideal static frequency-domain SISO/SIMO PHY fixture, not a completed realistic NR receiver or a physical geometric LOS dataset. See [`PHYSICS_VERIFICATION_STATUS.md`](PHYSICS_VERIFICATION_STATUS.md) for test evidence and scope.

**Edition 3.167 max-depth extension to 20:** Repeated the fixed `box_one_screen` LOS/blocked-NLOS cases with three matched seeds and 100,000 samples/source through depths 0–20, recording full complex CFR vectors. The last step (19→20) still changes the median CFR by 13.09% LOS and 19.21% NLOS; delay spread rises 3.11% and 2.71%; path counts rise 7.42% and 5.33%. Both scene materials have zero scattering coefficient, so this isolates the depth behavior of the built-in specular/refraction fixture. G3.01 remains open: a finite depth-20 reference is not proven ground truth and does not support choosing a production truncation. The provenance-backed result and four-panel plot are `results/rt-box-los-nlos-convergence-depth20-100k-20261022.json` and `figures/rt-box-los-nlos-convergence-depth20-100k-20261022.png`. This single controlled geometry does not qualify other scene families or material models.

**Edition 3.168 matched ray-budget/scattering controls:** A committed Sionna RT runner reproduced a fully paired 42-case zero-versus-0.5 screen-scattering matrix, with three seeds and four sample budgets. The zero control has zero diffuse rays/power and stable CFR by 2k samples for this fixture. At coefficient 0.5, diffuse path count increases with budget, and at depth 3 the 20k-budget CFR differs from the 100k comparison reference by median 11.9% LOS (up to 38.4%) and 7.8% blocked NLOS (up to 9.1%); the comparison reference is not ground truth. The scattering intervention activates the solver mechanism but is uncalibrated. Thus this closes a narrow flag-versus-coefficient diagnostic while leaving G3.02 budget convergence open and G3.03/G3.04 limited to mechanism evidence. Raw reports and the [matched diagnostic figure](figures/rt-box-los-nlos-scattering-budget-verified-20261022.png) are recorded in the physics status.


**Edition 3.172 candidate-cap saturation instrumentation:** The convergence runner now records Sionna’s candidate-buffer count before invalid-path filtering and an explicit cap-filled flag. On box_one_screen at depth 3, 20k samples/source, and screen S=0.5, every LOS seed fills cap 1k, though only 944–947 paths survive validation; cap 10k and 1M store 1,324–1,345 candidates and do not saturate. Matched LOS CFR error is 0.4648 at 1k and 6.92e-7 at 10k versus the finite 1M comparison reference. The blocked link stays at 238–244 candidates and does not approach cap 1k. This directly audits candidate-budget saturation in the convergence experiment and demonstrates actual cap truncation in one fixture. G3.05 remains open because this instrumentation is not yet integrated across canonical dataset generation or qualified across broader scene/material/depth/sample matrices. See physics-status edition 3.178 and the [candidate-aware figure](figures/rt-box-los-nlos-path-cap-sweep-depth3-s05-candidate-audit-20261022.png).


**Edition 3.172 candidate-cap saturation instrumentation:** The convergence runner now records Sionna candidate-buffer count before invalid-path filtering and an explicit cap-filled flag. On box_one_screen at depth 3, 20k samples/source, and screen S=0.5, every LOS seed fills cap 1k, though only 944–947 paths survive validation; cap 10k and 1M store 1,324–1,345 candidates and do not saturate. Matched LOS CFR error is 0.4648 at 1k and 6.92e-7 at 10k versus the finite 1M comparison reference. The blocked link stays at 238–244 candidates and does not approach cap 1k. This directly audits candidate-budget saturation in the convergence experiment and demonstrates actual cap truncation in one fixture. G3.05 remains open because this instrumentation is not yet integrated across canonical dataset generation or qualified across broader scene/material/depth/sample matrices. See physics-status edition 3.178 and the candidate-aware figure at figures/rt-box-los-nlos-path-cap-sweep-depth3-s05-candidate-audit-20261022.png.

**Edition 3.173 fail-closed cap handling for RT releases:** The static RT dataset builder now records Sionna’s pre-validation candidate count and rejects any sample that fills its configured per-source budget; rejection counts are preserved in the generation report. The retraced NR trajectory producer records the same counter per frame and aborts rather than publishing a trajectory when a frame fills its cap. The configured cap participates in immutable run identity. Mocked static and native LLVM trajectory regressions verify rejection behavior, and the full suite passes (691 passed, 3 skipped). This closes silent cap handling for the current single-source static and trajectory builders, not cap qualification across every scene/material/budget combination; G3.05 remains open for that matrix and future multi-source solver support.
**Edition 3.174 per-source candidate-cap accounting:** The shared pinned-Sionna audit now derives source-wise candidate counts from the PathsBuffer source-index array before invalid-path filtering. Current static and moving NR builders continue to enforce the cap fail-closed, and their provenance retains the per-source vector. Convergence, dataset ray-budget, and path-visual-QA tools also record or reject cap-filled results under the recorded cap. Native LLVM confirms two simultaneous transmitters each reach cap 1 without losing per-source attribution; full suite 693 passed/3 skipped, focused RT tests 53 passed, Ruff and mypy pass. G3.05 remains open: more scene families, material states, interaction depths, sample budgets, and direct complex-channel comparisons are needed before any cap can be qualified for release.
**Edition 3.175 candidate-aware cap stress on thin-screen and City43 scenes:** At commit `0776d4a`, repeated 1k/10k/1M path-cap comparisons with the pre-validation per-source counter and stored CFR vectors. At depth 6/100k samples/source on the S=0.5 box fixture, cap 10k still saturates all LOS seeds and produces median CFR L2 error 0.300 versus cap 1M; cap 1k saturates both LOS and blocked-NLOS seeds, with median errors 0.417 and 0.565. On the actual 16-building City43 scene at depth 3/20k samples/source, cap 1k saturates all LOS seeds but none of the NLOS seeds; LOS CFR error is 0.131 median, while cap 10k matches the finite 1M comparison within 7.2e-7 on these two links. The City43 figure shows all 16 OBJ meshes and both audited links. These matched diagnostics prove cap sensitivity in selected configurations, not material realism or universal sufficiency; G3.05 remains open.

**Edition 3.176 Type-2 multiport DM-RS qualification slice:** Added a native CPU regression check for a two-layer PUSCH configuration with Type-2 DM-RS and two CDM groups without data. It verifies the shared stream masks, the expected TS 38.211 V19.1.0 Table 6.4.1.1.3-2 frequency offsets, nonzero pilots, and mutual pilot orthogonality. This strengthens one standardized receiver-conformance case; it does not qualify other port groups, partial allocations, hopping, transform precoding, or RT production.

**Edition 3.177 four-layer Type-2 DM-RS check:** Extended the Type-2 pilot orthogonality regression to a four-layer configuration and checks the full pilot Gram matrix. The adapter currently permits 1/2/4 antenna ports, so no six-port claim is made. This improves one native PHY conformance slice; it does not validate MIMO channel estimation/separation, all DM-RS port assignments, or the remaining open G5.01 cases.

**Edition 3.178 Type-2 four-layer estimator reference:** Added a noiseless native CPU 4×4 complex flat-channel fixture and verified that Sionna's Type-2 multiport DM-RS estimator recovers all 16 channel coefficients over the complete tested grid within `1e-6`. This closes only that deterministic flat-channel separation fixture; frequency-selective MIMO, noise/interpolation behavior, and physical propagation remain unqualified.

**Edition 3.179 material-scattering evidence audit:** The G3.03 blocker cannot be cleared by assigning Sionna's ITU-R P.2040 permittivity/conductivity values alone: its diffuse `S` and angular scattering pattern require separate evidence. Published measurements at 8, 12, and 28 GHz are useful external-validation candidates but do not directly calibrate the project's 3.5-GHz RT stratum. The G3 candidate/license audit now records this frequency-scope limit and the target-frequency evidence needed; no new RT run or production data was generated.

**Edition 3.180 near-carrier wall-reflection source:** Added Landron et al.'s 4.0-GHz limestone, glass, and brick reflection-coefficient measurements as a possible near-band model prior. The study supports rough-surface reflection correction, not a target-frequency Sionna diffuse-scattering calibration or a full 3D scattering pattern, so G3.03 remains open pending validated transfer/measurement evidence.

**Edition 3.181 Type-2 single-symbol partial-allocation DM-RS (G5.01 partial):** Added eight native CPU checks for Type-2 DM-RS in 10-symbol mapping-A/B PUSCH allocations across all four additional-position values. The local pilot timing matches TS 38.211 V19.1.0 Table 6.4.1.1.3-3 and the Type-2 frequency comb matches Table 6.4.1.1.3-2; the focused native PUSCH file passes 55 tests. Other allocation lengths, Type-2 double-symbol partial timing, hopping, transform precoding, and scheduled full-slot behavior remain open; G5.01 stays open.

**Edition 3.182 end-to-end synchronized PUSCH under in-CP multipath (G5.09 partial):** Added a seeded CPU waveform-to-decoder fixture with a two-sample echo, 750-Hz CFO, two-sample capture offset, and 45-dB sample-domain AWGN. CP-based timing/CFO estimates meet the frozen tolerances; after correction, the matched Sionna PUSCH decoder recovers all transmitted bits and passes every CRC using analytical perfect CFR. This validates one static in-CP case, not DM-RS channel estimation, mobility, phase noise, IQ imbalance, or general NR receiver performance; G5.09 remains open.

**Edition 3.183 exact four-layer flat-MIMO receiver case (G5.13 partial):** The Type-2 four-layer DM-RS test now also sends the resulting known full-rank 4×4 flat-channel observation through Sionna's matched PUSCH decoder with analytical perfect CSI. All transmitted bits and CRCs are recovered exactly at zero noise. This complements channel-estimator separation with one flat MIMO decoding reference; it does not qualify frequency-selective/noisy MIMO or general receiver BLER.

**Edition 3.184 linear DM-RS interpolation against analytical channel truth (G5.10 partial):** Added an all-resource-element comparison for Sionna's linear LS interpolation on a static two-tap 4-RB channel. The estimated CFR has normalized MSE below 0.02 and improves on nearest-neighbor interpolation in this controlled noiseless case. Broader delay, Doppler, SNR, pilot-density, and MIMO interpolation behavior remains open.

**Edition 3.185 target-frequency scattering evidence audit (G3.03 remains open):** Found an exact-3.5-GHz anechoic measurement study for material reflection/scattering, but the accessible abstract and metadata do not disclose numerical material-specific angular/complex data. ITU-R P.1410-6 contributes a roughness- and angle-dependent power-domain model over 3–60 GHz, while explicitly omitting phase and polarization; a 2024 3.5-GHz method paper's accessible results calibrate a metal sheet rather than the candidate construction materials. These are actionable measurement leads, not a calibrated Sionna diffuse model. The source audit records the evidence limits; no RT qualification run or production data was generated.

**Edition 3.186 seed-name and result-provenance spot-audit (G0.07 partial):** Two files with date-like seed suffixes were checked against embedded RNG seeds, source revisions, commit dates, evidence grades, and tracked artifacts. Their source commits predate 2026-10-23, so the suffixes are seeds rather than evidence that the results were generated in the future. Both remain descriptive synthetic diagnostics; they lack exact command/timestamp provenance, and one raw-report hash has no tracked raw file. The broader result corpus still requires audit; do not promote these as fully reproducible benchmark evidence.

**Edition 3.187 partial-allocation DM-RS absolute symbol index (G5.01 partial):** Added a native Sionna regression check for mapping-B PUSCH with allocation `(start_symbol=3, duration=10)`. Its generated pilot sequence matches an independent TS 38.211 Gold-sequence reference using absolute slot symbol `l=3` and explicitly differs from the incorrect allocation-relative `l=0` sequence. This verifies the DM-RS sequence index for this one nonzero-start case; the adapter still emits a 10-symbol allocation grid, so full 14-symbol slot waveform assembly, placement, and decode remain unimplemented and G5.01 stays open.

**Edition 3.188 frequency-grid slot placement for partial PUSCH (G5.01 partial):** Added allocation-to-slot placement and inverse extraction methods to `SionnaPUSCHLSReceiver`. A 10-symbol mapping-B allocation starting at symbol 3 is embedded in a 14-symbol frequency grid with zero-filled unused symbols, extracted at the configured interval, and decoded with exact payload/CRC recovery. This verifies frequency-grid placement and receiver slicing for one scheduled allocation; it does not perform OFDM modulation, time-domain slot assembly, or multiplex other physical channels. G5.01 remains open.

**Edition 3.189 partial double-symbol DM-RS timing (G5.01 partial):** Added four native CPU cases for TS 38.211 V19.1.0 Table 6.4.1.1.3-4: mapping A with a 10-symbol allocation and mapping B with a 10-symbol allocation starting at symbol 3, each with `dmrs-AdditionalPosition` 0/1. Type-2 double-symbol positions and their Type-2 frequency comb match the reference. This closes one partial-allocation timing gap only; other durations, hopping, transform precoding, and broader scheduling remain open.

**Edition 3.190 full-slot OFDM round-trip for scheduled PUSCH (G5.01 partial):** Added `SionnaPUSCHLSReceiver.modulate_slot_grid` and extended the mapping-B partial-allocation receiver fixture to place a 10-symbol PUSCH at slot symbols `[3,13)`, OFDM-modulate all 14 symbols, demodulate, extract the scheduled interval, and recover payload/CRC. The waveform has the expected 14-symbol sample length and its demodulated grid matches the placed frequency grid within numerical tolerance. This uses Sionna's configured CP and no channel; it does not qualify exact TS CP timing, propagation, or coexistence with other physical channels. G5.01 remains open.

**Edition 3.191 scheduled partial-slot PUSCH through an in-CP echo (G5.09 partial):** Added a seeded sample-domain two-tap channel to the full-slot mapping-B waveform test, with a two-sample echo inside the configured cyclic prefix and proper-complex AWGN at variance `1e-5`. After full-slot OFDM demodulation and extraction at the configured symbol offset, the DM-RS-based Sionna PUSCH decoder recovers the exact payload and CRC. This is one static, high-SNR, in-CP case without CFO, phase noise, IQ imbalance, or mobility; G5.09 remains open.

**Edition 3.192 carrier-relative BWP placement (G5.02 partial):** Added explicit carrier-grid size/start and BWP start parameters, preserving the existing full-band default. A native CPU 6-RB BWP at CRB 7 in a 24-RB carrier starting at CRB 2 maps to carrier-relative bins 60–131. Full-carrier OFDM modulation/demodulation reconstructs the placed grid and the cropped PUSCH decodes with exact payload/CRC. This validates one integer-CRB offset and 30-kHz SCS; guard/DC conventions, additional offsets, and fractional allocations remain open.

**Edition 3.193 BWP placement matrix across numerologies (G5.02 partial):** Native CPU regression cases now verify selected lower, interior, and upper BWP positions for 4/6/12-RB allocations in a 24-RB carrier at 15/30/60/120-kHz SCS. Each case checks carrier-relative active bins, zero fill, inverse BWP extraction, and a full-carrier OFDM modulation/demodulation round-trip using CP scaled from the BWP grid. The separate 6-RB offset case still provides payload/CRC decode evidence. This does not establish standardized guard/DC conventions, all width/offset combinations, fractional allocations, or decoding across the matrix; G5.02 remains partial.

**Edition 3.194 end-to-end BWP placement matrix decode (G5.02 partial):** Extended the selected 16 width/offset/numerology cases to generate seeded PUSCH grids, map them into the carrier, run full-carrier OFDM modulation/demodulation and BWP extraction, and recover the exact transmitted payload with valid transport-block CRC in each case. This demonstrates end-to-end decoding for the tested configurations; guard/DC conventions, the full width/offset cross-product, and fractional allocations remain unqualified, so G5.02 remains partial.

**Edition 3.195 LS pilot bias/variance across DM-RS and SNR (G5.10 partial):** Added a native CPU 1,024-draw Monte Carlo regression over every pilot RE for 18 combinations of Type-1/Type-2, single-/double-symbol DM-RS, one/two reserved CDM groups, and complex AWGN variance 0.01/0.05/0.2. The test checks the expected pilot variance `N0/(2 × DMRS_length × reserved_CDM_groups)`, empirical pooled variance within 12%, and per-pilot empirical bias within five predicted standard errors. It strengthens the flat-channel pilot-site statistical reference; non-flat interpolation error/covariance, mobility/Doppler, and wider operating regimes remain open.

**Edition 3.196 linear-interpolator variance calibration diagnostic (G5.10 partial):** Added an 18-case CPU Monte Carlo check for a static two-tap channel over all REs, across Type-1/Type-2 DM-RS patterns and three noise variances. Empirical mean estimates agree with the noiseless interpolated result within six standard errors, but empirical/reported pooled variance reaches 0.68 in a tested Type-2 configuration. Inspection of pinned Sionna 2.2 source confirms its `LinearInterpolator` applies linear weights to the marginal variance tensor rather than exact covariance propagation; receiver metadata now marks this output as approximate. This is a scoped negative qualification result, not a corrected uncertainty estimator; calibrated non-flat interpolation variance and broader delay/Doppler behavior remain open.

**Edition 3.197 nearest-neighbor PUSCH variance reference (G5.10 partial):** Repeated the 18-case all-RE analytical two-tap Monte Carlo matrix with nearest-neighbor interpolation. For all resource elements with nonzero reported variance, pooled empirical variance agrees with Sionna's reported marginal variance within 12%; the mean remains within six standard errors of the noiseless nearest-neighbor estimate. The metadata explicitly distinguishes this mode from linear interpolation, whose reported variance remains approximate. This establishes a bounded marginal-variance mode for the tested static channels, not low-bias interpolation or dynamic-channel uncertainty calibration.

**Edition 3.198 paired PUSCH interpolation bias/variance tradeoff (G5.10 partial):** The paired all-RE test compares noiseless linear and nearest-neighbor estimator NMSE against exact two-tap channel truth across six selected Type-1/Type-2 DM-RS, symbol-length and additional-position configurations. Linear interpolation has lower noiseless NMSE in all six; nearest-neighbor reported variance matches Monte Carlo within 12%, while the linear variance remains approximate. The result is scoped to these static analytic channels and three noise levels, not universal method ranking or dynamic-channel uncertainty calibration.
**Edition 3.199 CP-timed symbol-static Doppler interpolation fixture (G5.10 partial):** Added a four-case native CPU Monte Carlo test for Dopplers 35/350 Hz and DM-RS additional positions 1/2 using normal-CP symbol start times at 30-kHz SCS. A two-tap frequency-domain CFR evolves between OFDM symbols; with 1,024 independent draws, the test bounds empirical mean bias, checks empirical/reported variance (linear 0.60–1.05; nearest-neighbor within 12%), and verifies lower noiseless interpolation NMSE for linear in these cases. It does not model intra-symbol ICI, waveform CP/ISI, or establish broad mobility robustness; G5.10 remains partial.

**Edition 3.200 spatial-buffer and time-window split grouping (G7.08 partial):** Added a deterministic pre-split utility that links parent subjects when any sampled 3D positions are within a declared Euclidean buffer in the same named meter coordinate frame, or their half-open time windows overlap on the same named clock. Spatial matches use a grid index followed by exact distance checks; temporal overlap chains are grouped transitively. Tests cover cell boundaries, exact buffer inclusion, coordinate-frame and clock separation, touching non-overlap endpoints, registry assignment, and invalid geometry/time inputs. This is a grouping primitive only: no frozen corpus has been processed, spatial coverage/false-merge consequences have not been audited, and the split lock is not externally pinned, so G7.08 remains open.

**Edition 3.201 fixed-delay TDL profile admission (G4.08 partial):** Standardized-record admission now distinguishes the fixed TS 38.101-4 V19.2.2 profiles from scalable TR 38.901 TDLs and checks profile availability against carrier range: A30 in FR1/FR2; B100 and C300 in FR1; C60 in FR2; D30 in FR1/FR2; A10 and D10 in FR2. A10/D10 additionally require an explicit nominal RF channel bandwidth greater than 200 MHz; the adapter stores it separately from sampled subcarrier-grid bandwidth. Native tests accept the supported cases and reject unavailable profile/range and 200-MHz/missing-bandwidth cases. This closes these profile admission combinations, not every standard bandwidth/numerology/test-case restriction; the broader G4.08 scope remains partial.

**Edition 3.202 fail-closed public release packaging (G7.11 partial):** Added `datasets.public_release.export_public_release`, which only packages explicitly licensed and redistribution-cleared files after verifying every artifact SHA-256 and the release manifest. For known OSM-derived outputs, the manifest must bind each file to the audited scene asset-tree hashes and provide a release-specific ODbL classification, attribution location, direct ODbL and OSM copyright links, source data offer, software revision, source-date/ID/conversion records or documented gaps, and complete third-party license closure. The exporter emits the attribution and license notice and tests reject restricted redistribution, incomplete closure, lineage mismatch, and altered artifacts. Current ODbL source records remain incomplete, so this implementation does not clear any present OSM-derived artifact for public release; it enforces a reviewed declaration and cannot independently establish arbitrary input provenance or legal classification.

**Edition 3.203 sample-domain per-path Doppler and ICI fixture (G5.09 partial):** Added `apply_time_varying_multipath` for causal integer-delay taps with per-path complex gain and Doppler applied at each receive sample. A native analytic one-tone OFDM fixture confirms a static two-tap channel remains diagonal after CP removal, while a 0.2-subcarrier-spacing echo Doppler creates measurable off-tone ICI. This supplies a sample-domain mobility primitive that retains intra-symbol variation; it does not model fractional delays, fading/Doppler evolution, or payload decoding, so G5.09 remains open for receiver impact under mobility.

**Edition 3.204 paired PUSCH receiver impact under full-waveform Doppler (G5.09 partial):** A predeclared controlled-fixture criterion required zero-Doppler DM-RS-estimating CPU receiver BLER ≤0.05, an absolute BLER increase ≥0.20 at 750-Hz echo Doppler on the same payloads, and paired exact two-sided McNemar/binomial p≤0.01. The hash-bound v1 run `docs/research/results/g5-time-varying-multipath-pusch-20261023.json` passes over 192 paired codewords: 0/192 CRC failures at 0 and 350 Hz, 93/192 at 750 Hz (BLER 0.484; Wilson 95% interval [0.415, 0.555]), and 192/192 at 1,500 Hz; the 750-Hz paired BLER increase is 0.484 with p=2.02e-28. This is conditional on one fixed synthetic two-path channel (zero independent channel draws), 6-RB/30-kHz PUSCH, 3-sample echo, with no waveform AWGN and decoder-assumed noise variance 1e-5. It demonstrates a reproducible receiver sensitivity to combined inter-symbol channel aging and intra-symbol variation, not an isolated ICI effect, deployment BLER curve, or general channel-population result; G5.09 remains open. Reproduce with `uv run --no-sync --locked python experiments/g5_time_varying_multipath_receiver.py`.

**Edition 3.205 CDL NLOS Doppler covariance against a ray-level reference (G4.07 partial):** Added native CPU coverage for CDL A/B/C using 4,096 independent draws and paired channel samples at 0/0.7 ms. An independent reference sums the profile's cluster powers over the randomized Cartesian pairing of Table 7.5-3 azimuth/zenith ray angles and applies the TR 38.901 velocity-projection Doppler phase for a 30-m/s UT at 3.5 GHz. Real and imaginary empirical covariance errors pass the draw-level confidence bounds for all three NLOS profiles. This closes a bounded statistical model-consistency gap; CDL-D/E LOS composition, broader speeds/angles, arbitrary arrays, and external physical validation remain open.

**Edition 3.206 G3.03 target-frequency literature disposition:** The follow-up source audit found exact-3.5-GHz building-entry measurements for concrete and coated glass, but they report transmission loss, not bistatic diffuse reflection. García Sánchez et al.'s 2023 facade study uses directional antennas and time-gating to reduce other multipath contributions while measuring building penetration loss; it does not report a bistatic diffuse-scattering calibration. The raw FR3 scattering dataset exposes bistatic VNA Touchstone files for five building materials at 8–12 GHz; this is useful for adjacent-band method validation but does not calibrate the project's 3.5-GHz scenes. The 2026 8/12/28-GHz study reports frequency-dependent scattering behavior. G3.03 remains open; no cross-frequency coefficient is transferred, and no RT qualification run is made for this literature update. The source disposition is recorded in `G3_SCENE_FAMILY_LICENSE_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md`.

**Edition 3.207 PUSCH symbol-static Doppler control (G5.09 partial):** A matched control evaluates the 750-Hz echo phase at each OFDM-symbol start and holds it over that symbol, preserving inter-symbol aging while removing intra-symbol evolution. On the same 192 payloads, continuous sample-time Doppler has 93 CRC failures (BLER 0.484); the symbol-static control has 88 (BLER 0.458). The continuous-minus-symbol-static BLER increase is 0.026 with paired exact two-sided p=0.405, failing the predeclared incremental criterion of increase ≥0.10 and p≤0.01. Thus the prior comparison to zero Doppler demonstrates combined time-variation sensitivity, while this fixture provides no evidence for an incremental intra-symbol ICI effect. The experiment calls Sionna modulation, demodulation, and decoding through the lazy PHY adapter. It uses one fixed synthetic channel, no waveform AWGN, and decoder-assumed noise variance 1e-5; G5.09 remains open. The v2 report `docs/research/results/g5-time-varying-multipath-pusch-symbol-control-20261023.json` binds source hashes and recomputable per-block outcomes.

**Edition 3.208 G3.03 material-fit acceptance contract:** Added project-defined, non-3GPP calibration margins to the Unified Multipath plan: held-out complete incidence-angle groups must meet median and p95 integrated reflected-plus-diffuse power errors of ≤1.5 dB and ≤3 dB, normalized angular-power CDF distance ≤0.10, and 90–98% empirical coverage by nominal 95% prediction intervals whose median width is ≤3 dB. V/H polarization and material-to-scene mapping are qualified separately; power/angular fit cannot validate coherent complex CSI without phase-referenced held-out field measurements. These criteria make future evidence assessable but do not close G3.03. Exact-carrier diffuse-scattering data remain unavailable in the audited sources, and production RT stays gated.

**Edition 3.209 corpus-wide provenance completeness scan (G0.07 partial):** A read-only auditor and fixture tests now scan `.json` and `.json.gz` results. Across 182 files, none is malformed, but all 182 have at least one provenance issue: missing command (182), missing UTC timestamp (180), missing config hash (177), missing recognized source hash (63), and malformed source hash (2). Repository-relative references resolve correctly and no declared local input is unresolved. Historical results remain unchanged. The scanner checks field syntax and local-reference existence, not source/config hash-to-byte equality or external provenance recovery; strict mode is available but is not enabled for this legacy corpus. G0.07 remains open. Reproduce with `uv run --no-sync --locked python scripts/audit_research_result_provenance.py`.

**Edition 3.210 dependency-ready qualification work (2026-10-23):** Split Policy v2 hardens finite numeric geometry against squared-distance underflow, coordinate/radius quotient overflow, booleans, and non-real inputs, with adversarial tests. A G3.03 measurement-only evaluator now enforces complete held-out incidence groups and the project-defined power, angular, and interval margins independently by material/polarization/angle stratum; synthetic fixtures test its contract but do not count as calibration evidence. G4.07 adds one native CDL-A, two-element spatial-covariance comparison to an independent TR 38.901 ray-level calculation; all four unique real covariance components pass the predeclared simultaneous 99% interval/precision rule for 4,096 draws. G5's waveform-AWGN Doppler receiver experiment observes 0/192 CRC failures at 0 Hz and 6/192 at 750 Hz (BLER increase 0.03125, exact paired p=0.03125), failing its predeclared >=0.20 increase and p<=0.01 criteria; the matched 750-Hz symbol-static control likewise shows no incremental ICI effect. These remain bounded simulation results. The provenance auditor now accepts correctly formed named source-to-hash maps; 182 of 183 results still have at least one metadata gap, while the newly generated AWGN-enabled G5 report has complete recognized metadata. No production RT corpus or advanced training is authorized by these additions.

**Edition 3.212 G3.03 statistical admission policy:** Require explicitly attested physical acquisition-unit IDs for every held-out incidence group, equal influence per unit, and verification of a hashed complete split manifest against a preregistered expected material × polarization × incidence-stratum set. Resample units as clusters and control family-wise error across metric × stratum claims with Bonferroni-adjusted two-sided percentile-bootstrap intervals having 95% family-wise coverage, wholly within each point margin. Fewer than 20 units per stratum is not assessable; 20 is a pilot floor and cannot qualify. These safeguards and a precision-powered sample-size plan must be preregistered and implemented/verified before any qualification claim; until then every G3.03 material result is unconditionally pilot-only.

**Edition 3.213 G3.03 evaluator hardening and bounded G4/G5 checks:** The measurement evaluator's hashed manifest now binds each held-out sample to its complete split, material, polarization, incidence group/angle, and acquisition/provenance IDs; the expected stratum set is also hash-bound. Equal-acquisition-unit summaries and Bonferroni-adjusted cluster-bootstrap intervals are exercised for both in-margin and failing-coverage synthetic fixtures. Because the evaluator cannot authenticate physical independence, these intervals remain diagnostic and can never enable a G3 qualification/admission pass; a precision-powered sample-size plan and external provenance review remain open. Added a native 15-kHz FR1 Case-A SSB candidate-symbol utility checked at the 3-GHz branch boundary (candidate timing only, no PBCH mapper) and a dual-VH CDL-A aggregate XPR covariance-trace check. Neither closes broad G5 conformance, all-profile polarization behavior, or RT/material qualification.

**Edition 3.214 bounded G4/G5/G7 component updates (2026-10-23):** G4.07 now includes one dual-VH CDL-A v19.2 per-port-pair XPR power statistical check using simultaneous 99% intervals. It remains a single profile/configuration and does not qualify broad standardized-profile or geometric-RT polarization behavior. G5.14 exports Case-A 15-kHz FR1 SSB candidate timing alongside the existing PSS/SSS fixture; PBCH/PBCH-DMRS, PRS, SRS, and broad G5 remain open. G7.02 canonical target definitions bind semantic fields, units, reference/gauge, aggregation, source, and version in a SHA-256 digest, while legacy defaults remain for noncanonical records. G7.06 adds tamper detection for a compressed UMCD object. G7.10 adds a cross-split geometry/CFR near-duplicate audit, reporting review candidates rather than proven leakage. G7.14 adds inventory-driven valid/unavailable target-coverage accounting and an UMCD target-map bridge. All are component/pilot evidence and do not complete their respective gates. No public dataset or RT production corpus has been created; RT production generation remains gated.
**Edition 3.469 G5.14 SRS cyclic-shift normalization correction (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Corrected the type-1 low-PAPR sequence helper to use an explicit cyclic-shift count and added the TS 38.211 SRS comb mapping `{2: 8, 4: 12, 8: 6}`. Regression definitions now compare the normalized shift phase against an independent scalar expression and cover invalid comb/count inputs. SRS hopping, multiport scheduling, complete configuration derivation, and independent standards validation remain open. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_TS/138200_138299/138211/19.01.00_60/ts_138211v190100p.pdf), clauses 5.2.2 and 6.4.1.4.2.
**Edition 3.470 G5.14 comb-bound SRS sequence entry point (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added `nr_srs_sequence_type1`, which derives the TS cyclic-shift count from transmission comb and prevents inconsistent caller-supplied counts. Regression definitions cover wrapper equivalence and out-of-range cyclic-shift indices. This remains a bounded sequence primitive; hopping and higher-layer configuration derivation remain open.
**Edition 3.471 G5.14 per-symbol SRS group-hopping primitive (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added the TS 38.211 Gold sequence recurrence and an SRS group-hopping index helper keyed by explicit frame-local OFDM-symbol position. Regression definitions compare against a scalar recurrence at first, middle, and late frame positions and reject invalid identities/positions. Resource scheduling, sequence hopping, cyclic-shift hopping, multiport behavior, and independent standards conformance remain open. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_TS/138200_138299/138211/19.01.00_60/ts_138211v190100p.pdf), clauses 5.2.1 and 6.4.1.4.2.
**Edition 3.472 G5.14 SRS sequence boundary and length-30 support (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Corrected the type-1 base-sequence availability rule so `v=1` is rejected below length 72, and added the clause 5.2.2.2 length-30 sequence formula with independent scalar regression definitions. The tabulated phase sequences for lengths 6, 12, 18, and 24 remain unimplemented; no full SRS conformance is claimed.
**Edition 3.473 G5.14 SRS group-hopping sequence integration (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a bounded sequence entry point that resolves `neither` and per-symbol `groupHopping` modes into a type-1 SRS sequence. It fails explicitly for `sequenceHopping`, whose eligibility and index derivation remain open. Regression definitions cover both implemented modes and the explicit unsupported mode.
**Edition 3.476 G3.03 deterministic evaluator-output binding (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The acquisition-package verifier now recomputes the complete current v2 material-calibration report from the exact bound scorer input and rejects altered metrics or status fields even when inventory hashes and input-hash echoes are updated. The synthetic v0.3 package fixture now uses the real evaluator, with an adversarial metric-tampering regression definition. Legacy v0.2/v1 outputs remain structural-only; evaluator recomputation does not establish measurement provenance or scientific qualification.
**Edition 3.477 G5.14 standardized short SRS sequences (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added TS 38.211 V19.2.0 phase tables for type-1 low-PAPR sequence lengths 6, 12, 18, and 24, completing the short-length table branch alongside the existing length-30 and prime-root formulas. Regression definitions compare representative rows with independent table values; the test file remains unrun in the consolidated-validation workflow. Configuration derivation, sequence hopping, multiport scheduling, and independent conformance remain open.
**Edition 3.478 G5.14 prime-root rounding correction (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Corrected the type-1 prime-root index to apply the TS 38.211 nearest-integer rule for `q = floor(q̄ + 1/2) + v(-1)^floor(2q̄)`. Updated the independent scalar regression expression; the existing v=1 length-72 case exercises a group where rounding changes the root. This is a sequence-level correction and does not establish full SRS conformance.
**Edition 3.479 G5.14 SRS slot/resource-symbol hopping indices (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Replaced the earlier absolute-symbol group-hopping index with the TS 38.211 radio-frame-local slot index and SRS-resource-local symbol ordinal; added the 12-symbol extended-CP slot option. Implemented sequence hopping from the Gold bit at the clause-defined slot/resource-symbol offset, with `v=0` below `M_ZC=72`. Scalar Gold-recurrence regressions cover group and sequence hopping, both slot lengths, and the eligibility boundary. These are authored but unrun; cyclic-shift hopping, complete resource scheduling/configuration derivation, and independent conformance remain open.
**Edition 3.474 G7.08 RT coordinate identity consistency (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The lineage adapter now requires RT split metadata to carry a finite recorded `rx` and, for complete identities, requires exact agreement with `split_positions_m` before deriving spatial links. Regression definitions cover conflicting physical and split coordinates, and input fixtures now carry both representations. This checks internal metadata consistency only; inventory completeness, external provenance, and signed release qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.475 G4.07 joint delay/four-angle family authored (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a 100-claim, five-profile family comparing normalized-delay/four-angle characteristic moments against the pinned CDL-A–E tables using 8,192 independent draws/profile. The test and seed-collision protocol are authored but unrun; this finite family does not qualify the complete delay/angular law or physical RT.
**Edition 3.480 G7.08 split-lineage evidence binding (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Spatial relation keys now bind exact buffer and position evidence; temporal keys bind exact half-open windows. Added a recomputing footprint audit and included its input-scoped digests in ChannelDataset lineage reports (schema v2). Regression definitions cover buffer sensitivity and altered-footprint rejection. No authoritative complete corpus has been audited or signed, so G7.08 and release qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.481 G7.08 footprint inventory in immutable split locks (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added split-registry v3 locks with the exact footprint audit digest, kept legacy v2 readable, and require an attached audit before freezing new spatial/time-linked locks. Added re-verification against the source footprint inventory and regression definitions for isolated-position changes and lock round-trip. The implementation is unvalidated; inventory authority/completeness and external signature remain release requirements.
**Edition 3.482 G5.14 per-symbol SRS sequence rows (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a bounded type-1 resource generator that emits hopping-resolved sequence rows for each SRS symbol and feeds the existing resource-grid mapper. Regression definitions cover all hopping modes, two-symbol placement, and both normal/extended-CP symbol counts. Frequency allocation, cyclic-shift hopping, multiport scheduling, and standards validation remain incomplete; no checks have run.
**Edition 3.483 G5.14 SRS hopping uses absolute slot start symbol (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Official TS 38.211 V19.2.0 shows both hopping indices depend on frame-local slot, absolute resource start symbol `l0`, and resource-local symbol `l'`; updated APIs, full-symbol sequence-row generation, and regression definitions accordingly. This corrects the prior ordinal-only offsets. No checks have run; frequency allocation, cyclic-shift hopping, and multiport scheduling remain open. [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.484 G7.08 saved-corpus signed split-lock re-audit (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a saved-ChannelDataset registry builder that retains complete footprints and binds the v3 audit into the lock. The lineage CLI can now verify a detached lock signature with an external trust file, compare exact parent roster, recompute footprints, and report hashes for the signed lock, signature, and keyring. Its schema v2 path explicitly excludes child-data coverage and remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`. Source-bound regression definitions include signed-lock success and changed isolated-footprint failure; no checks have run, and corpus authority/approval remain open.
**Edition 3.485 G5.09 retained-result input contract (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The ordered-impairment auditor now validates seed types/uniqueness, record objects, Boolean CRC vectors, finite CFO samples, and integer timing samples before recomputing retained summaries. Adversarial regression definitions include non-finite and overflow-scale CFO values. Frozen scientific criteria are unchanged; checks remain deferred.
**Edition 3.486 G5.14 RRC SRS periodicity choices (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added parsing for the TS 38.331 base periodicity CHOICE and Release 16 extension, with offset validation and extension precedence; the frequency-allocation helper now resolves periodic occasions from those fields. Regression definitions cover all period tiers, extension choices, invalid inputs, and integrated counter allocation. Full RRC messages, trigger/set selection, and positioning Hyper-SFN remain open.
**Edition 3.487 G5.14 aperiodic SRS target slots (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added target-slot resolution from the triggering DCI slot, optional RRC `slotOffset`, and the selected Release 17 available-slot-offset entry; enforced the ASN.1 value ranges and list/index contract. Regression definitions cover direct and selected offsets plus invalid inputs. DCI codepoint/resource-set interpretation remains caller-owned. References: [ETSI TS 38.331 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138300_138399/138331/19.01.00_60/ts_138331v190100p.pdf), [ETSI TS 38.214 V19.2.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138214/19.02.00_60/ts_138214v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.509 G5.09 ordered-impairment DFT-estimator path (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added opt-in finite-delay DFT estimation to the independent ordered-impairment CPU runner, estimator hash binding and auditor replay/source checks, separate v9/v10 schemas, CLI selection, and a fresh-channel protocol. Existing Sionna default-LS reports and frozen thresholds remain unchanged. Seed collision screening, experiment, and validation are pending; no qualification claim is made.
**Edition 3.510 G5.14 SRS cyclic-shift hopping integration (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Integrated TS 38.211 V19.2.0 `f_csh` derivation into single- and multi-port type-1 SRS sequence rows. Each resource-local symbol uses its frame-local radio-frame/slot/start-symbol coordinate and the configured hopping ID, subset, and granularity. Explicit offsets remain supported with range checks. Regression definitions compare against an independent scalar Gold recurrence and cover row/multiport propagation and invalid combinations. Higher-layer RRC decoding, positioning-report integration, independent mapping/standards validation, and consolidated checks remain open. [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.511 G7.08 signed child-binding roster re-audit (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Extended the saved ChannelDataset lineage auditor to accept one strict, bounded JSON child-binding roster per child kind in a signed split-registry v3. It checks complete child-kind coverage and exact correspondence to the frozen child bindings, records each roster's hash and size, and retains `NOT_QUALIFIED`; report fields explicitly distinguish binding-roster verification from child-artifact auditing/existence. Added the versioned roster schema and CLI parser regression definitions. Child artifact bytes, row coverage, and provenance are still not verified; complete-corpus authority/review and consolidated validation remain open.
**Edition 3.512 G5.14 eight-port SRS TDM mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a resource-local port-activity resolver for TS 38.211's `ports8tdm` pattern, a configured-port sequence entry point that composes the existing standardized cyclic-shift resolver, and multiport ordinary/positioning SRS grid masking that leaves inactive port-symbol REs zero and unoccupied. Both mappers require the complete ordered eight-port roster and retain eight-port power normalization. Regression definitions cover both symbol parities, derived sequences, both grid types, mapped activity masks, and inactive-grid zeros. Complete RRC decoding, scheduling constraints, independent standards validation, and consolidated checks remain open. [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.513 G5.14 decoded RRC SRS choice adapters (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added strict adapters for the TS 38.331 decoded `transmissionComb` CHOICE for ordinary and `-r16` positioning resources, preserving comb offset and cyclic shift as a validated configuration object. Added decoded port-count enum resolution including default `port1`, Release 19 `ports8`/`ports8tdm`, and the prohibition on combining `ports8tdm` with cyclic-shift or comb-offset hopping. Regression definitions cover supported alternatives, malformed choices, precedence of the eight-port enum, and incompatible hopping flags. Full ASN.1 message decode, other RRC alternatives/extensions, end-to-end scheduled reception, and consolidated validation remain open. [ETSI TS 38.331 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138300_138399/138331/19.01.00_60/ts_138331v190100p.pdf).
**Edition 3.522 G7.08 split child artifact byte audit (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a per-kind artifact roster contract and `--split-child-artifact-roster KIND=PATH`. The auditor requires exact coverage of the signed registry's frozen child IDs, resolves canonical paths relative to the manifest directory, and streams SHA-256/size checks through no-follow, single-link file descriptors. Reports distinguish exact artifact-byte verification from semantic child auditing and remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`; artifact roster paths/hashes are self-declared and unsigned unless independently attested. Regression definitions cover the signed-registry round trip and post-roster tampering.
**Edition 3.523 G7.08 signed child artifact roster (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Child artifact rosters now require a detached OpenSSH signature in the dedicated `umcd-channel-child-artifact-roster-v1@github.com` namespace, checked against the configured external trust file and split-registry signer. The report records signer, namespace, keyring hash, signature hash, and the authenticated child-to-artifact byte audit. Regression definitions cover namespace-bound signing and roster-byte tampering. This authenticates roster declarations and verifies listed bytes; it does not parse child semantics, prove per-row coverage, or establish corpus completeness, so G7.08 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`.
