**Edition 3.521 G3.03 held-out angular measurement binding (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Each v0.3 held-out crosswalk row now identifies one acquisition angular-measurement record. The verifier requires that record to belong to the mapped acquisition sample and compares its coordinate frame, zenith/azimuth edges, array order, power semantics, measured integrated-power matrix, solid-angle cells, and missing-bin roster with the exact scorer-input row; angular records cannot be reused across rows. Regression definitions include an acquisition/evaluator power-matrix mismatch. This binds submitted values but does not authenticate acquisition bytes or qualify measured angular distributions.
**Edition 3.520 G3.03 sample-to-file association integrity (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Every file referenced by a sample must also list that sample in its inventory `sample_ids`, closing a one-way reference gap between measurement records and exact-byte file inventory. A regression definition removes the reciprocal sample association. This checks package metadata consistency only; it does not prove that the bytes contain the claimed measurement or establish provenance.
**Edition 3.519 G3.03 sample-to-campaign/instrument binding (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** v0.3 samples now require explicit campaign and instrument IDs. The verifier requires the instrument to belong to the named campaign, the acquisition time to fall within campaign dates, and both instrument configuration and campaign protocol carrier frequencies to match the sample. The v0.3 instrument schema requires a positive configured carrier frequency. Regression definitions cover campaign/instrument mismatch, frequency mismatch, and an out-of-window acquisition. These joins improve internal traceability but do not authenticate equipment configuration or measurement provenance; G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.518 G3.03 calibration evidence attachment (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** A sample must now reference every run-evidence file listed by its selected calibration record, in addition to being acquired inside that calibration's ordered validity interval. A regression definition covers a missing calibration run file. This strengthens package traceability links but does not authenticate the files, calibration result, reference plane, or clock; G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.517 G3.03 calibration time validity joins (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The v0.2/v0.3 package verifier now rejects reversed calibration validity intervals and any sample acquired outside the interval of its referenced calibration. Regression definitions cover both cases. This checks timestamp consistency only; calibration truth, reference-plane suitability, clock traceability, and external evidence remain unauthenticated, so G3 stays `NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.516 G3.03 hash-bound specimen material mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a versioned material-mapping JSON contract and exact-byte evaluator binding for v0.3 packages. The verifier checks the mapping ID against scorer input, maps every package specimen exactly once to a controlled evaluator material ID, checks the documented specimen claim and evidence-file references, and requires every held-out acquisition crosswalk material to match the mapped specimen. Adversarial regression definitions cover crosswalk/mapping disagreement. This provides internally consistent, hash-bound package metadata; it does not authenticate the material claim or evidence, establish physical identity, or prove provenance. Packages remain proposal-only/`NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.515 G3.03 acquisition-to-evaluator metadata joins (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The v0.3 package verifier now checks sample polarization, incidence angle, carrier frequency, and calibration against the bound scorer row/input, and requires each sample specimen to agree with its acquisition unit's specimen. Regression definitions cover each mismatch. These joins prevent metadata-inconsistent records from entering the held-out row crosswalk but do not establish that a free-text specimen material claim is correctly mapped to the evaluator's material ID; the package lacks an authenticated canonical material-mapping artifact. Acquisition authenticity, physical independence, representativeness, and external attestations remain unverified; the package stays proposal-only/`NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.514 G3.03 held-out acquisition crosswalk uniqueness (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The acquisition-package verifier now rejects a held-out `acquisition_sample_id` mapped to multiple evaluator rows, preventing an identical acquisition sample from being counted repeatedly through distinct evaluator IDs. A regression definition covers duplicate crosswalk rows. This establishes an input-scoped uniqueness invariant only; acquisition identity, physical-unit independence, representative sampling, and external attestation remain unverified, and the package remains proposal-only/`NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.534 G5.14 DCI 0_3 scheduled-cell selection adapter (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a strict adapter for already-decoded Release 19 `mc-DCI-SetofCellsToAddModList` cell-set and `scheduledCellComboListDCI-0-3` values. It orders cell sets by `setOfCellsId`, resolves the optional set indicator and optional combo-row indicator, maps each ASN.1 combo index through the ordered `scheduledCellListDCI-0-3`, checks list/table bounds and cross-set cell uniqueness, and composes the result into the multi-cell aperiodic SRS scheduler. The wrapper scopes each `SRS-RequestCombo` row and resource-set mapping to the selected RRC cell set, while checking its configured cell order against the wider serving-cell roster. Regression definitions cover ordering, implicit single-set/single-row selection, invalid indexes/configurations, scheduler integration, and multi-set request-row scoping. It does not parse DCI bits or ASN.1 PER, resolve per-cell BWP indicators/offset eligibility, or prove timing feasibility; no validation has run. References: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf), [ETSI TS 38.331 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138300_138399/138331/19.01.00_60/ts_138331v190100p.pdf).
**Edition 3.535 G5.14 DCI 0_3 UL BWP indicator resolver (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported per-cell resolver that derives the shared 0/1/2-bit field width from the maximum configured noninitial UL-BWP count across the complete scheduled-cell list. It maps up to three noninitial BWPs by each cell's ascending configured BWP IDs and applies Table 7.3.1.1.2-1 for four noninitial BWPs (codepoints 0..3 map to BWP IDs 1..4). Per-cell codepoints with no matching configured BWP fall back to that cell's active BWP; the resolver also records when the field is absent, a cell has only one BWP, or the UE does not support DCI-driven BWP changes. Regression definitions cover normal and table mappings, unavailable codepoints, unsupported changes, and the zero-bit case. This helper is not yet connected to per-BWP SRS resource-set lookup or returned schedule provenance; no validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4 and Table 7.3.1.1.2-1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.536 G5.14 DCI 0_3 BWP-aware SRS scheduling (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a BWP-aware composition path that applies the per-cell DCI indicator result to each cell's configured per-BWP SRS resource sets, then passes only the selected BWP resources through cell-combination, request-list, trigger, and timing resolution. It derives the `SRS-OffsetCombo` cell roster from cells with multiple available offsets on any configured UL BWP and carries each selected UL BWP ID in returned schedule rows, including cells with no triggered set. Regression definitions cover distinct per-cell BWP-ID lists, per-BWP resource selection, offset-roster derivation, and returned BWP lineage. Raw DCI/PER and RRC decoding, detailed scheduling-feasibility rules, positioning-report integration, end-to-end receive observables, and all validation remain open. [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf), [ETSI TS 38.331 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138300_138399/138331/19.01.00_60/ts_138331v190100p.pdf).
**Edition 3.537 G5.14 mapped single-port SRS channel observation (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported LS estimator that consumes an ordinary, hopped, or positioning single-port mapped SRS grid and a receive grid on matching slot/subcarrier coordinates. It divides received by transmitted complex values only on occupied REs and returns immutable per-RE channel estimates with the exact mask and symbol roster; it does not interpolate, average across time/frequency, estimate noise, or separate multiple ports. Regression definitions cover frequency-selective ordinary SRS, hopped SRS, mask preservation, immutability, and invalid receive grids. This closes the mapped single-port observation primitive only; full synchronization/impairment handling, multiport separation, positioning reports, and consolidated validation remain open.
**Edition 3.538 G3 frozen qualification-run contract (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a strict, hash-bound preregistration contract for the joint G3 acceptance family and exact path-equivalence scenario roster; task/scenario scope; candidate/challenger configs and hashes; waveform; link-budget/noise settings; path-matching tolerances; branch-specific sampling units and planned counts; bootstrap seed/draws; solver caps and overflow policy; and repository/source identity. Integrity verification explicitly remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`, and the schema makes clear that finite solver caps are not ground truth. Regression definitions cover roster drift, missing link-budget terms, contract tampering, and nonfinite tolerances. Contract validation is not yet connected to actual qualification-run launch or the numerical run receipt; no validation was run and no G3 gate or production-generation decision changes.
**Edition 3.539 G3 contract-to-run-receipt binding (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The joint numerical run-manifest builder now optionally embeds a verified frozen qualification contract and rejects mismatched acceptance families, candidate/challenger receiver IDs, or exact path-equivalence scenario rosters against the captured evaluator input. The contract builder/schema were added to the manifest's fixed evaluator source roster, and verification reports the contract digest/binding while retaining `NOT_QUALIFIED`; omission remains available for diagnostic runs. This binds preregistration to the replay receipt but does not enforce future experiment launch policy, authenticate source/runtime/measurement provenance, or establish external authority. Regression definitions are authored and unrun.
**Edition 3.540 G3 preregistration source identity (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a read-only source-inventory API and `--print-source-bundle` CLI path so the exact fixed evaluator source roster and digest can be recorded before an experiment. The receipt builder and verifier now require that a supplied contract's source-bundle digest match the sources actually hashed into the receipt. The acquisition/evaluator evidence remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`; source identity is integrity metadata, not proof of runtime identity, external trust, or measurement provenance. Regression definitions are authored and unrun.
**Edition 3.541 G3 run scope and identity joins (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Strengthened the frozen contract to require the run receipt ID to match the preregistered run ID and the declared task scenario/condition scope to cover the family's exact path scenarios. Builder and verifier reject run-ID, scenario-scope, receiver-ID, acceptance-family, path-roster, and source-bundle mismatches. These are preregistration integrity joins only; waveform/link-budget execution, independent sampling truth, actual launch enforcement, external authority, and qualification remain open. Regression definitions are authored and unrun.
**Edition 3.542 Contract-bound G3 evaluation entry point (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added `run_g3_frozen_qualification_evaluation` and the `evaluate-preregistered` CLI path. It reads canonical input/contract bytes, validates the contract schema and digest, requires a current source-bundle match, joins run ID/family/receiver IDs/path roster before invoking scorer branches, writes the numerical artifact and embedded-contract replay receipt, and reports `NOT_QUALIFIED`. The existing `evaluate` path remains diagnostic. This does not bind declared waveform/link-budget settings to actual receiver execution, establish external source/measurement authority, or qualify science. Regression definitions cover successful receipt creation and rejection before scorer execution; no checks were run.
**Edition 3.543 G3 contract-to-evaluator execution-context binding (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added joint evaluator input v2 with hash-bound candidate/challenger configurations, waveform configuration, and link-budget/noise declarations. Contract-bound execution requires exact equality between those declarations and the frozen contract before invoking scorers; each receiver-impact record carries the digest of that context, and input validation rejects configuration-hash or row-binding drift. Regression definitions cover candidate/challenger hash mismatch, missing/mismatched row binding, and waveform/link-budget drift rejected before scoring. This establishes integrity of declared run inputs and the replayed numerical artifact; it does not independently attest that an external receiver used those settings, authenticate runtime/source/measurement provenance, or qualify G3. Validation has not run and results remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.544 G3 per-stratum preregistration counts (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Upgraded the frozen qualification contract to v2. Planned counts are now attached to every exact material, CSI, propagation, receiver, and path-equivalence stratum in the shared acceptance family; branch totals must equal the per-stratum sum, and the path-equivalence roster binds one physical episode per row. The contract enforces the evaluator/pilot executable floors (material 20, complex CSI 2, propagation 2, receiver 20 per stratum) while explicitly labeling them as non-powered floors; sample-size assurance still requires a separately justified design. Regression definitions cover below-floor counts, omitted strata, total mismatch, and path-episode count mismatch. No checks have run; G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.545 G5.14 multiport SRS receiver observation (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a multiport LS estimator for mapped ordinary, hopped, and positioning SRS grids. It estimates the per-subcarrier TX-port channel only when the transmitted symbol signatures are full rank, retains unoccupied and rank-deficient subcarriers with explicit reason codes, and reports conditional LS noise covariance from caller-supplied per-RX complex-noise variance. The model assumes a constant channel over the SRS symbols and independent white complex noise; synchronization errors, channel evolution, and interference are not modeled. Regression definitions cover frequency-selective multiport recovery, covariance, rank deficiency, and invalid receive/noise inputs. No checks have run; this is an observation primitive, not SRS/receiver qualification.
**Edition 3.544 G7/G8 target coverage independent-group counts (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** `TargetRecord` can carry a leakage-component ID, and the UMCD corpus audit supplies the frozen registry component for each parent episode. The target coverage report now counts distinct components per split × family × target alongside element coverage, so several correlated rows in one component do not inflate the independent-group count. The corpus report schema advances to v3. Regression definitions cover repeated records in one component and distinct components. These counts are only as trustworthy as the supplied leakage relations and frozen inventory; no minimum independent-group criterion or broad G8 qualification is implied. Validation has not run.
**Edition 3.545 G7 frozen target-cell independent-group minimum (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Corpus-audit inventory v2 requires `minimum_independent_groups` on every expected split × family × target cell. The auditor compares this preregistered threshold with distinct component IDs derived from the frozen split registry and fails closed below the threshold, so correlated rows cannot satisfy it. The report records both observed and required component counts. Regression definitions include multiple rows sharing one component and threshold failure. This verifies declared inventory support only; it does not establish scientifically appropriate thresholds, complete leakage relations, or release qualification. Validation has not run.
**Edition 3.546 G7 corpus-audit inventory v2 schema enforcement (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a strict Draft 2020-12 JSON Schema for the frozen corpus inventory, covering parent bundle rows and leakage relations, target-cell support minimums, split-lock metadata, legal/external attestation fields, and the optional paired NR trajectory inventory fields. The auditor validates every inventory against this schema before reading its declared artifacts. Regression definitions cover schema-valid fixtures and omission of a required target support minimum. This enforces document structure only; hashes, signatures, source authority, and scientific eligibility remain separate checks. Validation has not run.
**Edition 3.547 G3 signed joint-run receipt verification (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added detached OpenSSH signature verification for exact captured joint run-manifest bytes in namespace `loslab-g3-joint-run-manifest-v1@github.com`, using an externally supplied allowed-signers file. The report records signer principal, namespace, signature digest, and trust-file digest. The signature verifier joins the fixed evaluator source roster. Regression definitions cover valid signature replay and rejection after manifest-byte tampering. This authenticates bytes under the supplied trust file only; it does not establish trust-file custody, reviewer-role authorization, statement truth, or scientific qualification, and reports stay `NOT_QUALIFIED`. Validation has not run.
**Edition 3.548 G3 run-manifest v2 dependency identity (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** New run-manifest v2 source bundles include exact `pyproject.toml` and `uv.lock` bytes alongside evaluator sources, binding declared and locked dependency versions into preregistration and numerical receipts. The v1 schema and 19-file source roster remain supported for historical receipts. Regression definitions cover lockfile drift and v1 replay compatibility. Installed package inventory and loaded runtime/binary identity remain unverified; receipts stay `NOT_QUALIFIED` and no validation has run.
**Edition 3.549 G5.14 DCI 0_3 selected-BWP SRS frequency bounds (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The BWP-aware multi-cell aperiodic SRS scheduler now requires a global-subcarrier start/size for each configured UL BWP, checks every scheduled allocation against the DCI-selected BWP extent, and retains that extent in the returned cell schedule. Regression definitions cover allocation underflow and overflow at the selected BWP bounds. This does not establish inter-cell collision freedom or complete timing feasibility; no tests or static checks have run.
**Edition 3.550 G3 signed split-preregistration subject binding (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** `split_preregistration` attestation verification now requires its exact package-file subject roster to include the evaluator-bound split-manifest file. Package consistency joins those inventoried bytes to the manifest embedded in scorer input. An adversarial regression definition re-signs a package whose split attestation omits that file and expects rejection. This checks which bytes the signer covered; it does not prove preregistration timing, claim truth, or scientific adequacy. Validation has not run and G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`.

**Edition 3.551 G3 joint scene/condition roster consistency (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Before invoking evaluator branches, the joint evaluator now requires complex-CSI, propagation, receiver-impact, and path-equivalence rosters to project to the same scene-family × LOS/NLOS cells, and checks the path episode roster against family v4. This blocks a hash-valid family from silently declaring different scene/condition coverage across branches. Observed episode checks were added in edition 3.552. No measurement truth, independent sampling, external trust, or G3 qualification is established. G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`; production RT remains gated.

**Edition 3.552 G3 receiver episode identity and contract-bound input v3 (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an input-v3 schema requiring receiver-impact rows to carry the scene family, LOS/NLOS condition, and preregistered episode ID alongside the execution-context digest. The joint evaluator now compares observed receiver episodes with the path roster, and contract-bound evaluation requires v3; v1/v2 remain diagnostic replay formats. The run-manifest source bundle includes and validates the v3 schema. Regression definitions cover receiver episode drift and rejection of v2 for new contract-bound runs. This closes cross-branch row identity only; it does not establish receiver runtime truth, material-measurement coverage, provenance, or scientific qualification. G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`; production RT remains gated.

**Edition 3.553 G7 signed multi-artifact child roster v2 (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a typed v2 artifact roster that can bind multiple artifacts to each frozen split child, while requiring at least one ChannelDataset NPZ per child. The audit verifies bytes and SHA-256 for every declared artifact and additionally checks every NPZ row against the frozen child, kind, and parent; supplemental types are explicitly byte-only until semantic adapters exist. The audit report advances to bundle v6 and records artifact counts/types and byte-only counts. Regression definitions cover multi-artifact children and missing primary NPZs. This is signed-roster-scoped evidence, not proof the roster itself is complete or institutionally authoritative; external trust, full semantic coverage, and public release remain open.

**Edition 3.554 G7/G8 stratified target inventory and exact-byte admission (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added corpus-audit inventory v3 while retaining v2 reads. Expected coverage can now be frozen by split, source family, scene, declared carrier-frequency band, derived network-topology digest, derived antenna-configuration digest, task, and target. Scene IDs join the exact split-registry relation; band declarations are checked against frozen half-open frequency intervals; topology and antenna digests are derived from the episode; and target/task bindings must cover the exact observed target roster. Optional detached OpenSSH verification authenticates the exact inventory bytes in a dedicated namespace and reports signer/trust digests, while the audit remains nonqualifying. Regression definitions cover strata, band mismatch, valid signature, and post-signing edits. No checks have run; signer custody, authoritative inventory completeness, scientific thresholds, and release qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.555 G5 multi-resource PRS grid composition (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added bounded composition for multiple caller-resolved downlink PRS resources in one slot. The composer preserves resource IDs and member masks, sums contributions on shared resource elements, reports their collision mask/count, and checks resource-count and total-grid-cell budgets before stack allocation. Single-resource mapping now accepts an exact reserved-RE mask in addition to the coarse reserved-symbol mask. Regression definitions cover disjoint resources, overlapping resources, allocation rejection, and exact reserved-RE behavior. RRC decoding, occasion/muting orchestration, SSB collision integration, antenna/beam mapping, wider timing-channel checks, and receiver qualification remain open; this software path does not produce qualification evidence.

**Edition 3.556 G3 receiver-row configuration binding (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Joint evaluator input v4 requires each receiver-impact row to identify the candidate/challenger configuration and carry its exact configuration digest. Contract-bound preflight and run-manifest replay require v4 and compare each row with the frozen contract before scorer execution; inputs v1-v3 remain available for diagnostic replay. The v4 schema, source-bundle roster, and regression definitions cover valid bindings and mismatch/rejection paths. No tests or static checks have run. This establishes declared row/configuration lineage, not proof that an external receiver executed the declared configuration; G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED` and production RT remains gated.

**Edition 3.557 G3 path-coverage contract consistency (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Frozen-contract construction now rejects a bidirectional coverage minimum that differs from the shared acceptance-family threshold. The path-roster evaluator checks each episode against the same 95% criterion in both directions, so a preregistered contract cannot silently declare a looser or stricter value than the scorer applies. Regression definitions cover both sides of the mismatch. No checks have run. This resolves threshold declaration drift only; the path branch still accepts a supplied comparison object and does not recompute it from raw solver-valid path records. G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED` and production RT remains gated.

**Edition 3.558 G3 path-comparison contract binding (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Contract-bound v4 path rows must declare candidate/challenger configuration IDs, delay tolerance, and coverage criterion; preflight rejects drift from the frozen contract before scorer execution. The matcher’s empty-path diagnostic now retains its configuration block so that result is bindable too. Regression definitions cover schema omissions, contract mismatch, and empty-path metadata. No checks have run. Caller-supplied comparisons are still not recomputed from raw solver-valid path records; this does not establish path truth or G3 qualification.

**Edition 3.559 G7 signed child-artifact subtree inventory (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added child-artifact roster v3 with a signed, single-directory `artifact_root`; the audit enumerates that subtree without following links and requires exact equality between its files and the declared roster. Hard links and special entries are rejected, and file-count/depth bounds are enforced. Child audit and outer split-lineage report advance to v4 and bundle v7. Regression definitions cover exact inventory and an unlisted artifact. This proves completeness only relative to the roster's declared subtree, not completeness of the authoritative release inventory or signer trust. Validation has not run; production release remains gated.

**Edition 3.560 G5.09 paired independent-channel SNR curve implementation (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a bounded diagnostic SNR-grid entry point to the independent ordered-impairment PUSCH runner. Each point reuses channel, payload, oscillator, and standardized AWGN random streams; the noise variance follows the repository's unit-reference convention and the measured transmit waveform power is recorded. The whole curve is capped at 16,384 transport blocks, uses a versioned schema, and is hash-bound to its schema/source. A curve auditor checks the shared roster, noise/SNR binding, retained per-point CRC totals, and recomputes channel-cluster pointwise intervals plus a simultaneous max-deviation band over the three receiver conditions and complete SNR grid. The output is explicitly diagnostic-only: the frozen single-point G5.09 criteria are not transferred across the grid, and receiver qualification is not claimed. Changing the runner changes its source digest; existing source-bound G5.09 artifacts remain historical and must not have hashes refreshed or be replayed as new confirmation. Regression definitions are authored; no tests or native runs have been made.

**Edition 3.561 G4 independent CDL metadata scalars (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a compact scalar reference derived from the hash-pinned ETSI TR 38.901 v19.2.0 CDL-A–E tables and CPU regression definitions for LOS status, component/output-cluster counts, normalized delay summary, angle spreads, XPR, and LOS K factor. The reference retains no full CDL tables and supports selected metadata checks only; per-tap normative references and full statistical qualification remain open. No tests have run.

**Edition 3.563 G7 immutable-store retry and directory durability semantics (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Episode, NR-record, and NR-trajectory immutable publishers now inspect existing idempotent-retry destinations using bounded stable single-link reads, reject byte conflicts explicitly, and fsync the destination directory after publication on supported POSIX systems. Path-based NR record-manifest reads now use the bounded 1 MiB stable reader used by inventory scans and reject duplicate JSON keys. Regression definitions inject failure before each store's final manifest publication and require a successful retry and round-trip without leaked temporary files; a record-manifest size-bound case is also defined. NR trajectory member records remain individually committed and can be left as valid standalone records if trajectory-index publication fails; this is not a multi-object transaction. Full corruption/failure coverage and measured end-to-end read/write memory remain open. No tests or static checks have run.

**Edition 3.564 G7 interrupted episode object publication retry regression (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a failure-injection regression definition that interrupts an episode bundle after one immutable layer has been published but before the next layer, asserts that no bundle manifest was committed, then retries the same content-addressed bundle and checks the complete readback and temporary-file cleanup. This covers one mid-object interruption point for the UMCD episode store only; equivalent NR and format-wide corruption/failure matrices plus measured full-path memory remain open. No tests or static checks have run.

**Edition 3.565 G7 partial NR trajectory record publication retry regression (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a failure-injection regression definition that stops a two-record trajectory after its first NR record and manifest are committed but before the second record object is published. It asserts that no trajectory index exists, then retries and requires the complete ordered trajectory to read back with no leaked temporary files. The writer remains intentionally manifest-last rather than transactional across records; partial first-record artifacts are valid immutable store entries. Other serialization formats, systematic corruption/failure coverage, and measured full-path memory remain open. No tests or static checks have run.

**Edition 3.593 G7 target empty-value semantics (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Canonical `TargetValue` rejects zero-element arrays, which otherwise carry no element on which to record an availability mask or reason. A no-path/unavailable result must use the declared target shape, zero storage for masked elements, and explicit reason codes. A regression definition covers empty-array rejection. This does not complete domain-specific target units, gauges, semantic ranges, or shape policies. No tests or static checks have run.

**Edition 3.594 G5 SNR-curve schema accepts current receiver report versions (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Updated the paired diagnostic SNR-curve schema to accept current per-point independent-channel report versions v19 and v20, which the producer emits when retaining the latest waveform evidence. Added schema regression definitions for both versions. The curve remains diagnostic-only; no receiver qualification or validation pass is implied.

**Edition 3.595 G4 CDL-D LOS temporal reference uses independent ETSI rows (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Rebased the native CDL-D LOS-plus-diffuse Doppler covariance expectation on the byte-pinned ETSI-derived tap/angle fixture, including separate specular and diffuse powers, AoA/ZoA, and cluster spreads. The ETSI source digest and exact fixture bytes are pinned; the Sionna CDL-D table digest is retained only as generator provenance. Confidence criteria and configuration are unchanged. The result remains a bounded model-consistency check, not general or measurement qualification; no tests have run.

**Edition 3.596 G4 CDL-D/E LOS delay-angular and complex-mean references use independent ETSI rows (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Rebased the native delay/angular-moment and coherent-LOS complex-mean/diffuse-variance regressions on the byte-pinned ETSI-derived CDL component fixture. The reference calculation checks the specular-first/diffuse-following roster and zero-delay diffuse component before composing the first tap; the native Sionna table digest remains implementation provenance only. Existing familywise confidence criteria are unchanged. These remain bounded model-consistency checks, not general or measurement qualification; no tests have run.

**Edition 3.597 G4 CDL angular moments, diffuse-ray geometry, and CDL-E temporal reference use independent ETSI rows (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Rebased NLOS CDL-A–C delay/angular-moment expectations, A–E diffuse-ray power/angle geometry, and CDL-E LOS temporal covariance on the byte-pinned ETSI-derived component fixture. Sionna package-table hashes remain generator provenance where asserted, not expectation inputs. Existing native configurations and confidence rules are unchanged. These remain bounded model-consistency checks; no tests have run and broader G4 qualification remains open.

**Edition 3.598 G4 CDL-D two-port LOS/diffuse spatial covariance uses independent ETSI rows (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Rebased the deterministic LOS steering and diffuse ray-pairing expectation on byte-pinned ETSI-derived CDL-D powers and AoA/ZoA rows with cluster spreads. The installed Sionna table digest remains checked only as generator provenance. The two-element configuration and simultaneous confidence criteria are unchanged; this is a bounded model-consistency check, not external channel qualification, and no tests have run.

**Edition 3.599 G4 NLOS spatial covariance references use independent ETSI rows (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Rebased the CDL-A/B base two-port covariance and the CDL-A/B/C three-orientation covariance expectations on byte-pinned ETSI-derived powers, angles, cluster spreads, and XPR. Packaged Sionna table digests are checked only as generator provenance. Existing array/orientation configurations and simultaneous confidence criteria are unchanged; these are bounded model-consistency checks, no tests have run, and broader array qualification remains open.

**Edition 3.600 G4 Core-1 CDL receive covariance uses independent ETSI profile values (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Rebased the CDL-A–E receive-array covariance expectation on byte-pinned ETSI-derived powers, LOS components, four-angle values, spreads, and XPR. Sionna CDL table digests remain generator provenance. The receive-array topologies and familywise criteria are unchanged. This closes the shared profile-input gap for this Core-1 branch, not the separate transmitter/joint covariance or physical-channel claims; no tests have run.

**Edition 3.601 G4 CDL-A four-element spatial covariance uses independent ETSI rows (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Rebased the 4×4 receive-array covariance expectation on byte-pinned ETSI-derived CDL-A powers, AoA/ZoA, and spreads. The installed Sionna table digest remains generator provenance. Array geometry and simultaneous confidence criteria are unchanged; this is a bounded model-consistency check, no tests have run.

**Edition 3.602 G4 CDL-A dual-polarization trace uses independent ETSI power/XPR values (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Rebased the dual-VH covariance-trace expectation on byte-pinned CDL-A component powers and XPR. The installed Sionna table digest remains generator provenance. The trace law and single-statistic confidence criterion are unchanged; this is bounded model consistency, not full polarization or channel qualification, and no tests have run.

**Edition 3.603 G4 four-element NLOS covariance extends to CDL-A/B/C (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Extended the existing 4×4 half-wavelength receive-array covariance regression from CDL-A to CDL-A/B/C. Each expected covariance is calculated from the byte-pinned ETSI-derived component powers, AoA/ZoA, and cluster spreads; the installed Sionna table digest is checked only as implementation provenance. The frozen 16-component familywise 99% interval and 0.05 normalized-power half-width criteria are unchanged. Regression definitions are authored; no tests or native runs have been made, and broad G4 qualification remains open.

**Edition 3.606 G4 four-element LOS covariance extends to CDL-D/E (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Extended the same 4×4 half-wavelength receive-array reference to all five profiles. CDL-A–C diffuse covariance is calculated from ETSI-derived cluster rows; CDL-D/E additionally add the separately normalized LOS steering outer product from the ETSI-derived specular angle. Sionna profile-table digests remain implementation provenance only. The simultaneous 16-component 99% confidence and 0.05 normalized-power half-width criteria are unchanged. Regression definitions are authored; no tests or native runs have been made, and broad G4 qualification remains open.

**Edition 3.608 G7 legacy RT split migration in candidate registry builder (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The candidate split-registry builder now invokes the canonical legacy RT identity migration only when every row is RT-sourced and none carries split identity fields. Migration requires a complete internally consistent saved asset closure; partial/current identities continue through normal lineage validation. Build-report v2 binds each input's exact original archive bytes and records the migration algorithm per input. Regression definitions cover successful migration and rejection before either output is published for an incomplete closure or partial identity. These self-declared saved assets are not authoritative scene provenance; reports remain `NOT_QUALIFIED` and no tests have run.

**Edition 3.620 G5.14 DCI 0_3 TDRA and frequency-hopping widths (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The decoded-configuration width resolver now derives the time-domain assignment width from the `tdra-FieldIndexListDCI-0-3` roster size and derives the frequency-hopping flag width from the complete scheduled-cell configuration (one bit if any listed cell enables hopping, otherwise zero). Regression definitions cover multiple TDRA entries, the single-entry zero-bit case, mixed hopping configuration, and the all-disabled case. Other DCI field widths, raw ASN.1/PER and CRC/polar decoding, standards conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. No validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.621 G1 NumPy loaded-scene geometry becomes the canonical RT visibility target (2026-10-27, focused software validation):** Static RT generation now extracts one loaded-scene triangle inventory, records its digest/object/triangle counts, and uses the NumPy query as the direct-visibility target. D1 request and run identity bind that geometry digest. Mitsuba finite-segment visibility and depth-zero PathSolver remain fail-closed cross-checks; ambiguous or disagreeing static candidates are skipped. Dynamic trajectories reuse one inventory for static geometry and re-extract it after each configured scene-object translation, recording per-frame digests and all three oracle states. Static and dynamic UMCD target descriptions now name the NumPy source. The static mocked RT regression and cache modules pass 16 tests; the dependency-isolated visibility modules pass 15 tests; Ruff check and compilation pass. The native screen-scene regression exceeded three minutes in LLVM/Sionna initialization without reaching the test and was stopped, so native qualification remains open. No production RT data were generated. A cache integrity regression also exposed that ZIP parsing preceded payload hash verification; the loader now verifies the digest before parsing. The narrow cache/visibility changes do not establish thin-wall or material-aware LOS, source-asset completeness, or G1 qualification.
**Edition 3.758 G4 TDL covariance and Doppler expectations use independent first-tap components (2026-10-26, focused validation):** Replaced adapter-derived first-tap power and K-factor in the Core-1 scalable TDL-A–E covariance, configured TDL receive-covariance, and scalable/fixed-profile Doppler expectations with component powers from the pinned TR 38.901 and TS 38.101-4 fixtures. The independent NumPy reference validates fixture identity/roster and RX correlation inputs, including D/E first-tap specular-plus-diffuse composition. Twenty-one CPU-only covariance/component tests plus the existing scalable PDP-reference tests pass (29 total); Ruff, compilation, and diff checks pass. Native Core-1 and Doppler cases remain deferred: the pytest venv lacks Torch/Sionna, while the system interpreter containing them lacks pytest. No G4 qualification or production RT authorization follows.
**Edition 3.759 G1 loaded-scene triangle inventory feeds the independent visibility oracle (2026-10-26, focused validation):** Added a duck-typed extractor for loaded Mitsuba/Sionna scene-object mesh buffers, including the loader-applied world transform, stable object ordering, metric scaling, topology checks, and a deterministic SHA-256 over exact world vertices/faces. A separate query API binds `visible`/`blocked`/`ambiguous` results to that inventory without calling Mitsuba ray intersection. Fifteen isolated geometry tests pass; Ruff and compilation pass. The bounded native screen-scene test was stopped after more than three minutes in Sionna/Mitsuba pytest initialization with no test output. This does not yet replace generation-time labels or model material transmission, and is not G1 or G3 qualification.

**Edition 3.760 G5 bounded sample-domain multi-link waveform composition (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added `compose_received_waveforms` for small-scale channel-filtered TX-to-RX contributions, with a flat validated per-link power-gain matrix, exact integer arrival offsets, capture-window clipping/padding, resource bounds, and coherent summation; the gain matrix accepts `NetworkLinkBudget.power_gain_linear` directly. `add_independent_receiver_waveform_awgn` adds reproducible receiver-specific noise. The APIs expose each link contribution and share one declared sample rate. The focused network-PHY module passes 46 tests with Ruff, formatting, compilation, and whitespace checks. This fills a sample-domain composition primitive only: it does not produce/calibrate each link, model frequency-selective path loss, fractional delay/CFO or asynchronous clocks, or qualify PUSCH receiver impact. G5 receiver qualification remains open and production RT/advanced training remain gated.


**Edition 3.761 G7 specialized profile-holdout feature lineage (2026-10-27, focused validation):** The profile-proxy baseline now builds and verifies a strict observation-only input contract over the audited `ChannelModelInputs.observed_cfr` field. It binds the exact normalized-CFR transform source, input/output shapes, full feature-matrix digest, and per-fold train/validation/test feature digests; the metadata-only profile oracle is explicitly outside the primary fit/prediction route. The runnable report carries this contract and is covered by focused regression tests. This closes one specialized experiment path only; the complete adapter, transform, training, and inference-artifact leakage audit remains open.

**Edition 3.762 G7 canonical experiment model-input lineage (2026-10-27, focused validation):** New model artifacts use strict contract v2, which records the transform implementation digest, exact full feature-matrix digest, and train/validation/test matrix digests. The canonical experiment runner verifies the hashes against the in-memory training and scoring matrices before completing the run; verification of v1 artifacts remains supported. The focused runner module passes 15 tests, with Ruff, formatting, compilation, and both v1/v2 schema checks passing. This closes the main runner path only: third-party inference adapters and the global model-input leakage audit remain open.

**Edition 3.763 G7/10 immutable build-node foundation (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added `artifacts.build_dag` and a strict build-node manifest schema. Each node validates configuration against its supplied JSON Schema and hashes the schema, resolved configuration, source revision/bundle, input files, dependency node keys, and declared outputs. Output objects are content-addressed and verified; dependencies are checked before execution; the final node manifest is published last. Seven focused tests pass with Ruff, compilation, schema, and diff checks. This foundation is not yet wired into the canonical dataset/receiver/evaluation workflows; the full end-to-end DAG remains open.
**Edition 3.764 G7/10 canonical experiment input materialization through the build DAG (2026-10-27, focused validation):** `run_experiment` now accepts an optional `build_cache_root`; when set, ordered input `ChannelDataset` files are validated and concatenated through the immutable build-node API. The run manifest binds the node key, manifest digest, materialized object digest, and cache-reuse result while retaining original input-file hashes. A cache replay regression confirms the same node/object and identical model feature-matrix identity across separate runs. The runner/profile-holdout modules pass 24 tests and the build-DAG module passes seven; Ruff, format, compilation, and diff checks pass. This connects one training-data preprocessing edge only; generation, receiver, evaluation, and paper-output workflows still need DAG integration.
**Edition 3.765 G5 per-link residual CFO in sample-domain waveform composition (2026-10-27, focused validation):** `compose_received_waveforms` accepts per-link `[rx, tx]` residual CFO and initial phase, applies the oscillator on the shared capture-time axis after integer alignment, and retains both matrices in the result contract. This includes phase accumulation during link arrival delay while keeping allocations bounded to one link's captured samples. The focused network-PHY module passes 55 tests with repository-wide coverage and unrelated Sionna RT initialization disabled; Ruff, formatting, compilation, and whitespace checks pass. It does not model fractional delay, phase-noise evolution, multi-numerology clocks, calibrated links, or establish receiver impact/qualification. Production RT and advanced training remain gated.

**Edition 3.766 G5 per-link fractional timing in sample-domain waveform composition (2026-10-27, focused validation):** `compose_received_waveforms` applies a bounded per-link fractional delay with a 33-tap Blackman-windowed sinc interpolator over only the captured source slice, retaining the delay and kernel radius in the result contract. A bandlimited-tone regression verifies fractional phase response after integer arrival alignment; invalid delay and shape/type cases fail closed. The network-PHY module passes 61 tests with coverage disabled and the unrelated Sionna RT conftest skipped; Ruff, formatting, compilation, and whitespace checks pass. Zero extension at source boundaries is explicit. This does not model phase noise, calibrated links, or qualify receiver impact; production RT and advanced training remain gated.

**Edition 3.767 G7 candidate split-registry build through immutable DAG (2026-10-27, focused validation):** The candidate-registry builder now accepts an optional cache root and binds the named input dataset hashes, exact split-policy bytes, spatial buffer, dataset-size ceiling, runtime versions, configuration schema, source bundle, and source revision in a content-addressed node. Registry/report artifacts are verified on reuse and copied to caller destinations through exclusive writes; the original non-DAG builder remains available. The report bytes retain the strict v2 schema and `NOT_QUALIFIED` status; cache identity is returned separately from the on-disk report. All 10 focused script tests pass, including exact output replay through a second output directory. This does not establish authoritative inventory, signer custody, or release qualification.

**Edition 3.768 G5 seeded node-shared phase noise in sample-domain composition (2026-10-27, focused validation):** `compose_received_waveforms` now accepts TX and RX oscillator linewidth vectors and a required seed when either is positive. It generates one reproducible Wiener phase trajectory per oscillator node and batch draw, shared across incident links and antenna ports, and applies the relative TX-minus-RX phase on the capture timeline. The result retains linewidths and seed; the memory guard includes oscillator state/work. The focused network-PHY module passes 71 tests with Ruff, formatting, compilation, and whitespace checks. This implements white-frequency-noise phase evolution only; PLL-shaped spectra, calibrated link generation, and end-to-end receiver impact remain open. G5 qualification and production RT/advanced-training gates remain closed.

**Edition 3.769 G5 receiver I/Q imbalance in sample-domain multi-link composition (2026-10-27, focused validation):** `compose_received_waveforms` now accepts per-receiver gain mismatch and quadrature-skew vectors and applies the memoryless receiver transformation to each channel-filtered link contribution before summation. This preserves source attribution and the exact linear sum; the contract retains both vectors and the resource bound includes impairment work. The focused network-PHY module passes 78 tests with Ruff, formatting, compilation, and whitespace checks. Transmitter I/Q imbalance remains an upstream-of-channel impairment, and this receiver model excludes DC offset, frequency-selective mismatch, and nonlinear RF effects; G5 receiver qualification remains open.

**Edition 3.770 UMCD artifact-scoped lineage status claims (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added typed claims for `proposed`, `synthetic_debug`, `native_verified`, `scenario_qualified`, `geometry_qualified`, `receiver_qualified`, `externally_calibrated`, and `external_tested` to UMCD run provenance v2. Claims are scoped and bind SHA-256 references; higher tiers require the plan-specific run, conformance, geometry, receiver, calibration, or external-test evidence roster. The canonical provenance digest covers claims, the episode store round-trips v2, and v1 records remain readable. Seven focused provenance/claim tests pass with Ruff, formatting, compilation, schema parsing, and diff checks. Claims are producer declarations: evidence-content validation, trusted review, automatic gate promotion, and generation-report propagation remain open.

**Edition 3.771 UMCD lineage evidence byte-roster audit (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added an optional bounded verifier that requires exact equality between claimed evidence digests and supplied immutable payloads, recomputes SHA-256, and emits a schema-validated report with status `EVIDENCE_BYTES_VERIFIED` and scientific status `NOT_QUALIFIED`. Proposed claims with no evidence are separately reported as `NO_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED`. Ten focused provenance/lineage tests pass, including missing/extra/tampered/oversized payload rejection and episode-store round-trip; Ruff, formatting, compilation, schema parsing, and diff checks pass. Payload acquisition remains caller-owned; this does not semantically inspect reports, verify signatures, establish independence, or authorize lineage promotion.

**Edition 3.772 UMCD lineage audit reads evidence files safely (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added direct file acquisition for the exact claimed evidence roster: each relative path is opened beneath a declared root through no-follow directory descriptors, and evidence files must be stable single-link regular files. SHA-256 is streamed under a total-byte ceiling without loading the artifact into memory. The report remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`; eleven focused provenance/lineage tests pass, including symlink, hardlink, path-traversal, tamper, roster, and size rejection. Evidence semantics, external signer/reviewer trust, and scientific admission remain separate requirements.

**Edition 3.773 Runnable UMCD lineage evidence audit (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added `loslab lineage-evidence-audit`, which consumes canonical provenance-v2 JSON plus a strict digest-to-relative-path map, invokes the stable evidence-file audit, binds report v2 to the provenance record digest, validates the report schema, and publishes the new report without replacement. Four workflow tests pass for successful CLI invocation, tampered evidence rejection before publication, legacy provenance rejection, and output immutability. This is byte-integrity workflow evidence only; semantic gate checks, external trust, and scientific promotion remain open.
**Edition 3.774 G8 split/family/class support and absent-class metrics (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added a frozen split × family × class support audit that preserves declared empty strata and absent classes, separately counts rows and distinct caller-declared independent groups, and rejects undeclared strata/labels or missing group identity. The legacy common experiment runner embeds this report in each result, records source family IDs in prediction artifacts, and validates the report against a versioned JSON Schema. Binary evaluation returns undefined (`null`) class-dependent metrics with explicit reasons when a required class is absent, while retaining defined quantities such as accuracy and Brier score. Eleven focused pytest cases and the runner integration test pass. A broader metrics-module run has one unrelated existing failure in `test_estimator_curve_comparison_rejects_unmatched_channel_truth`, where unsupported estimator metadata is rejected before the expected truth-mismatch check. Other model routes and caller-supplied physical-group validity remain unauthenticated; G8 remains open.

**Edition 3.775 G8 train-only probability calibration and prior adjustment (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added strict natural-target prior declarations with evidence digests, separate group-balanced Platt calibration, source-prior estimation from a calibration partition, frozen validation/test application, inverse threshold mapping for raw-score model interfaces, prediction artifacts retaining raw/calibrated/adjusted probabilities, and v2 model-input split binding for calibration indices. Signed split calibration rows are honored; unlocked pilot runs reserve a deterministic group-disjoint calibration subset. Versioned schemas are validated at runtime and the workflow limitations are documented in `docs/research/PROBABILITY_PRIOR_POLICY.md`. Nine prior/calibration tests, two runner/signed-split tests, and one CLI config test pass; Ruff, formatting, compilation, schema parsing, and diff checks pass. This pilot route assumes label shift and trusts caller-declared target-prior evidence and group identities; external prior evidence review, physical-independence proof, sealed-test controls, other model routes, and scientific calibration remain open. No production RT or advanced training is authorized.

**Edition 3.776 G8 low-confidence coverage audit (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added an optional config-bound margin around the validation-frozen classification threshold. The test-only descriptive report gives flagged row/group counts overall and by source family and domain, is schema-validated at runtime, and stores the exact Boolean mask beside retained predictions. Four direct evaluator tests, the legacy runner integration, and the CLI config test pass; Ruff passes. No test labels are used to select the margin or threshold, and no error-risk claim is made. Expected-population missingness/selection auditing and authoritative held-out site/material/antenna eligibility are still open because their required roster and identity contracts are not present.

**Edition 3.777 G8 expected-population and outcome accounting (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added a strict frozen sample roster with separate `included`, `excluded`, `rejected`, `no_path`, and `empty` dispositions; exact identity joins to observed ChannelDataset metadata/source/domain/group; expected-versus-observed family/domain counts; and a mismatch report for missing included rows, duplicates, unrostered rows, non-included observations, and identity drift. A bounded workflow reads stable input files, binds roster/dataset digests, validates schemas, and publishes without replacement; mismatch reports are retained and return exit status 2. Five evaluator tests and two workflow tests pass, with Ruff/format checks clean. This is consistency against a caller-declared roster and does not prove population completeness, reason truth, or physical independence. Low-confidence coverage is partially implemented; held-out site/material/antenna eligibility, authoritative inventories, and broader G8 remain open.

**Edition 3.778 G5.14 UL SRS-RSRP positioning measurement report (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added a typed measurement utility that averages calibrated per-RE powers in watts over an explicit SRS occasion/RE mask, reports each receiver branch, maps terrestrial and satellite receiver categories to the TS 38.215 reference points, and reports a bounded max-branch diversity combiner that is never below an individual branch. Zero received power remains an explicit diagnostic with null dBm values. A strict report schema is validated at runtime; seven tests pass. This implements the power averaging/report slice against TS 38.215 V19.2.0 clause 5.2.5 only. Calibration evidence and SRS mapping are caller supplied; UL-RTOA, Rx-Tx timing, AoA, path power/TDCT, end-to-end reception, and receiver qualification remain open. Production RT and advanced training stay gated.

**Edition 3.779 G5.14 SRS positioning timing report arithmetic (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added versioned reports for UL-RTOA reference-time arithmetic from SFN/subframe identity and gNB Rx-Tx time difference from caller-supplied TRP timestamps, with separate TS 38.104 receiver/transmitter reference-point mappings and digest-bound timing-calibration declarations. The 13 focused SRS measurement tests pass; targeted Ruff, formatting, compilation, schema, and diff checks pass. This does not detect first paths, verify timing acquisition/closest-downlink selection, or calibrate clocks. UL-AoA, path power/TDCT, synchronized reception, independent standards validation, and receiver qualification remain open. Production RT and advanced training stay gated.

**Edition 3.780 G7 canonical split identity admission (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Removed string coercion/whitespace trimming at the UMCD episode split builder and audit, plus CFR and temporal duplicate-audit boundaries. IDs must now be non-empty canonical strings, and temporal window boundaries must be finite real values rather than booleans or numeric strings. The focused UMCD split and duplicate-audit suites pass 21 tests; Ruff, formatting, and diff checks pass. The existing connected-component policy is unchanged; this prevents identity aliasing but does not prove the supplied groups exhaust physical/spatial/time leakage. Split authority and corpus qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.781 end-to-end resource-budget preflight (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added `loslab resource-budget` with strict request/report schemas and an immutable, digest-bound JSON workflow. Per-stratum pilot distributions require 10–100 unique measured scene/link conditions, source-measurement and configuration digests, and a runtime/hardware identity whose canonical digest is checked. Rows include solver, receiver, preprocessing, downstream time, GPU peak memory, and raw-path/CFR/waveform/manifest/other retained bytes. Empirical higher p50/p90/p99 projections use planned-unit counts and explicit 2×–3× headroom; device memory is kept per-process, and byte calculations remain integer-exact. Nine focused tests exercise quantiles, large-byte arithmetic, pilot-roster rejection, environment drift, exact input hashing, no-overwrite behavior, and CLI wiring; Ruff, formatting, compilation, schema, and diff checks pass. This is conditional preflight software, not measured pilot evidence, a confidence bound, capacity approval, or cost estimate; representative SISO/MIMO native, receiver, and model measurements remain required before scaling.

**Edition 3.782 G5.14 mapped receive-grid SRS-RSRP observation (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added `measure_ul_srs_rsrp_from_grid` for caller-calibrated multiport SRS mappings and complex receive grids. It enforces shape and cell bounds, hashes the exact received and mapped grids, selects only configured SRS REs, applies the watts-per-grid-unit-squared scale, and emits the existing UL SRS-RSRP report inside a distinct `OBSERVED_NOT_QUALIFIED` wrapper. Noise and interference power on those REs remain in the observation; no synchronization, rejection, or receiver qualification is claimed. The SRS measurement module passes 15 tests; Ruff, formatting, compilation, schema validation, and diff checks pass. UL-AoA/path-power/TDCT, raw ASN.1/PER, receiver timing acquisition, calibration validation, and scientific qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.783 G5 network-composed SRS-RSRP observation (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added `measure_ul_srs_rsrp_from_noisy_network_grid`, joining the existing multi-transmitter resource-grid composer and independent receiver AWGN output to configured SRS RE measurement. It verifies the selected receiver grid equals the coherent TX-contribution sum plus its noise grid, enforces a selected-grid cell bound, hashes the receive grid, noise grid, mapped SRS resource, and per-transmitter contributions, and reports branch-wise desired, coherent-interference, measured-noise, and simulated-SINR components alongside the standard full-grid SRS-RSRP. The SRS measurement module passes 17 tests and the network-PHY module passes 78; Ruff, formatting, compilation, schema, and diff checks pass. The versioned report remains `OBSERVED_NOT_QUALIFIED`; caller calibration, synchronization, receiver execution, and scientific qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.784 G8 profile-holdout identity admission (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Removed case-folding and string coercion from the profile-holdout adapter. Dataset metadata now must use canonical uppercase A–E profile labels and lowercase `tdl`/`cdl` family labels, and group IDs pass unchanged into split checks. Four focused boundary regressions pass. This prevents category aliasing in this runner only; other model routes, complete leakage review, and physical independence of group IDs remain open.
**Edition 3.785 G5.14 UL-AoA observation contract (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added `report_ul_aoa` and a strict schema for caller-supplied azimuth and vertical-angle estimates, binding the sample ID, GCS/LCS convention and frame ID, estimated-channel digest, estimator configuration, and antenna-calibration digest. Seven focused cases pass for both coordinate systems, schema agreement, and malformed bounds/identities. The contract follows TS 38.215 V19.2.0 clause 5.2.4 direction conventions and remains `OBSERVED_NOT_QUALIFIED`; it does not perform AoA estimation or validate calibration/receiver behavior. UL SRS-RSRPP/TDCT/TDCP, raw ASN.1/RRC, synchronization, and receiver qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.786 G5 PUSCH exact transmit-payload retention and diagnostic audit (2026-10-27, focused software validation):** Standardized PUSCH records now retain the exact transmitted transport-block bits in an evaluation-only sidecar outside `NRObservation`, `NRModelInputs`, and named targets. NR record schema v4 binds sidecar presence and content in the content-addressed NPZ while preserving v1–v3 reads. A new diagnostic decoder verifies the payload digest and reports TB CRC plus bit-error counts; the legacy CRC-only entry point still reads records without payloads. The NR record/store suites pass 75 tests with Ruff check, JSON schema parse, and compilation passing. The selected CPU-native decode integration check remained CPU-bound without output after ten minutes and was stopped; transmitter-to-decoder runtime evidence remains pending. Receiver qualification and preregistered G5 acceptance remain open.
**Edition 3.787 G5 payload/provenance consistency follow-up (2026-10-27, focused validation):** `NRChannelRecord` now validates the sidecar's exact `[TX, bit]` shape and SHA-256 against provenance when constructing/reading a record, and rejects provenance declarations without a payload. A regression modifies stored metadata, recomputes the outer content-address digest, and confirms the semantic payload digest check still rejects it. The isolated NR record/store suites pass 78 tests; Ruff, compilation, and diff checks pass. Root `tests/conftest.py` performs Sionna RT/Mitsuba initialization even for dependency-light dataset tests, so this slice used `--confcutdir=tests/test_datasets` to exclude that unrelated initialization. The prior native TX-to-decoder run remains unverified; G5 acceptance and receiver qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.788 G5.14 bounded SRS hopping verification (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Verified the existing type-1 frequency-tree recurrence and scheduled per-symbol `n-SRS` integration against ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0 clause 6.4.1.4.3, including explicit-counter enforcement, odd/even tree radices, periodic/aperiodic counter behavior, and single-/multiport hopped grid mapping. Thirteen selected SRS regressions pass with Ruff clean. This supersedes earlier “not implemented” and “unrun” statements for the bounded software component only; decoded higher-layer schedule completeness, raw ASN.1/PER and CRC/polar processing, independent full conformance, and receiver qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.789 G5 retained-PUSCH-payload mutation guard (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Record admission now stores the sidecar shape as an immutable tuple, and `write_nr_record` rechecks bit values, size, shape, and SHA-256 immediately before publication. This prevents a caller from re-enabling NumPy array writes or mutating nested shape provenance to publish a self-inconsistent NR object. The isolated NR record/store suites pass 79 tests; changed Python files pass Ruff and compilation, and `git diff --check` passes. Native transmitter-to-decoder execution and G5 receiver qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.790 bounded City43 zero-scattering LOS Phase-0 diagnosis (2026-10-27, existing diagnostics only):** The retained depth-10, seed-23, 100k/1M SISO comparison is dominated by a zero-interaction aggregate: valid zero-order records increase 9→75, collapse to one coefficient/delay/geometry fingerprint, and explain 0.999949 of total CFR-delta norm under separate coherent aggregation. Matched nonzero paths have sub-millimeter vertex and sub-0.0001 ns delay changes; their maximum coefficient relative error is 7.9e-5. Interaction-order counts grow with budget, including 15→105 order-10 paths, but reported order-10 coherent power remains far below the zero-interaction term. The bounded interpretation is budget-dependent discovery/multiplicity of repeated zero-interaction records followed by coherent accumulation. The reports do not explain why the solver returns those records, qualify other configurations, or make 1M ground truth. No new RT run was made. Full evidence and source digests are in `docs/research/CITY43_ZERO_SCATTERING_PHASE0_DIAGNOSIS_2026-10-27.md`; G3 and production RT remain gated.

**Edition 3.791 scenario GBSM frozen split-component audit (2026-10-27, focused validation):** `ScenarioGBSMRun.audit_split_assignment` now checks that every generated episode is present in the supplied immutable split registry, every parent retains the shared `trajectory_session` relation for the exact run ID, and the complete run resolves to one component and split. Its v1 report binds the frozen registry digest, ordered parent assignments, and canonical report digest under a strict JSON Schema. Two focused regressions and Ruff/compilation/diff checks pass. The signed child-artifact roster and corpus audit do not yet consume this report, so it is not complete descendant coverage or release qualification.

**Edition 3.792 scenario GBSM signed child-artifact admission (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Split child-artifact roster v6 adds typed scenario GBSM run-manifest and record-content entries with canonical multi-child owner lists. The signed-roster auditor traverses each run using the canonical store reader, matches all records to frozen parent/child rows, verifies the shared trajectory-session relation through the frozen registry, and emits run/component/split audit digests. Complete split-audit reports now use schema v10 when a v6 roster is present. Three scenario split-roster regressions and all 41 split-lineage auditor tests pass; Ruff, compilation, JSON-schema, and diff checks pass. This extends semantic coverage for one descendant family only; full descendant inventory, signer/inventory authority, and release qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.793 causal FIR support in multi-link sample-domain composition (2026-10-27, focused validation):** `compose_received_waveforms` accepts up to 128 caller-supplied complex causal taps per RX/TX link, applies filtering before oscillator/receiver impairments, preserves filter tails across capture alignment, accounts for tap/interpolation work in the declared element ceiling, and retains exact tap tensors in its result. The network-PHY module passes with Ruff, compilation, and diff checks. The FIR contract requires caller-declared/calibrated complex response taps and does not infer phase from frequency-dependent power-only link-budget data. Link-budget-to-FIR conversion, calibration, asynchronous clocks, and receiver qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.794 bounded network-waveform PUSCH handoff (2026-10-27, focused/native validation):** Added a fail-closed handoff from aligned, noisy network sample captures to the configured Sionna PUSCH demodulator/decoder. It verifies the source-contribution sum, exact AWGN addition, sample-rate/FFT-grid match, caller-supplied W/RE receiver noise, bounded tensor counts, exact tensor/configuration digests, and a strict `OBSERVED_NOT_QUALIFIED` report schema. Eight focused cases pass, including a one-transport-block V100 smoke with a clean CRC. This is software-path evidence for one 2-RB/30-kHz synthetic case; it does not validate the sample-to-RE noise conversion, synchronization/acquisition, multi-cell interference response, acceptance cohort, or G5 qualification.
**Edition 3.795 Sionna unitary-FFT noise variance conversion (2026-10-27, focused/native validation):** Sionna PHY 2.2.0 applies `1/sqrt(N)` in its FFT and IFFT, discards CP samples, applies only unit-magnitude phase compensation, and permutes bins. The network PUSCH handoff therefore derives equal per-resource-element variance from iid proper-complex sample AWGN for this pinned version; alternate receiver conventions still require an explicit W/RE profile. A native 512-slot noise-only measurement matches the analytic variance within 1.5%, and the one-block CUDA PUSCH smoke passes with the derived variance. Synchronization/acquisition and G5 qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.759 G0 exact runtime and model-table inventory (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added a strict schema-bound `loslab-runtime-inventory` CLI that hashes lock/config inputs, installed distribution RECORD/WHEEL/direct-URL metadata, and the installed Sionna TR 38.901 model JSON tables. Its opt-in accelerator probe reports Torch/CUDA build and both V100 devices without allocating tensors. Six focused tests pass; both report modes validate against schema, with Ruff/formatting, compilation, lock, and diff checks clean. The inventory is self-reported integrity metadata only; API compatibility decisions, loaded native-library identity, independent attestation, and scientific qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.760 G0 pinned Sionna API compatibility preflight (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Runtime inventory schema v2 adds explicit API checks for repository-used Sionna PHY/RT, Mitsuba, and Dr.Jit symbols against the pinned versions. The live Sionna PHY check reports all used module exports available; the explicit RT check reports the missing `sionna-rt` package without importing RT modules. Eight focused tests pass with schema, Ruff/format, compilation, lock, and diff checks. This is a software compatibility preflight only; native-library identity, backend behavior, external attestation, and scientific qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.761 G8 estimator-comparison truth-mismatch regression repaired (2026-10-27, focused validation):** The curve-comparison fixture now supplies complete v2 Sionna-LS/finite-delay-DFT metadata, so it reaches its intended unmatched-channel-truth assertion. The direct regression passes and `tests/test_evaluation/test_metrics.py` passes all 41 cases. This is a fixture alignment, not new receiver evidence; model-route coverage, physical independent-group evidence, and broad evaluation support remain open.
**Edition 3.762 G8 phase-free power baseline on shared splits (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added the canonical `power_only` baseline, computed from observed CFR as per-link/per-subcarrier dB power and registered under model-input artifact contract v3 with exact transform and matrix digests. A runnable common-runner invocation compares it to MLP/raw-MLP on identical seed splits. The direct phase-invariance and integration cases pass; the complete runner module passes 18 tests, with Ruff/format, compilation, and diff checks. This closes only the power-only slice of G8.08; classical observability estimators and other falsification/leakage controls remain open.
**Edition 3.764 G8 seeded phase-randomization control (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added an observation-only `phase_randomized` route that multiplies each sample/link/subcarrier CFR by independent seeded unit phase, then trains the same MLP implementation as `raw_mlp` on the exact shared split. Contract v4 binds the transform implementation, seed through resolved run configuration, full/partition matrices, and model artifact. All 20 runner tests pass; Ruff, formatting, compilation, schema JSON parsing, CLI help, and diff checks pass. This is a falsification control only; shuffled, permuted-label, source/metadata-only, oracle-leak controls and classical receiver estimators remain open. It does not qualify CSI fidelity, NR receiver impact, or production RT.
**Edition 3.766 G4 scalable TDL proper-complex test family specified (2026-10-27, implementation validation):** Added an independent-reference properness regression definition for all Rayleigh taps in scalable TDL-A–E, excluding the zero-delay LOS/diffuse aggregate for D/E. Its ETSI reference digest, 95-tap/380-claim family, draw count, confidence/precision bounds, and five seeds are frozen. The fresh seeds are clear within the 2,273-file saved-corpus scan (`G4_SCALABLE_TDL_PROPERNESS_SEED_AUDIT_2026-10-27.json`, SHA-256 `e2cb2de0141253d9e295cea201dd7502488931659f27ad44084922338db9142f`). The dependency-light reference-roster test passes; the native statistical test was not run. This closes only a test-definition gap and does not qualify G4.
